**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁתְּהֵא מְנַטֶּפֶת.** טִפָּה טִפָּה לְתוֹךְ הַנֵּר, וְטַעְמָא גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִסְתַּפֵּק מִמֶּנּוּ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁהִקְצָהוּ לְנֵר חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם מְכַבֶּה:


בשביל שתהא מנטפת – drop by drop into the candle and the reason is a decree lest he be supplied from it and since he set it aside for the candle, he is liable because of [the prohibition] of extinguishing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:3)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא שֶׁל חֶרֶס.** דְּמָאִיס, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי גָּזְרִינַן, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין הַפְּתִילָה הַדּוֹלֶקֶת בְּתוֹךְ הַכְּלִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ הַשֶּׁמֶן, אָתֵי לְאִסְתַּפּוּקֵי מִנֵּיהּ, דְּסָבַר אֵין כָּאן מִשּׁוּם מְכַבֶּה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:4)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר.** דְּלֹא גָּזַר דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאִסְתַּפּוּקֵי מִנֵּיהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא רוֹאֶה הַשֶּׁמֶן נוֹטֵף עַל הַפְּתִילָה שֶׁתַּחְתָּיו:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:5)


**אִם חִבְּרָהּ הַיּוֹצֵר מִתְּחִלָּה כוּ'.** וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם חִבְּרָהּ בַּעַל הַבַּיִת בְּסִיד אוֹ בְּחַרְסִית מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת לֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ, דְּמִשּׁוּם אִסּוּרָא דְּשַׁבָּת בָּדֵיל מִנֵּיהּ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:4:6)


**וְיִתֵּן רֹאשׁ הַפְּתִילָה.** שֶׁהוּא יוֹצֵא מִן הַצַּד לִשְׁאֹב הַשֶּׁמֶן וְנִמְשָׁךְ דֶּרֶךְ הַפְּתִילָה לָרֹאשׁ הַדּוֹלֵק. וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן מַתְנִיתִין פְּלֻגְתָּא דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבָּנָן בִּשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל בֵּיצָה, וּבְחֶרֶס, וּבִקְעָרָה. דְּאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן שְׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל בֵּיצָה, בְּהָא קָאָמְרִי רַבָּנָן, דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא מְאִיסָא אָתֵי לְאִסְתַּפּוּקֵי מִינַּהּ, אֲבָל שֶׁל חֶרֶס דְּמָאִיס אֵימָא מוֹדוּ לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וְאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן שֶׁל חֶרֶס, בְּהָא קָאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, אֲבָל בְּהַהִיא אֵימָא מוֹדֶה לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן. וְאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן הָנֵי תַּרְתֵּי, בְּהָא קָאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַשְּׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל בֵּיצָה וְהַחֶרֶס מֻנָּחִים בְּתוֹךְ חֲלַל הַנֵּר לְמַעְלָה, וְלֹא מַפְסִיק מִידֵי בֵּין הַנֵּר וּבֵינָם, וְלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר שֶׁמָּא יִסְתַּפֵּק, דְּבָדֵיל מִנֵּיהּ, אֲבָל בִּקְעָרָה דְּמַפְסְקָא, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵצֶל הַנֵּר מֻנַּחַת וְיֵשׁ כָּאן הֶפְסֵק וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר גּוּפֵיהּ הוּא, אֵימָא מוֹדֶה דְּנִגְזֹר. וְאִי אַשְׁמְעִינַן בְּהַהִיא, בְּהַהִיא קָאָמְרִי רַבָּנָן, אֲבָל בְּהָנָךְ תַּרְתֵּי מוֹדוּ לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, צְרִיכָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:1)


**מִפְּנֵי עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים.** כְּגוֹן פַּרְסִיִּים שֶׁאֵין מַנִּיחִין לְהַדְלִיק אוֹר בְּיוֹם אֵידָם אֶלָּא בְּבֵית עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁלָּהֶם:


מפני עובדי כוכבים – such as the Persians who do not allow the lighting of a candle on the day of their festivals other than in their houses of idolatrous worship.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:2)


**מִפְּנֵי לִסְטִים.** שֶׁלֹּא יִרְאוּ שֶׁיֵּשׁ שָׁם אָדָם וְיָבֹאוּ עָלָיו:


מפני לסטים – that they do not fear that there is there a person who will come upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:3)


**מִפְּנֵי רוּחַ רָעָה.** הַשּׁוֹרָה עָלָיו, וּכְשֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה נוֹחַ לוֹ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ רוּחַ רָעָה, מִין מִמִּינֵי הַחֹלִי הַבָּא לְבַעֲלֵי הַמָּרָה הַשְּׁחוֹרָה, שֶׁלֹּא יָנוּחוּ אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁיֵּשְׁבוּ בַּחֹשֶׁךְ וּבְהַסְתָּרָה מִבְּנֵי אָדָם:


מפני רוח רעה – that comes down upon him and when he doesn’t see [it], it is pleasant for him. But Maimonides explains [the words] רוח רעה as a kind of the illnesses that come upon those with black bile that they cannot rest other than when they sit in the dark and when hiding their faces from people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:4)


**וְאִם בִּשְׁבִיל הַחוֹלֶה שֶׁיִּישַׁן פָּטוּר.** הַאי חוֹלֶה, חוֹלֶה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ סַכָּנָה הוּא, דְּאִלּוּ מְכַבֶּה בִּשְׁבִיל חוֹלֶה שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ סַכָּנָה חַיָּב לְמַאי דְּסָבַר הַאי תַּנָּא דִּמְלָאכָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְגוּפָהּ חַיָּב עָלֶיהָ. וְכֵן מִפְּנֵי עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים, מִפְּנֵי לִסְטִים, מִפְּנֵי רוּחַ רָעָה, כֻּלְּהוּ אִית בְּהוּא סַכָּנָה. וּבְדִין הוּא דְּלִתְנֵי מֻתָּר, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי סֵיפָא חַיָּב, תָּנָא רֵישָׁא פָּטוּר:


ואם בשביל החולה שיישן פטור – this illness is when there is danger involved for if one extinguishes a flame for a sick person who is not in danger, he is liable [for a sin-offering] for what reason this Tanna/teacher holds that labor that is not necessary for its own sake, one is liable for it; and similarly because of idolaters and because of robbers and because of an evil spirit where there is danger, but according to the law, where it teaches that it is permitted – but because it (i.e., the Mishnah) was required to teach at its conclusion that it is prohibited, the first part of the Mishnah taught that one is exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:5)


**כְּחָס עַל הַפְּתִילָה חַיָּב.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְגוּף הַכִּבּוּי אֶלָּא לְצֹרֶךְ דָּבָר אַחֵר, שֶׁלֹּא תִּדְלַק הַפְּתִילָה אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִפְקַע הַנֵּר, חַיָּב, דִּמְלָאכָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְגוּפָהּ חַיָּב עָלֶיהָ:


כחס על הפתילה חייב – and even though he does not need it for the body of the extinguishing but rather for the needs of something else, that he should not kindle the wick or that he should not split the candle, he is liable for that is labor that is not necessary for its own sake, and one is liable for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:6)


**פּוֹטֵר בְּכֻלָּן חוּץ מִן הַפְּתִילָה.** שֶׁאֵין לְךָ כִּבּוּי הַצָּרִיךְ לְגוּפוֹ, אֶלָּא כִּבּוּי שֶׁל פֶּחָמִים, וְכִבּוּי שֶׁל הִבְהוּב פְּתִילָה, שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה הַכִּבּוּי לְהַאֲחִיז הָאוּר מַהֵר כְּשֶׁיִּרְצֶה לְהַדְלִיקוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


פוטר בכולן חוץ מן הפתילה – for you do not have something extinguished that is needed for itself, but rather the extinguishing of coals and the extinguishing of the singeing of the wick that makes the extinguishing process to take hold of the fire rapidly when one wants to kindle it. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:5:7)


**שֶׁהוּא עוֹשָׂהּ פֶּחָם.** שֶׁהוּא מִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ עַכְשָׁיו בְּכִבּוּי זֶה פֶּחָם, שֶׁתְּהֵא מְהוּבְהֶבֶת לְהָאִיר יָפֶה:


שהוא עושה פחם – that he intends to make it now with the extinguished – which is coals which will be singed to light well.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:6:1)


**בִּשְׁעַת לֵדָתָן.** בִּשְׁעַת סַכָּנָה מְזֻמָּן פֻּרְעָנוּתָא:


בשעת לידתן – at the time of danger which is designated for retribution.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:6:2)


**וּבַחַלָּה וּבְהַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר.** לְפִי שֶׁצָּרְכֵי הַבַּיִת הֵן וְהִיא מְצוּיָה בַּבַּיִת, תְּלוּיִין בָּהּ:


**Regarding Challah and lighting Shabbat candles.** because these are among the needs of a household, and she is found at home, and therefore she is bound to them.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:1)


**צָרִיךְ אָדָם לוֹמַר בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ.** וְצָרִיךְ לְמֵמְרִינְהוּ בְּנִיחוּתָא כִּי הֵיכִי דִּלְקַבְּלוּ מִנֵּיהּ:


צריך אדם לומר בתוך ביתו – and he needs to say them in a gentle way (Tractate Shabbat 34a) in order that he will accept it from him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:2)


**עִם חֲשֵׁכָה.** כְּשֶׁהוּא סָמוּךְ לַחֲשֵׁכָה וְיֵשׁ עֲדַיִן שְׁהוּת בַּיּוֹם לְעַשֵּׂר וּלְעָרֵב, אֲבָל קֹדֶם לַחֲשֵׁכָה הַרְבֵּה לֹא, דִּלְמָא פָּשְׁעִי וְאָמְרֵי עֲדַיִן יֵשׁ שְׁהוּת בַּיּוֹם:


עם חשיכה – which is near darkness and there is yet time during the day to tithe or to make an Eruv but not a lot of time prior to darkness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:3)


**עִשַּׂרְתֶּם.** לִסְעוּדַת שַׁבָּת, שֶׁאַף אֲכִילַת עֲרַאי שֶׁל שַׁבָּת קוֹבַעַת לְמַעֲשֵׂר:


עשרתם – for the Sabbath meal, for even for an incidental meal of the Sabbath establishes for tithing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:4)


**עֵרַבְתֶּם.** עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין וַחֲצֵרוֹת. וְהָנֵי תַּרְתֵּי שַׁיָּךְ לְמֵמְרִינְהוּ בִּלְשׁוֹן שְׁאֵלָה, דְּשֶׁמָּא כְּבָר עָשׂוּ, אֲבָל בְּנֵר לֹא שַׁיָּךְ לְמֵימַר הִדְלַקְתֶּם אֶת הַנֵּר, דְּדָבָר הַנִּרְאֶה לָעַיִן הוּא אִי אַדְלִיק אִי לֹא אַדְלִיק:


ערבתם – the joining of borders and courtyards and for these two it belongs to mention them in the form of a question, lest they have already been made, but with a candle, it does not belong to mention “have you kindled the candle” for this is a matter that is apparent to the eye – either that I should light or that I should not light.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:5)


**סָפֵק חֲשֵׁכָה.** מִתְּחִלַּת שְׁקִיעַת הַחַמָּה כָּל זְמַן שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כּוֹכָב אֶחָד בִּלְבַד וַדַּאי יוֹם, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁנִּרְאִים שְׁנֵי כּוֹכָבִים בֵּינוֹנִים הוּא סָפֵק חֲשֵׁכָה, וְקָרוּי בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, וְנוֹתְנִים עָלָיו חֻמְרֵי יוֹם וְחֻמְרֵי לַיְלָה, וּמִשֶּׁיֵּרָאוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה כּוֹכָבִים בֵּינוֹנִים הוּא וַדַּאי לַיְלָה לְכָל דָּבָר:


ספק חשיכה – from the beginning of the setting of the sun, all the time that one star alone appears, it is definitely daylight and all the while that two medium-sized stars appear, it is doubtfully dark and it is called twilight and we place upon it the stringencies of the daytime and the stringencies of the nighttime, and when three medium-sized stars appear, it is definitely dark for all matters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:6)


**אֵין מְעַשְּׂרִין אֶת הַוַּדַּאי.** דְּתִקּוּן מַעֲלְיָא הוּא. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵינוֹ אָסוּר אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת, קָסָבַר הַאי תַּנָּא דְּגָזְרוּ עַל הַשְּׁבוּת אַף בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת:


אין מעשרין את הודאי – this is a valid preparation but because of rabbinic decrees designed to enhance the character of the Shabbat as a day of rest/Shevut, this Tanna/teacher holds that they made a decree designed to enhance the character of the Sabbath as a day of rest even at twilight.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:7)


**וְאֵין מַטְבִּילִין אֶת הַכֵּלִים.** לְהַעֲלוֹתָן מִידֵי טֻמְאָתָן, דַּהֲוֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן כְּלִי, וְאִית בֵּיהּ נַמִּי שְׁבוּת:


ואין מטבילין את הכלים – to elevate them from their ritual impurity for it is like repairing a utensil which has the rabbinic decree designed to enhance the character of the Sabbath as a day of rest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:8)


**וְאֵין מַדְלִיקִין אֶת הַנֵּרוֹת.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן, דִּסְפֵקָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא הוּא, וְזוֹ וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר זוֹ קָתָנֵי. וְרַבּוֹתַי פֵּרְשׁוּ וְאֵין מַדְלִיקִין אֶת הַנֵּרוֹת, אֵין אוֹמְרִים לְנָכְרִי לְהַדְלִיק:


ואין מדליקין את הנרות - all the more so since it is a doubtful Biblical commandment and this, and you don’t have to mention that, is taught. But my teachers explained that one does not kindle the candles nor does one tell a heathen to kindle [them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:9)


**אֲבָל מְעַשְּׂרִים אֶת הַדְּמַאי.** וְלֹא דָּמִי לִמְתַקֵּן, דְּרֹב עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ מְעַשְּׂרִין הֵן:


אבל מעשרין את הדמאי – and it is not similar to repairing for most ignoramuses/Amei HaAretz tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:10)


**וּמְעָרְבִין.** עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת, דְּחֻמְרָא בְּעָלְמָא הוּא, אֲבָל לֹא עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין, דְּיֵשׁ לָהֶם סֶמֶךְ מִקְּרָאֵי:


ומערבין – for the joining of courtyards is a mere stringency but not the joining of Sabbath boundaries that have support from Biblical verses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 2:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/2:7:11)


**וְטוֹמְנִין אֶת הַחַמִּין.** בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל, דְּאִלּוּ בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל אֲפִלּוּ מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם אָסוּר. וְטַעְמָא דְּטוֹמְנִין אֶת הַחַמִּין בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל, שֶׁלֹּא אָסְרוּ לְהַטְמִין אֶת הַחַמִּין בְּשַׁבָּת עַצְמוֹ בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל אֶלָּא גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְצָא קְדֵרָתוֹ שֶׁנִּצְטַנְּנָה וְיַרְתִּיחֶנָּה בָּאוּר וְנִמְצָא מְבַשֵּׁל בְּשַׁבָּת, וּבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת לֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר בְּהָכִי, דִּסְתָם קְדֵרוֹת בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת רוֹתְחוֹת הֵן וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא נִצְטַנְּנָה וְיַרְתִּיחֶנָּה, הִלְכָּךְ טוֹמְנִין אֶת הַחַמִּין בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּשַׁבָּת:


וטומנין את החמין – concerning something that does not add vapor, for if it were something that added vapor, even while it is still daylight, it is prohibited, and the reason is that we cover up hot foods at twilight with something that does not add vapor for they (i.e., the Sages) did not prohibit covering up hot foods on the Sabbath itself with something that does not add vapor as a decree lest one finds a pot that has cooled off and its growing hot in fire and it is found to be cooking on the Sabbath and at twilight, one cannot make a decree on this for mere pots at twilight boil and one cannot suspect lest it cooled off and became hot. Therefore, we cover hot foods at twilight even though we do not cover hot foods on the Sabbath.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:1)


**כִּירָה.** מָקוֹם עָשׂוּי בָּאָרֶץ כְּדֵי שְׁפִיתַת שְׁתֵּי קְדֵרוֹת, וְהָאֵשׁ עוֹבֶרֶת תַּחַת שְׁתֵּי הַקְּדֵרוֹת:


כירה – a place made on the ground open to two pots and fire passes underneath the two pots.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:2)


**בִּגְבָבָא.** עֵצִים דַּקִּים כְּקַשׁ שֶׁגּוֹבְבִין מִן הַשָּׂדֶה:


בגבבא – thin wood like straw that they gather/rake in from the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:3)


**נוֹתְנִים עָלֶיהָ תַּבְשִׁיל.** מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, לְהַשְׁהוֹתוֹ שָׁם בְּשַׁבָּת:


נותנים עליה תבשיל – from the eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday) to tarry there on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:4)


**בְּגֶפֶת.** פְּסֹלֶת שֶׁל זֵיתִים וְשׁוּמְשְׁמִין לְאַחַר שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ שַׁמְנָן:


גפת – refuse of olives and sesame (see Talmud Shabbat 47b) after the oil is pressed out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:5)


**לֹא יִתֵּן.** מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁהוֹתָהּ שָׁם בְּשַׁבָּת:


לא יתן – from the eve of the Sabbath in order to have it tarry there on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:6)


**עַד שֶׁיִּגְרֹף.** הַגֶּחָלִים וְיוֹצִיאֵם מִן הַכִּירָה:


עד שיגרוף – the coals and remove them from the double oven.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:7)


**אוֹ שֶׁיִּתֵּן הָאֵפֶר.** עַל גַּבֵּי גֶּחָלִים לְכַסּוֹתָם וּלְצַנְּנָם. וְטַעְמָא, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יֶחְתֶּה בַּגֶּחָלִים בְּשַׁבָּת כְּדֵי לְמַהֵר בִּשּׁוּלוֹ. וְדַוְקָא בְּתַבְשִׁיל שֶׁלֹּא בָּשַׁל כָּל צָרְכּוֹ, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בָּשַׁל כָּל צָרְכּוֹ וּמִצְטַמֵּק וְיָפֶה לוֹ, הוּא דְּגָזְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא יֶחְתֶּה, אֲבָל תַּבְשִׁיל שֶׁלֹּא בָּשַׁל כְּלָל אוֹ בָּשַׁל כָּל צָרְכּוֹ וּמִצְטַמֵּק וְרַע לוֹ, מֻתָּר לְהַשְׁהוֹתוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי כִּירָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ גְּרוּפָה וּקְטוּמָה, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יֶחְתֶּה, הוֹאִיל וְהֵסִיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ. וְכֵן תַּבְשִׁיל שֶׁבָּשַׁל כָּל צָרְכּוֹ וְהִשְׁלִיךְ בְּתוֹכוֹ אֵבֶר חַי סָמוּךְ לְבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, נַעֲשָׂה הַכֹּל כְּתַבְשִׁיל שֶׁלֹּא בָּשַׁל כְּלָל, שֶׁהֲרֵי הֵסִיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ:


או שיתן האפר – on top of the coals to cover them and cool them off and the reason for the decree is lest he rake the coals under the ashes (Talmud Shabbat 34b) to speed up its cooking. And specifically, for a cooked dish that was not completely cooked, or even if it was completely cooked, and it reduced in size and it is improved by it, for they decreed lest he rake but the a dish that was not properly cooked at all or was completely cooked and which is deteriorated by boiling down (Talmud Shabbat 37b), it is permissible to have it tarry on the double oven even though it was not raked and folded, we don’t fear lest he rake it since he has removed it from his mind and similarly, a dish that was cooked completely and he cast into it raw meat prior to Twilight, everything was made like a dish that had not been cooked at all for he had completely removed it from his mind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:8)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים.** מַיִם חַמִּין נוֹתְנִין עַל גַּבֵּי כִּירָה לְאַחַר שֶׁגָּרַף, דְּלֹא צְרִיכִי לְבִשּׁוּלֵי וְלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר שֶׁמָּא יֶחְתֶּה:


בש"א – they put hot water on top of the double stove after he has raked it for it is not necessary to cook it and there is no need to decree lest he stir it/rake [the coals underneath the ashes].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:9)


**אֲבָל לֹא תַבְשִׁיל.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגָּרַף, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְגָרֵף כָּל הַגֶּחָלִים עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר שָׁם נִיצוֹץ אֶחָד, וְאָתֵי לְחַתּוֹיֵי מֵאַחַר דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּבִשּׁוּלֵיהּ:


אבל לא תבשיל – even though he had removed the ashes and coals from the stove for it is impossible to remove all of the ashes/coals until there no longer remains even one spark and one would come to stir the embers after he is pleased with the cooking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:10)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים נוֹטְלִים וְכוּ'.** אֲפִלּוּ חַמִּין דְּשָׁרִינַן לְהַשְׁהוֹת עַל גַּבֵּי כִּירָה גְּרוּפָה וְשֶׁנָּתַן אֶת הָאֵפֶר, לְאַחַר שֶׁנְּטָלָן אֵין מַחֲזִירִין דְּמֵחֲזֵי כִּמְבַשֵּׁל בְּשַׁבָּת:


בש"א נוטלים וכו' – even hot foods for we are permitted to have them tarry on top of the double stone that has been cleared of ashes and when he placed the ashes after he removed them, he doesn’t return them/put them back because it appears like cooking on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:1:11)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים.** בֵּין חַמִּין בֵּין תַּבְשִׁיל יְכוֹלִין לְהַחְזִיר לְאַחַר שֶׁנְּטָלָן. וְלֹא שָׁרוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל לְהַחְזִיר אֶלָּא בְּעוֹדָן בְּיָדוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא הֵנִיחָן עַל גַּבֵּי דָּבָר אַחֵר, אֲבָל לְאַחַר שֶׁהֵנִיחָן בַּקַּרְקַע אוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי דָּבָר אַחֵר אָסוּר לְהַחְזִירָן אֲפִלּוּ לְבֵית הִלֵּל, דַּהֲוֵי כְּמַטְמִין לְכַתְּחִלָּה בְּשַׁבָּת:


וב"ה אומרים – whether hot or cooked, one can put them (i.e., the ashes) back after having removed them, but the School of Hillel did not permit returning them other than while they are still in his hand that he had not placed them on another thing, but after he placed them in the ground or on another thing, it is forbidden to return them, even according to the School of Hillel because it is like hiding something ab initio on Shabbat.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:1)


**תַּנּוּר.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁצַּר לְמַעְלָה וְרָחָב לְמַטָּה נִקְלָט חֻמּוֹ לְתוֹכוֹ טְפֵי מִכִּירָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הִסִּיקוּהוּ בְּקַשׁ וּבִגְבָבָא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יֶחְתֶּה, דִּלְעוֹלָם אֵינוֹ מֵסִיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ:


תנור – because it is narrow above and wide below, it absorbs its heat into it more than a double stove and even if he heated it with straw and rakings, we fear that he might rake the coals under the ashes, for he never removes it from his mind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:2)


**בֵּין מֵעַל גַּבָּיו.** לִסְמֹךְ אֵצֶל דָּפְנוֹ:


בין מגביו – for support near its walls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:2:3)


**כֻּפָּח.** עָשׂוּי כְּכִירָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁאָרְכּוֹ כְּרָחְבּוֹ וְאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא מְקוֹם שְׁפִיתַת קְדֵרָה אַחַת, וְהָאֵשׁ עוֹבֶרֶת תַּחְתֶּיהָ, וְהֶבְלוֹ רַב מֵהֶבֶל הַכִּירָה, שֶׁהַכִּירָה פְּתוּחָה מִלְּמַעְלָה שִׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי קְדֵרוֹת וְהַכֻּפָּח אֵין פָּתוּחַ אֶלָּא שִׁעוּר קְדֵרָה אַחַת, וּפָחוֹת מֵהֶבֶל הַתַּנּוּר:


כופח – it is made like a double stove but its length is like its width and is stove which only has room for one pot (Tractate Shabbat 38b), and the fire passes underneath it and its vapor is greater than that of the double stove for the double stove is open above for two pots and the brazier/small stove is not open other than for the measure of one pot and has less than the vapor of an oven.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:1)


**אֵין נוֹתְנִין.** בְּשַׁבָּת:


One cannot give on Shabbat


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:2)


**בֵּיצָה בְצַד הַמֵּחַם.** קוּמְקוּם שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁמְּחַמְּמִים בּוֹ הַמַּיִם עַל גַּבֵּי הָאֵשׁ:


ביצה בצד המיחם – a copper kettle in which they heat up water on top of the fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:3)


**שֶׁתִּתְגַּלְגֵּל.** שֶׁתִּצָּלֶה קְצָת עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא מְגֻלְגֶּלֶת כְּלוֹמַר מְעֹרֶבֶת:


שתתגלגל – that it will roast a bit until it becomes roasted/rounded, that is to say, mixed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:4)


**וְלֹא יַפְקִיעֶנָּה בְסוּדָרִין.** לֹא יִשְׁבְּרֶנָּה עַל סוּדָר שֶׁהוּחַם בַּחַמָּה כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּצָּלֶה בּוֹ, דְּגָזְרִינַן תּוֹלְדוֹת חַמָּה אַטּוּ תּוֹלְדוֹת הָאוּר:


ולא יפקיענה בסודרין – he should not crack it on wrapping that was warmed by the sun in order that it be roasted by it, for we decreed that derivatives of the sun are because of the derivatives of the heat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:5)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַתִּיר.** דְּסָבַר לֹא גָּזְרִינַן תּוֹלְדוֹת חַמָּה אַטּוּ תּוֹלְדוֹת הָאוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ור"י מתיר – for he holds that we do not make the decree that the derivatives of the sun are because of the derivatives of the heat and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:3:6)


**לֹא יַטְמִינֶנָּה בְחֹל וּבַאֲבַק דְּרָכִים.** שֶׁהוּחְמוּ מִכֹּחַ הַחַמָּה, וּבְהָא לֹא שָׁרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי, דְּגָזַר חֹל אַטּוּ רֶמֶץ, הוֹאִיל וְתַרְוַיְהוּ דֶּרֶךְ הַטְמָנָה אָתֵי לְמֵימַר מַה לִּי רֶמֶץ מַה לִּי חֹל. אִי נַמִּי גָּזַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁמָּא יָזִיז עָפָר מִמְּקוֹמוֹ, שֶׁמָּא לֹא יְהֵא שָׁם חֹל עָקוּר כְּכָל הַצֹּרֶךְ, וְאָתֵי לַאֲזוּזֵי עָפָר הַדָּבוּק, וַהֲוֵי תּוֹלָדָה דְּחוֹרֵשׁ:


לא יטמיננה בחול ובאבק דרכים – that were warmed by the power of the sun and in that, Rabbi Yosi did not permit, for he decreed that the sand are because of the hot ashes/embers for since both of them were through putting a dish in a warm place or under covers to keep it warm for the Sabbath (Talmud Shabbat 39a), they will come to say – what is the difference between hot ashes and sand. Alternatively, Rabbi Yosi decreed lest he move the ashes dust from its place lest the sand there will not be removed according to its need, and he will come to move the attached dust and that is a derivative of ploughing.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:1)


**סִלּוֹן.** צִנּוֹר שֶׁמַּמְשִׁיכִין הַמַּיִם לְתוֹכוֹ, וְהָיָה אוֹתוֹ צִנּוֹר מְשֻׁקַּע בְּחַמֵּי טְבֶרְיָא וּמִתְחַמֵּם מִכֹּחַ אוֹתָם הַמַּיִם הַחַמִּים, וּכְשֶׁהָיוּ הַמַּיִם צוֹנְנִין נִמְשָׁכִין בְּתוֹכוֹ מִתְחַמְּמִין בְּאוֹתוֹ סִלּוֹן שֶׁנִּתְחַמֵּם בְּתוֹךְ חֲמֵי טְבֶרְיָא. וְאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁהַמַּיִם שֶׁנִּמְשְׁכוּ בְּאוֹתוֹ צִנּוֹר בְּשַׁבָּת דִּינָם כְּדִין חַמִּין שֶׁהוּחְמוּ בְּשַׁבָּת, שֶׁאֲסוּרִים לִרְחֹץ בָּהֶן אֲפִלּוּ אֵבֶר קָטָן, וַאֲסוּרִים אַף בִּשְׁתִיָּה. וְהַמַּיִם שֶׁעוֹבְרִים בּוֹ בְּיוֹם טוֹב דִּינָם כְּדִין חַמִּין שֶׁהוּחְמוּ בְּיוֹם טוֹב, שֶׁאֲסוּרִים לִרְחֹץ בָּהֶם כָּל הַגּוּף, אֲבָל מֻתָּר לִרְחֹץ בָּהֶן יָדָיו וְרַגְלָיו, וּמֻתָּרִין בִּשְׁתִיָּה, וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וְחָזְרוּ בָּהֶם אַנְשֵׁי טְבֶרְיָא וְשָׁבְרוּ אֶת הַסִּלּוֹן:


סלון – a pipe that conducts the water in it and this pipe/duct was sunk in the hot springs of Tiberias and warmed from the power of those warm waters, and when the cold waters were drawn into it, they are warmed by that pipe/duct that was warmed by the hot springs of Tiberias, and the Sages said that the waters that were drawn into it that pipe on Shabbat, there law is like the law of waters that were warmed on Shabbat – that it is forbidden to bathe in them even a small limb and it is forbidden even for drinking, but the waters that pass through them on a Jewish holy day/Yom Tov, their law is like the law of hot waters that were heated on Yom Tov – that is forbidden to bathe one’s entire body in them, but it is permitted to wash one’s face, hands and feet in them, but drinking is permitted, and the Halakha is according to the Sages. The men of Tiberias retracted and broke the duct/pipe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:2)


**מוּלְיָאר.** פֵּרְשׁוּ בַּגְּמָרָא מַיִם מִבִּפְנִים וְגֶחָלִים מִבַּחוּץ, וְהוּא כְּלִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בֵּית קִבּוּל קָטָן אֵצֶל דָּפְנוֹ מִבַּחוּץ מְחֻבָּר לוֹ, וְנוֹתְנִים שָׁם גֶּחָלִים, וְהַמַּיִם בְּקִבּוּלוֹ הַגָּדוֹל, וְאִם גְּרָפוּהוּ מִן הַגֶּחָלִים מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם שׁוֹתִין מִן הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּקִבּוּלוֹ הַגָּדוֹל בְּשַׁבָּת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֻחֲמוּ קְצָת מֵחֲמַת כְּלִי, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל אֶלָּא מְשַׁמֵּר וּמְקַיֵּם חֹם שֶׁלָּהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטַנְּנוּ:


מוליאר – They explained in the Gemara (Tractate Shabbat 41a and Rashi’s commentary) – water from inside and coals from outside and it is a utensil that has a small receptacle near its wall from the outside which is attached to it, and they place there the coals and water in the large receptable on the Sabbath, and even though it warmed a bit from the warmth of the utensil, because it does not add vapor, but preserves and maintains their heat that it not become cold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:4:3)


**אַנְטִיכִי.** הוּא כְּלִי נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי שׁוּלַיִם וּמְשִׂימִין הַמַּיִם לְמַעְלָה וְהָאֵשׁ לְמַטָּה בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַשּׁוּלַיִם, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהָאֵשׁ לְמַטָּה בֵּין שְׁתֵּי הַשּׁוּלַיִם חֻמּוֹ נִשְׁאָר הַרְבֵּה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגָּרַף הַגֶּחָלִים מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת הַמַּיִם מִתְחַמְּמִים בּוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין שׁוֹתִים מֵהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת:


אנטיכי – it is a copper utensil which has two rims/saucers [at the bottom of the vessel and they place the water above and the fire below between the two saucers and since the fire is below between the two rims/saucers, the heat remains more and even though the coals were raked from the eve of the Sabbath, the water is heated up in it on the Sabbath – therefore we don’t drink from them on Shabbat.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:1)


**הַמֵּחַם.** קוּמְקוּם שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין אוֹתוֹ עַל גַּב הָאֵשׁ לְחַמֵּם הַמַּיִם שֶׁבְּתוֹכוֹ:


המימם – the copper kettle that they place on top of the fire to heat the water that is in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:2)


**שֶׁפִּנָּהוּ.** מֵעַל הַכִּירָה וְיֵשׁ בְּתוֹכוֹ מַיִם חַמִּין:


שפינהו – [that they removed it] from on top of the double stove and there is within it hot water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:3)


**לֹא יִתֵּן לְתוֹכוֹ.** מַיִם מֻעָטִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְחַמְּמוּ מִן הַמַּיִם הַחַמִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ בְּתוֹךְ הַמֵּחַם, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ כִּמְבַשֵּׁל בְּשַׁבָּת:


לא יתן לתוכו – a bit of [cold] water in order that it can be heated from the water that remained within the vessel for heating water for this is like cooking on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:4)


**אֲבָל נוֹתֵן לְתוֹכוֹ.** מַיִם מְרֻבִּים עַד שֶׁיָּשׁוּבוּ הַכֹּל פּוֹשְׁרִין:


אבל נותן לתוכו – a lot of water until everything returns to be lukewarm/tepid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:5)


**אוֹ לְתוֹךְ הַכּוֹס.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא כְּלִי שֵׁנִי, דַּוְקָא לְהַפְשִׁירָן מֻתָּר, אֲבָל מֻעָטִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיֻּחֲמוּ לֹא, דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְהַאי תַּנָּא דִּכְלִי שֵׁנִי מְבַשֵּׁל. וּלְקַמָּן תַּנִּינַן אֲבָל נוֹתֵן הוּא לְתוֹךְ הַקְּעָרָה, דְּמַשְׁמַע דִּכְלִי שֵׁנִי אֵינוֹ מְבַשֵּׁל. וַהֲלָכָה דִּכְלִי שֵׁנִי אֵינוֹ מְבַשֵּׁל:


או לתוך הכוס – even though it is a second-degree utensil, especially to make them tepid, it is permitted, but not a little bit of [cold] water in order to heat them, for the Tanna/teacher of this Mishnah holds a that a second-degree utensil cooks and further on [in this Mishnah] we are taught that he may put them into a tureen or a plate, which implies that a second-degree utensil does not cook and the Halakha is that a second-degree utensil does not cook.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:6)


**שֶׁהֶעֱבִירָן.** מִן הָאוּר:


שהעבירן – from the fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:7)


**מְרֻתָּחִין.** בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת:


מרותחין – at twilight.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:8)


**לֹא יִתֵּן לְתוֹכָן תְּבָלִין.** מִשֶּׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ, דִּכְלִי רִאשׁוֹן כָּל זְמַן שֶׁרוֹתֵחַ מְבַשֵּׁל:


לא יתן לתוכן תבלין – from after it gets dark for a first-degree utensil – all the time that it boils, it cooks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:9)


**אֲבָל נוֹתֵן הוּא לְתוֹךְ הַקְּעָרָה.** דִּכְלִי שֵׁנִי אֵינוֹ מְבַשֵּׁל:


אבל נותן הוא בתוך הקערה – for a second-degree utensil does not cook.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:10)


**תַּמְחוּי.** כְּמִין קְעָרָה גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁמְּעָרֶה כָּל הָאִלְפָּס לְתוֹכוֹ וּמִשָּׁם מְעָרֶה לַקְּעָרוֹת:


תמחוי – a kind of large plate that stirs up the entire tightly-covered stew pot into it and from there, stirs it into the plate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:11)


**לַכֹּל הוּא נוֹתֵן.** אֲפִלּוּ לִכְלִי רִאשׁוֹן:


לכל הוא נותן – even into a first-degree utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:5:12)


**חוּץ מִדָּבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ חֹמֶץ אוֹ צִיר.** הַנּוֹטֵף מִמְּלִיחַת דָּגִים, שֶׁהֵם מְבַשְּׁלִין הַתְּבָלִין, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וְדַוְקָא תְּבָלִין הוּא דְּאָסוּר לִתֵּן בִּכְלִי רִאשׁוֹן אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁהֶעֱבִירוֹ מִן הָאוּר, אֲבָל מֶלַח אֲפִלּוּ בִּכְלִי רִאשׁוֹן לֹא בָּשְׁלָהּ, אֶלָּא עַל גַּבֵּי הָאוּר בִּלְבַד, לְפִיכָךְ מֻתָּר לָתֵת מֶלַח אֲפִלּוּ בִּכְלִי רִאשׁוֹן לְאַחַר שֶׁהֶעֱבִירוֹ מִן הָאוּר:


חוץ מדבר שיש בו חומץ או ציר – that drips from the saltiness of the fish and they cook the spices, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda. But especially spices that are prohibited to place in a first-degree utensil even after they have removed it from the fire, but salt, even in a first-degree utensil does not cook, other than over the fire alone. Therefore, it is permissible to put salt, even in a first-degree utensil, after they have removed it from the fire.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:1)


**אֵין נוֹתְנִין.** בְּשַׁבָּת:


אין נותנין – on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:2)


**כְּלִי תַּחַת הַנֵּר לְקַבֵּל בּוֹ אֶת הַשֶּׁמֶן.** הַמְּטַפְטֵף, מִשּׁוּם דְּשֶׁמֶן מֻקְצֶה הוּא וְאָסוּר לְבַטֵּל כְּלִי מֵהֵיכָנוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר לְהוֹשִׁיב כְּלִי בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל עוֹד לִטְּלוֹ מִשָּׁם, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ כְּקוֹבֵעַ לוֹ מָקוֹם וּמְחַבְּרוֹ בְּטִיט, וְדָמִי לִמְלָאכָה, וּכְלִי זֶה מִשֶּׁיִּפֹּל בּוֹ הַשֶּׁמֶן מֻקְצֶה הוּא וְאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ:


כלי תחת הנר לקבל בו את השמן – that drips because the oil is Muktzeh/set aside and designated for a special purpose, and forbidden for any other use or handling and it is forbidden to nullify a utensil from its place, that is to say, to restore a utensil in a place that it is not able anymore to take it from there, for it is like he establishing for it a place and attaching it with plaster, and it is similar to creative work and this utensil, when the oil falls into it becomes Muktzeh and it is forbidden to handle it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:3)


**אֵין נֵאוֹתִין.** אֵין נֶהֱנִין מִן הַשֶּׁמֶן הַמְּטַפְטֵף מִן הַנֵּר בְּשַׁבָּת, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִן הַמּוּכָן, שֶׁכְּבָר הֻקְצָה לְהַדְלָקָה:


אין ניאותין – we don’t benefit from the dripping oil from the candle on the Sabbath because it is not from something prepared for it was designated for kindling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:4)


**מְטַלְטְלִין נֵר חָדָשׁ.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מָאוּס וְחָזֵי לְאִשְׁתַּמוּשֵׁי בֵּיהּ:


מטלטלין נר חדש – which is not repulsive, and it appropriate to use it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:5)


**אֲבָל לֹא יָשָׁן.** דְּמֻקְצֶה מֵחֲמַת מִאוּס הוּא:


אבל לא ישן – not counted on (and therefore forbidden to handle) on account of its repulsiveness (see Talmud Shabbat 44a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:6)


**חוּץ מִן הַנֵּר הַדּוֹלֵק.** בְּעוֹד שֶׁהוּא דּוֹלֵק אָסוּר, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יְכַבֶּה. וְלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מֻקְצֶה מֵחֲמַת מִאוּס, וְלֹא מֻקְצֶה מֵחֲמַת אִסּוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שֶׁמַּתִּיר לְטַלְטֵל כָּל הַנֵּרוֹת חוּץ מִן הַנֵּר הַדּוֹלֵק, שֶׁנֵּר שֶׁהִדְלִיקוּהוּ לְלֵילֵי שַׁבָּת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכָּבָה אָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ כָּל אוֹתוֹ שַׁבָּת, דְּמִגּוֹ דְּאִתְקְצַאי לְבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת אִתְקְצַאי לְכֻלֵּי יוֹמָא, אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר נֵרוֹת הֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ, שֶׁאֵין מֻקְצֶה לְשַׁבָּת אֶלָּא מֻקְצֶה מֵחֲמַת חֶסְרוֹן כִּיס, דְּמוֹדֶה בֵּיהּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


חוץ מן הנר הדולק – while it is still burning, it is prohibited, as a decree lest one extinguish it. But Rabbi Shimon does not agree with Muktzeh on account of its repulsiveness nor Muktzeh in consequence of a ritual prohibition (e.g., a candlestick which on the entrance of the Sabbath could not be moved because a light was burning on it), and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon who permits carrying all candles except from the candle that is burning for the candle, once it was kindled for the Sabbath evening, even though it is that it was extinguished, it is prohibited to carry it all the entire Shabbat, because once it became Muktzeh at twilight, it became Muktzeh for the entire [Sabbath] day, but [regarding] the other candles, the Halakha is according to him, for there is no Muktzeh on the Sabbath other than Muktzeh because the object is too expensive for a use for which it is not originally made, which Rabbi Shimon agrees with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:7)


**נוֹתְנִים כְּלִי תַחַת הַנֵּר.** בְּשַׁבָּת לְקַבֵּל נִיצוֹצוֹת שֶׁל שַׁלְהֶבֶת הַנּוֹטְפוֹת מִן הַנֵּר, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִדָּלֵק מַה שֶּׁתַּחְתָּיו, דְּנִיצוֹצוֹת אֵין בָּהֶם מַמָּשׁ, וְאֵין הַכְּלִי מְבֻטָּל מֵהֵיכָנוֹ בַּהֲכִי:


נותנים כלי תחת הנר – on Shabbat to receive the sparks of the flame that is dripping from the candle in order that it will not kindle what is below it, for the sparks have nothing of substance and the utensil is not canceled out from its use by this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 3:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/3:6:8)


**וְלֹא יִתֵּן לְתוֹכוֹ מַיִם.** וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, דְּגָזְרִינַן עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת אַטּוּ שַׁבָּת, וּבְשַׁבָּת אִי הֲוָה עָבֵיד כִּי הַאי גַּוְנָא הֲוֵי מְכַבֶּה וְחַיָּב, הִלְכָּךְ בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת אָסוּר:


לא יתן בתוכו מים – even from the Eve of the Sabbath, for they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed the Eve of the Sabbath on account of the Sabbath, and on the Sabbath if they did it in such a manner, it would be considered extinguishing and one would be liable. Therefore, it is prohibited on the Eve of the Sabbath.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:1)


**בַּמֶּה טוֹמְנִין.** הַבָּא לְסַלֵּק קְדֵרָה מֵעַל גַּבֵּי כִּירָה בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת וּלְטָמְנָהּ בְּדָבָר אַחֵר, וְאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים אֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל אֶלָּא בְּדָבָר הַמַּעֲמִיד הֶבֶל. אֵיזֶהוּ דָּבָר הַמּוֹסִיף וְאָסוּר:


במה טומנין – A person who comes to remove a pot from on to of a double stove on the Eve of the Sabbath and to cover it (i.e., put dishes in the chafing stove to keep them warm) with another thing, and the Sages said that we don’t cover them with something that adds vapor but rather with a thing that preserves the vapor. What is the thing that adds and is prohibited?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:2)


**לֹא בְגֶפֶת.** פְּסֹלֶת שֶׁל זֵיתִים וְשׁוּמְשְׁמִין, כְּשֶׁהוּא כָּנוּס יַחַד חַם מְאֹד:


לא בגפת – the refuse of olives and sesame when gathered together very hot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:3)


**לַחִים.** יֵשׁ בָּהֵן הֶבֶל הַרְבֵּה יוֹתֵר מִיְּבֵשִׁין:


לחים – they have a lot of vapor, more than when dry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:4)


**זָגִין.** קְלִפֵּי הָעֲנָבִים. חַרְצַנִּים, גַּרְעִינֵי הָעֲנָבִים:


זגין – grape peels, the pomace/shells of grapes, and seeds of grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:5)


**מוֹכִין.** כָּל דָּבָר רַךְ קָרוּי מוֹכִין, כְּגוֹן צֶמֶר גֶּפֶן וּתְלִישֵׁי צֶמֶר רַךְ שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה וּגְרִירַת בְּגָדִים בְּלוּיִים:


מוכין – every soft thing is called מוכין/soft, spongy substance – like wool of a vine, detached pieces of soft wool of an animal and the scrapings-off of outworn garments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:6)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵן לַחִין.** אַכֻּלְּהוּ קָאֵי, אַתֶּבֶן וְזָגִין וּמוֹכִין וַעֲשָׂבִים. וְלַחִין שֶׁאָמְרוּ, לַחִין מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָן, לֹא לַחִין מֵחֲמַת מַשְׁקִין שֶׁנָּפְלוּ עֲלֵיהֶן לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּבְשׁוּ, וּמוֹכִין לַחִין מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָן מַשְׁכַּחַת לַהּ כְּגוֹן בַּצֶּמֶר הַסָּמוּךְ לָאַלְיָה אוֹ בַּצֶּמֶר שֶׁבֵּין יַרְכוֹת הַבְּהֵמָה:


בזמן שהן לחין – all of these refer to straw and pomace of grapes and soft-spongy substance and grasses, and moist things that they mentioned [but] moist on account of themselves, not moist on account of liquids that fell upon them after they had dried and moist soft-spongy substances on account of themselves which we find like wool that is close to the tail or wool that is between the thigh of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:7)


**נְסֹרֶת.** פְּסֹלֶת שֶׁמְּנַסְּרִין הַנַּגָּרִים מִן הָעֵץ כְּשֶׁמְּגָרְרִים אוֹתוֹ בִּמְגֵרָה:


נסורת – the fine saw-dust/chips that the carpenters saw/plane from the tree when they saw it with a saw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:8)


**נְעֹרֶת.** דַּק דַּק שֶׁמְּנַעֲרִין מִן הַפִּשְׁתָּן כְּשֶׁמְּנַפְּצִין אוֹתוֹ:


נעורת – very thin, when they thoroughly beat the flax (Talmud Shabbat 49a), and they empty it out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:1:9)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹסֵר בְּדַקָּה.** בִּנְעֹרֶת שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן דַּקָּה, אֲבָל בִּנְסֹרֶת מוֹדֶה דְּשָׁרֵי בֵּין דַּקָּה בֵּין גַּסָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וּמַה שֶּׁאָסְרוּ לְהַטְמִין מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַטְמִין בְּרֶמֶץ וְיָבֹא לַחְתּוֹת בַּגֶּחָלִים מִשֶּׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ. וְאָסְרוּ לְהַטְמִין בְּשַׁבָּת בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְבַשֵּׁל, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְצָא קְדֵרָתוֹ שֶׁנִּצְטַנְּנָה וְיַרְתִּיחֶנָּה בָּאוּר בְּשַׁבָּת. וּבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת מֻתָּר לְהַטְמִין בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּסוֹף פֶּרֶק בַּמֶּה מַדְלִיקִין, דְּלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר שֶׁמָּא יִמְצָא קְדֵרָתוֹ שֶׁנִּצְטַנְּנָה וְיַרְתִּיחֶנָּה, דִּסְתָם קְדֵרוֹת בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת רוֹתְחוֹת הֵן. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ בִּסְתָם קְדֵרוֹת בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת רוֹתְחוֹת הֵן, פֵּרוּשׁ שֶׁאֵין הַדַּעַת סוֹבַלְתּוֹ, מִפְּנֵי שִׁבּוּשׁ הַנֻּסְחָאוֹת וְגִרְסָאוֹת מְהֻפָּכוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצָא בַּגְּמָרָא שֶׁלְּפָנָיו בְּפֶרֶק בַּמֶּה מַדְלִיקִין, שֶׁהוּא הָיָה גּוֹרֵס מִפְּנֵי מָה אָמְרוּ אֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל מִשֶּׁחָשֵׁכָה גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַטְמִין בְּרֶמֶץ, וּמִפְּנֵי מָה אָמְרוּ אֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַרְתִּיחַ, וְאֵין הַגִּרְסוֹת כֵּן, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי מָה אָמְרוּ אֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל מִשֶּׁחָשֵׁכָה, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַרְתִּיחַ, וְאֵין טוֹמְנִין בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַטְמִין בְּרֶמֶץ:


ר"י אוסר בדקה – thin hatchelled flax, but with fine chips/saw-dust he admits that they permit it whether they are thin or thick, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda. For what they (i.e., the Rabbis) prohibited to cover up while it was still day is a something that increases/adds vapor as a decree lest they cover it with hot ashes and he will come to stir the coals when it gets dark, and they forbid to cover it up on the Sabbath with something that does not increase vapor, and even though they do not nullify the decree lest they find his pot that has cooled off and heat it up with fire on the Sabbath. But at twilight [on Friday], it is permissible to cover it up with something that does not increase vapor as we said at the end of the chapter [two of Tractate Shabbat] “במה מדליקין ] – “With what may we kindle the Sabbath lights” for we are not able to make the decree lest he find that his pot has cooled off and he will heat it, for mere pots at twilight are hot. And Maimonides explained that mere pots at twilight are hot, as he explains, that the opinion does not suffer it because of the confusion of textual versions, for the textual versions are opposite each other, for he found in the Gemara that was before him in the chapter במה מדליקין/With what may we kindle the Sabbath candles – that he had the reading – for what reason did they say that we don’t cover it up with a thing that does not increase vapor once it becomes dark, as a decree lest he cover it with hot ashes, and for what reasons did they say that we don’t cover it with something that increases vapor while it is still daylight (on Friday) , decreed lest it becomes hot, and the readings are not such, but rather, for what reason did they say that we don’t cover them with a thing that does not increase vapor once it becomes dark as a decree lest it become hot and we don’t cover it with a thing that adds vapor while it is still daylight, as a decree lest he cover it with hot ashes.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:1)


**בִּשְׁלָחִין.** עוֹרוֹת. תַּרְגּוּם וְהִפְשִׁיט, וְיַשְׁלַח:


שלחין – hides, it is the Aramaic translation of “he stripped/flayed [skin] and stretched forth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:2)


**וּמְטַלְטְלִין אוֹתָן.** בֵּין שֶׁטָּמַן בָּהֶן בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא טָמַן בָּהֶן, דְּחָזוּ לְמִזְגָּא עֲלַיְהוּ, כְּלוֹמַר לְהִשָּׁעֵן עֲלֵיהֶם:


ומטלטלין אותן – whether he covered them or whether he didn’t cover them for it appears like he mixed them – that is to say, to rely upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:3)


**וְאֵין מְטַלְטְלִין אוֹתָן.** דְּמֻקְצוֹת הֵן לִטְווֹת וְלֶאֱרֹג, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטָּמַן בָּהֶן לְפִי שָׁעָה, לֹא הִפְקִירָן לְגַמְרֵי כְּשֶׁלֹּא יִחֲדָן לְהַטְמָנָה, אֲבָל יִחֲדָן לְהַטְמָנָה מְטַלְטְלָם:


ואין מטלטלין אותן – for they are Muktzeh/forbidden for use and/or handling on the Sabbath to spin or to weave, and even though he covered them for the time, he did not make them ownerless completely, and when he couldn’t designate them out for covering, but designated them for covering from carrying them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:4)


**כֵּיצַד יַעֲשֶׂה.** זֶה שֶׁטָּמַן בָּהֶם כֵּיצַד יִטֹּל קְדֵרָתוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלָן וַהֲרֵי הִיא טְמוּנָה כֻּלָּהּ בָּהֶן:


כיצד יעשה – this one who covered them, how shall he take his pot – since it is prohibited to carry them and it is all covered in them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:5)


**נוֹטֵל.** כִּסּוּי שֶׁל קְדֵרָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ תּוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן עָלָיו לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן, דְּלֹא נַעֲשָׂה בָּסִיס לָהֶן, שֶׁאֵין עָשׂוּי אֶלָּא לְכַסּוֹת אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה:


נוטל – the cover of the pot that has the status of a utensil upon it, and even though that they are upon it, and we don’t care that it was not make a base for them, for it was not made other than to cover the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:6)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר אוֹמֵר קֻפָּה מַטָּהּ עַל צִדָּהּ וְנוֹטֵל.** כְּשֶׁבָּא לִטְּלָהּ מַטֶּה אֶת הַקֻּפָּה עַל צִדָּהּ, שֶׁמָּא יִטֹּל אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה וְיִפְּלוּ הַגִּזִּין שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן לְתוֹךְ הַגֻּמָּא שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ הַקְּדֵרָה, וְאִם יִצְטָרֵךְ לַחְזֹר וּלְהַטְמִינָהּ לֹא יוּכַל לְטַלְטֵל הַגִּזִּין לְכָאן וּלְכָאן וְלַעֲשׂוֹת גֻּמָּא וּלְהַחְזִירָהּ לְתוֹכָהּ:


רבי אלעזר אומר קופה מטה על צדה ונוטל – when he comes to take it, he tilts the basket on its side lest he take the pot and the wool-sheerings that from one side and from the other into the hole that is in the pot and if it is required to go back and to cover it, he won’t be able to carry the sheerings from here and there and to make a hole and return them into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים נוֹטֵל.** אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה, וְאִם לֹא נָפְלוּ הַגִּזִּין שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן וְלֹא נִתְקַלְקְלָה הַגֻּמָּא מַחְזִיר הַקְּדֵרָה לִמְקוֹמָהּ, וְלֹא אָסְרִינַן לֵיהּ לִטֹּל אֶת הַקְּדֵרָה לְכַתְּחִלָּה גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא תִּתְקַלְקֵל הַגֻּמָּא. וּמוֹדִים חֲכָמִים שֶׁאִם נִתְקַלְקְלָה הַגֻּמָּא שֶׁלֹּא יַחְזִיר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכ"א נוטל – [The Sages state that he takes] the pot and if the wool- sheerings fell out, and the hole did not become ruined, he returns pot to its place, and they did not prohibit him to take the pot, ab initio, a decreed lest the hole become ruined. But the Sages admit that if the hole did become ruined, he should not return the objects, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:8)


**לֹא יְכַסֶּנּוּ מִשֶּׁתֶּחְשָׁךְ.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְהַטְמִין בְּשַׁבָּת בֵּין בְּדָבָר הַמּוֹסִיף הֶבֶל בֵּין בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹסִיף הֶבֶל בְּדֶרֶךְ הַטְמָנָה:


לא יכסנו משתחשך – for it is forbidden to cover it on the Sabbath, whether it is something that adds vapor, whether it is something that does not add vapor in the manner of covering it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:9)


**וְנוֹתֵן.** בְּשַׁבָּת, תַּחַת הַכַּר שֶׁמֵּשִׂים מְרַאֲשׁוֹתָיו, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מָלֵא מוֹכִין אוֹ נוֹצָה, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ הַטְמָנָה בְּכָךְ:


ונותן – on the Sabbath, under the pillow that he places under his head, and even though it is filled with spongy-wool substance or feathers, this is not the manner of covering with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 4:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/4:2:10)


**כֶּסֶת.** גָּדוֹל מִכַּר:


כסת – larger than a pillow.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:1)


**בַּמֶּה בְּהֵמָה יוֹצְאָה.** לְפִי שֶׁאָדָם מְצֻוֶּה עַל שְׁבִיתַת בְּהֶמְתּוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, וּמִידֵי דְּמִנְטְרָא בֵּיהּ הַבְּהֵמָה לֹא הֲוֵי כְּמַשְּׂאוֹי וּמִידֵי דְּלֹא מִנְטְרָא בֵּיהּ הֲוֵי כְּמַשְּׂאוֹי:


במה בהמה יוצאה – since a person is commanded regarding the resting of his animal on the Sabbath and anything that guards through it the animal is not like a burden and anything that does not guard it is like a burden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:2)


**אַפְסָר.** חֶבֶל שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים עַל פִּי הַבְּהֵמָה:


אפסר – a rope which ties the mouth of the animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:3)


**וְהַנָּאקָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ בְּנָאקָה לְבָנָה דַּוְקָא, שֶׁצְּרִיכָה נְטִירוּתָא יְתֵרְתָּא:


והנאקה – In the Gemara (Tractate Shabbat 51b), it establishes it specifically as a white camel which requires greater guarding/watching.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:4)


**בַּחֲטָם.** לוֹקְחִין כְּמִין טַבַּעַת שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל וְנוֹקְבִין חֹטֶם הַנָּאקָה וּמַכְנִיסִין אוֹתוֹ בְּתוֹכוֹ:


בחטם – they take a kind of iron ring and pierce the nose of the camel and place it inside it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:5)


**לֻבְדְּקִיס.** חֲמוֹר הַבָּא מִמְּדִינַת לוּד, וְהֵם קָשִׁים וַחֲזָקִים, וּצְרִיכִין שְׁמִירָה יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר חֲמוֹרִים הַנִּמְצָאִים בַּיִּשּׁוּב:


ולובדקס – a donkey that comes from Libya and they are hard and strong and need greater watching/guarding than other donkeys found in the settlement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:6)


**בִּפְרֻמְבְּיָא.** רֶסֶן שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


בפרומביא – an iron bridle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:7)


**בְּשֵׁיר.** כְּמִין אֶצְעָדָה סְבִיב צַוָּארוֹ וְטַבַּעַת קְבוּעָה בּוֹ, וּמַכְנִיסִין בַּטַּבַּעַת חֶבֶל וּמוֹשְׁכִין הַבְּהֵמָה:


בשיר – a kind of clasp/brooch around its neck and a ring is fixed within it and they bring in a rope through the ring and pull the animal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:8)


**וְכָל בַּעֲלֵי הַשֵּׁיר.** כְּגוֹן כְּלָבִים שֶׁל צַיָּדִים וְחַיּוֹת קְטַנּוֹת:


וכל בעלי השיר - such as hunting dogs and small animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:9)


**יוֹצְאִים בַּשֵּׁיר.** כָּרוּךְ עַל צַוָּארָן הַחֶבֶל הַקָּבוּעַ בַּשֵּׁיר:


יוצאים בשיר – tied on their necks with a rope set in the clasp.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:10)


**וְנִמְשָׁכִין בַּשֵּׁיר.** וְאִם רָצָה מוֹשֵׁךְ הַבְּהֵמָה בַּחֶבֶל שֶׁבַּשֵּׁיר:


ונמשכין בשיר – and if he wants he pulls the animal with the rope in the clasp/brooch.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:11)


**וּמַזִּין עֲלֵיהֶן.** בִּמְקוֹמָן כְּמוֹת שֶׁהֵן בְּצַוַּאר הַבְּהֵמָה, אִם נִטְמְאוּ בְּמֵת:


ומזין עליהן – in their places as they are in the neck of the animal if it became ritually defiled through [contact with] a dead corpse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:1:12)


**וְטוֹבְלִין בִּמְקוֹמָן.** מַכְנִיסִים הַבְּהֵמָה בַּמַּיִם לְהַטְבִּיל הַשֵּׁיר. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן שֶׁכָּל הַכֵּלִים הַמְּיֻחָדִים לַבְּהֵמָה אֵין מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה, הַשֵּׁיר וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה וְצָרִיךְ טְבִילָה, הוֹאִיל וְנַעֲשׂוּ לָאָדָם שֶׁיַּנְהִיג בּוֹ אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה, כִּכְלִי הֶעָשׂוּי לְתַשְׁמִישׁ אָדָם דָּמִי:


וטובלין במקומן – they have the animal enter the water to ritually immerse the brooch/clasp and even though we hold that all utensils unique to the animal do not receive ritual defilement. The brooch/clasp and other things like it receive ritual defilement and require ritual immersion since they are made for humans to lead with it an animal as it is like a utensil made for usage by a human being.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:1)


**בְּמַרְדַּעַת.** כְּמִין אֻכָּף קָטָן, וּמְנִיחִים אוֹתוֹ עַל הַחֲמוֹר כָּל הַיּוֹם כֻּלּוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְחַמֵּם:


מרדעת – a kind of small saddle and we place it on the donkey all day long in order that the donkey can be kept warm.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:2)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהִיא קְשׁוּרָה לוֹ.** מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, דְּגַלֵּי דַּעְתֵּיהּ שֶׁהַחֲמוֹר צָרִיךְ לוֹ לְחַמְּמוֹ, כִּדְאָמְרֵי אֱנָשֵׁי חַמְרָא אֲפִלּוּ בִּתְקוּפַת תַּמּוּז קָרִיר לֵיהּ, וְלָאו מַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא. אֲבָל לִקְשֹׁר מַרְדַּעַת עַל הַחֲמוֹר בְּשַׁבָּת אָסוּר, לְפִי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְקָשְׁרָהּ לוֹ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן סוֹמֵךְ עַצְמוֹ בְּצִדֵּי הַבְּהֵמָה וְנִמְצָא מִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּבַעַל חַיִּים, וְאִם עָבַר וּקְשָׁרוֹ אָסוּר לָצֵאת בּוֹ:


בזמן שהיא קשורה לו – from the Eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday) that he reveals his opinion that the donkey needs it to warm it up as people say that a donkey, even in the season of Tammuz (i.e., the middle of the summer) is cold for it, and it is not a burden but to tie/attach a cushion/pack-saddle on the donkey is prohibited because it is impossible to tie it on him other than if he supports it on the side of the animal and it is found that a living creature is being used and if he transgressed and tied it on, it is prohibited to go out with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:3)


**לְבוּבִין.** בָּעוֹר שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים לָהֶם כְּנֶגֶד זַכְרוּת שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלוּ עַל הַנְּקֵבוֹת:


לבובין – with leather that is we tie them up corresponding to their male genitalia so that they will not go up on the females.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:4)


**שְׁחוּזוֹת.** שֶׁאוֹחֲזִים אַלְיָה שֶׁלָּהֶן קְשׁוּרָה לְמַעְלָה כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעֲלוּ עֲלֵיהֶן זְכָרִים:


שחוזות – that grasps their tails tied upwards so that the males will come upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:5)


**כְּבוּלוֹת.** שֶׁכּוֹבְלִים אַלְיָה שֶׁלָּהֶן וְקוֹשְׁרִים אוֹתָהּ בְּרַגְלֵיהֶן כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלוּ עֲלֵיהֶן זְכָרִים:


כבולות – they chain up their tails and tie them with their legs in order that the males will not come upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:6)


**כְּבוּנוֹת.** שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים בֶּגֶד סְבִיב הַכְּבָשִׂים בַּיּוֹם שֶׁנּוֹלָדִים לִשְׁמֹר צַמְרָן שֶׁלֹּא יִטָּנֵף:


כבונות – they tie a cloth around the lambs during the day to protect their wool so that it not become soiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:7)


**צְרוּרוֹת.** דַּדֵּיהֶן צְרוּרוֹת, פְּעָמִים לְיַבֵּשׁ הֶחָלָב, שֶׁמְּהַדְּקִים אוֹתָן בְּחֹזֶק, וּפְעָמִים קוֹשְׁרִין לָהֶן כִּיס בְּדַדֵּיהֶן שֶׁלֹּא יְטַפְטֵף הֶחָלָב לָאָרֶץ וְיִפָּסֵד:


צרורות – their breast nipples are tied up sometimes to dry up the milk when they squeeze them with strength and sometimes they tie a pocket on their nipples so that the milk will not drip to the ground and be lost.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:8)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹסֵר בְּכֻלָּן.** דְּמַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא:


ר' יוסי אומר בכולן – that is a burden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:9)


**חוּץ מִן הַכְּבוּנוֹת.** שֶׁהוּא שְׁמִירַת צַמְרָן שֶׁלֹּא יִטָּנֵף וַהֲוֵי לְהוּ תַּכְשִׁיט:


חוץ מן הכבונות - which is protection for their wool so that it will not be soiled and it is an ornament/decoration.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:2:10)


**יוֹצְאוֹת צְרוּרוֹת לְיַבֵּשׁ.** רַבִּי יְהוּדָה סְבִירָא לֵיהּ כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּלָאו מַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא, מִיהוּ לְיַבֵּשׁ דְּמִהַדֵּק שַׁפִּיר לֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר דִּלְמָא נָפִיל וְאָתֵי לַאֲתוֹיֵי, אֲבָל לְחָלָב דְּלֹא מִהַדֵּק שַׁפִּיר אָסוּר, חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא נָפִיל וְאָתֵי לַאֲתוֹיֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


יוצאות צרורורת ליבש – Rabbi Yehudah holds like the first teacher [of the Mishnah] that it is not a burden; however, to [keep them] dry which is fastened better one cannot make this decree lest it miscarry which it comes to include, but for milk where it is not fastened well, it is forbidden and we suspect lest it miscarry which it comes to include. And the Halakha is according to the first teacher [of the Mishnah].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:1)


**מְטוּטֶלֶת.** חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל מַטְלִית קְשׁוּרָה בִּזְנָבוֹ לְסִימָן אוֹ לְדָבָר אַחֵר:


מטוטלת – a piece of strip/lining tied to its tail as a sign or for something else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:2)


**עָקוּד.** שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִים יְדֵיהֶם עִם הָרַגְלַיִם בִּכְבָלִים שֶׁלֹּא יִבְרְחוּ:


עקוד – they tie their hands and legs with chains so that they do not escape.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:3)


**רָגוּל.** שֶׁכּוֹפְפִים יָדוֹ עַל זְרוֹעוֹ וְקוֹשְׁרִין:


רגול – fold his hand over his arm and tie them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:4)


**לֹא יִקְשֹׁר גְּמַלִּים זֶה בָזֶה וְיִמְשֹׁךְ.** אֶחָד, וְכֻלָּן הוֹלְכִין, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה כְּמוֹלִיכָן לַשּׁוּק לְמָכְרָן:


לא יקשור גמלים זה בזה וימשוך – as one and they all go so that it would not appear as leading them to the market to sell them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:5)


**אֲבָל מַכְנִיס הוּא חֲבָלִים לְתוֹךְ יָדוֹ.** וְהוּא שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ רָאשֵׁי הַחֲבָלִים תְּלוּיִין וְיוֹצְאִין מִתַּחַת יָדוֹ לָאָרֶץ טֶפַח אוֹ יוֹתֵר, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה כְּמוֹ שֶׁנּוֹשֵׂא חֲבָלִים תְּלוּיִין בְּיָדוֹ:


אבל מכניס הוא חבלים בתוך ידו – so that the heads of the ropes would not be hanging and coming out from under his hands to the ground by a handbreadth or more in order that it would not appear like he is carrying the hanging ropes by his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:3:6)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִכְרֹךְ.** לָאו לְעִנְיַן שַׁבָּת קָא מַיְרֵי הַשְׁתָּא אֶלָּא לְעִנְיַן כִּלְאַיִם, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, הַמַּכְנִיס חֲבָלִים בְּיָדוֹ קְצָתָם שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן וּקְצָתָם שֶׁל צֶמֶר, לֹא יִכְרֹךְ זוֹ בְּזוֹ, דְּכִי כָּרִיךְ לְהוּ הֲווּ כִּלְאַיִם, וְיָדוֹ מִתְחַמֶּמֶת בַּאֲחִיזָתָן וְאָסוּר:


ובלבד שלא יכרוך – this is not in speaking in regard to the matter of the Sabbath here, but to the matter of mixed seeds and this is how it should be understood: He who gathers ropes in his hand, some of them of flax and some of them of wool, he should not twist one with the other for when he twists them, it is mixed seeds and his hand is warmed while holding them and this is forbidden.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:1)


**בְּזוֹג.** כְּמִין פַּעֲמוֹן שֶׁתּוֹלִין בְּצַוַּאר הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁתַּשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל בַּהֲלִיכָתָהּ:


with a bell - A bell hung around the neck of the animal which emits a sound when it moves


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא פָקוּק.** בְּמוֹכִין, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵין הָעִנְבָּל שֶׁלּוֹ מְקַשְׁקֵשׁ לְהַשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּרְאֶה כְּמוֹלִיכוֹ לִמְכֹּר בַּשּׁוּק:


אע"פ שהוא פקוק – [plugged] with a soft-spongy substance, for now its clapper of the bell does not ring to cause a sound to be made because it appears like he is leading it to be sold in the marketplace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:3)


**בְּסֻלָּם שֶׁבְּצַוָּארוֹ.** כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מַכָּה נוֹתְנִים בְּצַוָּארוֹ עֵצִים כְּמִין שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַחְזֹר רֹאשׁוֹ לְחַכֵּךְ חַבּוּרָתוֹ:


בסולם שבצוארו – when it has a wound, they place on its neck wood in the shape of warp and woof in order that it not turn its head to grasp at his wound.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:4)


**וְלֹא בִרְצוּעָה שֶׁבְּרַגְלוֹ.** בְּהֵמָה שֶׁמַּכָּה רַגְלֶיהָ זוֹ בְּזוֹ בַּהֲלִיכָתָהּ, עוֹשִׂין לָהּ כְּמִין טַבַּעַת שֶׁל רְצוּעָה עָבָה וְקוֹשְׁרִים אוֹתָהּ בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁרַגְלֶיהָ מְנַקְּשׁוֹת זוֹ בְּזוֹ:


ולא ברצועה שברגלו – an animal that strikes its legs against each other while it is walking, they make a sort of ring of a thick strap and tie it at the place where its legs strike against each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:5)


**בְּחוּטִין.** שֶׁעוֹשִׂים לָהֶן לְסִימָנָא, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְחַלְּפוּ בְּתַרְנְגוֹלוֹת אֲחֵרִים:


בחוטין - they make it as a sign so that exchange it for other chickens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:6)


**וְלֹא בִרְצוּעוֹת.** שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין שְׁתֵּי רַגְלֵיהֶן יַחַד בִּרְצוּעָה קְצָרָה, שֶׁלֹּא יְדַלְּגוּ וְיִשְׁבְּרוּ אֶת הַכֵּלִים:


ולא ברצועות – that they tie/fasten its two legs together with a short cords so that they don’t jump and break the utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:7)


**בַּעֲגָלָה שֶׁתַּחַת הָאַלְיָה.** כְּמִין עֲגָלָה קְטַנָּה קוֹשְׁרִין תַּחַת הָאַלְיָה שֶׁל הַכְּבָשִׂים שֶׁאַלְיָתָן גְּדוֹלָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּלְקֶה בָּאֲבָנִים וּבַסְּלָעִים:


בעגלה שתחת האליה – like a small wagon which they tie under the tail of the sheep for their tails are large in order that it does not become damaged by stones and rocks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:8)


**חֲנוּנוֹת.** עֵץ אֶחָד יֵשׁ שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ יַחְנוּן, מְבִיאִין קֵיסָם מִמֶּנּוּ וּמְנִיחִין לָהּ בְּחָטְמָהּ כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּתְעַטֵּשׁ וְיִפְּלוּ הַתּוֹלָעִים שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ. וְלַזְּכָרִים אֵין צְרִיכִין לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן, שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ שֶׁמְּנַגְּחִים זֶה בָּזֶה נוֹפְלִים הַתּוֹלָעִים מֵאֲלֵיהֶם:


חנונות – there is a kind of wood called חנון where they bring chips from it and place it on its nose in order that it should sneeze and the worms from its head would fall off but for the males, it is not necessary to do this for when they gore each other, the worms fall off on their own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:9)


**בְּגִימוֹן.** כְּמִין עֹל שֶׁל גֶּמִי נוֹתְנִים עַל צַוַּאר הָעֵגֶל, שֶׁיְּהֵא לָמוּד לָכוֹף רֹאשׁוֹ כְּשֶׁיִּגְדַּל:


בגימון – like a reed-grass yoke that is placed on the neck of the calf in order that it would be accustomed to bend its neck when it gets older.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:10)


**בְּעוֹר הַקֻּפָּר.** שֶׁרֶץ שֶׁנִּימָיו חַדִּין כְּמַחַט, וְקוֹשְׁרִין עוֹרוֹ בְּדַדֵּי הַפָּרָה שֶׁלֹּא יִינְקוּהָ הַשְּׁרָצִים:


בעור הקופר – a reptile that its fibers are thin like a needle and we tie its skin at the nipples of the cow so that the vermin do not suck [its milk].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:11)


**וְלֹא בִרְצוּעָה שֶׁבֵּין קַרְנֶיהָ.** בֵּין לְנוֹי בֵּין לִשְׁמֹר אָסוּר, דְּכָל נְטִירוּתָא יְתֵרְתָּא מַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא:


ולא ברצועה שבין קרניה – whether for beauty or to protect it, it is forbidden for all additional protection is a burden for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 5:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/5:4:12)


**פָּרָתוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה.** לֹא שֶׁלּוֹ הָיְתָה אֶלָּא שֶׁל שְׁכֶנְתּוֹ, וְעַל שֶׁלֹּא מִחָה בָּהּ נִקְרֵאת עַל שְׁמוֹ:


פרתו של רבי אלעזר בן עזריה – it did not belong to him, but to his neighbor and because he did not protest it [as it was forbidden] , it is called by his name.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:1)


**בַּמֶּה אִשָּׁה.** שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ. אַכֻּלְּהוּ קָאֵי, אַחוּטֵי צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתָּן וּרְצוּעָה שֶׁמְּקַלַּעַת בָּהֵן שֵׂעָר שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ, וּמַה טַּעַם לֹא תֵצֵא בָּהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים בְּחֹל לֹא תִטְבֹּל בָּהֶם עַד שֶׁתְּרַפֵּם, לְפִיכָךְ בְּשַׁבָּת לֹא תֵצֵא בָּהֶם, דִּלְמָא מִיתְרַמְיָא לַהּ טְבִילָה שֶׁל מִצְוָה וְשָׁרְיָא לְהוּ, וְאָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


במה אשה. שבראשה – it refers to all with threads of wool and flax and a strap that plaits/twists on them the hair of her head. And what is the reason that she should not go out with them on the Sabbath? Because the Sages stated that she should not immerse in them on weekdays until they become weak/decay, therefore on the Sabbath, she should not go out in them lest perhaps she remove it when she is engaging in the commanded ritual immersion and unties them and comes to carry them four cubits in the public domain


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:2)


**עַד שֶׁתְּרַפֵּם.** שֶׁתַּתִּירֵם קְצָת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ רְפוּיִין וְיִכָּנְסוּ הַמַּיִם בֵּינֵיהֶם, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ חוֹצְצִים בַּטְּבִילָה:


עד שתרפם – to permeate/penetrate them a bit and that they would be softened and that water could enter between them so that they would not leave a space between the partitions during the ritual immersion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:3
