[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:3)


**בְּטֹטֶפֶת.** צִיץ שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין עַל הַמֵּצַח מֵאֹזֶן לְאֹזֶן:


בטוטפת – a fringe that they tie on the forehead from ear to ear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:4)


**סַנְבּוּטִין.** תְּלוּיִין בַּטֹּטֶפֶת וּבָאִין עַל הַצְּדָעַיִם עַד הַלְּחָיַיִם. עֲנִיּוֹת עוֹשׂוֹת אוֹתָן שֶׁל מִינֵי צִבְעוֹנִין, עֲשִׁירוֹת עוֹשׂוֹת אוֹתָן שֶׁל כֶּסֶף וְשֶׁל זָהָב. וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁחֲשׁוּבִין הֵן חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְיָא לַחֲבֶרְתָּהּ:


סנבוטין – hanging on the forehead ornament and comes on the temples until the jaw. Poor women make them of kinds of colors/dyed stuff; the rich make them of silver or gold and because they are important, they suspect lest they loosen and point to its neighbor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:5)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵינָן תְּפוּרִין.** בַּשְּׂבָכָה שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ, אֲבָל תְּפוּרִים לֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ לַאֲתוֹיֵי, שֶׁאֵינָהּ נוֹטֶלֶת הַשְּׂבָכָה מֵרֹאשָׁהּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִיא מְגַלָּה שְׂעָרָהּ:


בזמן שאינן תפורין – with her head-dress on her head but if they are sewn on, one doesn’t have to fear to include it for one does not take the head-dress/netting from her head in the public domain, for she would reveal her hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:6)


**בְּכָבוּל.** חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל בֶּגֶד כְּמוֹ מִצְנֶפֶת קְטַנָּה שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין אוֹתָהּ עַל הַמֶּצַח, וְנוֹתְנִין הַצִּיץ עָלֶיהָ, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיק הַצִּיץ בַּמֵּצַח, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה מִתְקַשֶּׁטֶת בּוֹ בְּלֹא צִיץ:


בכבול – a piece of cloth like a small turban that we tie on the forehead and place the fringe on it so that one would not damage the fringe on the forehead, but sometimes the woman is decorated with it without the fringe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:7)


**לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** אֲבָל לֶחָצֵר שָׁרֵי. וְכָל הַנִּזְכָּר לְמַעְלָה אֲסוּרִים אַף בֶּחָצֵר, דְּגָזוּר בְּהוּ שֶׁלֹּא תִּתְקַשֵּׁט בְּשַׁבָּת כְּלָל, לֹא בֶּחָצֵר וְלֹא בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּבְכָבוּל הִתִּירוּ, שֶׁלֹּא לֶאֱסֹר אֶת כָּל תַּכְשִׁיטֶיהָ וְתִתְגַנֶּה עַל בַּעֲלָהּ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ דְּלִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים קָאֵי אַכֻּלְּהוּ תַּכְשִׁיטִין הָאֲמוּרִין בְּמַתְנִיתִין, דְּכֻלְּהוּ לֹא אֲסִירִי אֶלָּא גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא תַּעֲבִירֵם הָאִשָּׁה אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


לרה"ר – but to the courtyard, it is permitted, and everything mentioned above is prohibited, even in the courtyard, for they decreed concerning this that a woman should not decorate herself on Shabbat at all, neither in the courtyard nor in the public domain, but the hair-net they permitted so as to not prohibit all of her ornaments and become repulsive to her husband. But Maimonides explained that the public domain refers to all of the ornaments that are mentioned in our Mishnah and all of them are not forbidden but as a decree lest a woman bring them four cubits within the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:8)


**עִיר שֶׁל זָהָב.** עֲטֶרֶת זָהָב עֲשׂוּיָה כְּמִין עִיר צוּרַת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


עיר של זהב – a golden crown made like the shape of Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:9)


**וְלֹא בְקַטְלָא.** תַּכְשִׁיט שֶׁנָּתוּן בַּצַּוָּאר בְּדֹחַק וְהָאִשָּׁה חוֹנֶקֶת עַצְמָהּ בּוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה בַּעֲלַת בָּשָׂר, וּבְעַרְבִי מכנק"א:


ולא בקטלא - an ornament that is placed on the neck in an emergency and a woman chokes herself with it in order that she shows that she shows her flesh, and in Arabic, “Makneka.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:10)


**וְלֹא בִנְזָמִים.** נִזְמֵי הָאַף, אֲבָל נִזְמֵי הָאֹזֶן יוֹצְאִין בָּהֶם:


ולא בנזמים – nose-rings, but earrings, one can out with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:11)


**חוֹתָם.** צוּרָה לַחְתֹּם בָּהּ אִגְּרוֹת אוֹ כָּל דְּבַר סֵתֶר, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּתַכְשִׁיט הוּא לָהּ אָסוּר, דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְיָא, אֲבָל יֵשׁ עָלֶיהָ חוֹתָם, דְּלָאו תַּכְשִׁיט הוּא לָהּ, אָמְרִינַן לְקַמָּן דְּחַיֶּבֶת חַטָּאת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמּוֹצִיאָתוֹ בְּאֶצְבָּעָהּ דֶּרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ, לְפִי שֶׁפְּעָמִים שֶׁהַבַּעַל מֵסִיר אוֹתוֹ מֵאֶצְבָּעוֹ וְנוֹתֵן אוֹתוֹ לְאִשְׁתּוֹ לְהַצְנִיעוֹ, וְהִיא נוֹתַנְתּוֹ בְּאֶצְבָּעָהּ וְהוֹלֶכֶת בּוֹ, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הוֹצָאָתוֹ בְּכָךְ. וְכֵן טַבַּעַת שֶׁאֵין עָלֶיהָ חוֹתָם דְּלֹא הֲוֵי תַּכְשִׁיט לָאִישׁ, חַיָּב חַטָּאת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמּוֹצִיאוֹ בְּאֶצְבָּעוֹ דֶּרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ, שֶׁפְּעָמִים שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה נוֹתַנְתּוֹ לוֹ שֶׁיּוֹלִיכֶנּוּ לָאֻמָּן, וְהוּא מוֹצִיאוֹ בְּאֶצְבָּעוֹ:


חותם – a form to seal letters or any secret thing and even though it is an ornament, it is forbidden lest she loosen it and point it out but if it has a seal, it is not an ornament for her, we say further on that she is liable for a sin-offering and even though she brings it out with her finger in the manner of clothing, because sometimes the husband removes it from his finger and gives it to his wife to hide it and she puts it on her finger and walks with it, and it is found that in the manner of removing it and similarly a ring that has no seal on it that is not an ornament for a man for it is not an ornament for a man, he would be liable for a sin-offering even though he removes it from his finger in the manner of clothing, for sometimes a woman will give it to him that he would take it to an artisan and he removes it with his finger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:1:12)


**וְאִם יָצָאת.** הָאִשָּׁה בְּכָל הָנָךְ דְּאָסְרִינַן עַד הָכָא בְּמַתְנִיתִין, אֵינָהּ חַיֶּבֶת חַטָּאת, דְּכֻלְּהוּ תַּכְשִׁיטִין נִינְהוּ, וְרַבָּנָן הוּא דְּגָזוּר בְּהוּ דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְיָא:


ואם יצאת - if a woman went out with all of these that we forbid until here in our Mishnah, she is not liable for a sin-offering for all of these are ornaments and the Rabbis are the ones who decreed lest she loosen it and show it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:1)


**בְּסַנְדָּל הַמְּסֻמָּר.** שֶׁל עֵץ הוּא, וְתוֹחֲבִים בּוֹ מַסְמְרוֹת לְחַזְּקוֹ, וַאֲסָרוּהוּ בְּשַׁבָּת וּבְיוֹם טוֹב מִשּׁוּם מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁהָיָה, שֶׁפַּעַם אַחַת הָיוּ נֶחְבָּאִים בַּמְּעָרָה מִפְּנֵי הַגְּזֵרָה, וְשָׁמְעוּ קוֹל מֵעַל גַּבֵּי הַמְּעָרָה, כִּסְבוּרִין שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶן הֵן בָּאִים, דָּחֲקוּ זֶה אֶת זֶה וְהָרְגוּ זֶה אֶת זֶה בַּמַּסְמְרִים שֶׁבְּסַנְדְּלֵיהֶן, וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּעֲשֶׂה זֶה בְּשַׁבָּת הָיָה אֲסָרוּהוּ בְּשַׁבָּת וּבְיוֹם טוֹב שֶׁהוּא יוֹם שֶׁל כְּנוּפְיָא כְּמוֹ שַׁבָּת:


בסנדל המסומר – [a nail-studded shoe] of wood in which they fasten nails to strengthen it and they prohibited it on the Sabbath and on Jewish holy days because of an incident that took place that one time, they were hiding in a cave because of the decree [of the Romans against Torah study] and they heard a sound on top of the cave, and they thought that they were coming upon them, they pushed each other and killed each other with the nails that were in their sandals/shoes and because this event took place on Shabbat, they forbade it on Shabbat and on Jewish holy days which is a day of crowds/assembly like the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:2)


**וְלֹא בְיָחִיד.** וְלֹא בְּסַנְדָּל יָחִיד:


ולא ביחיד – and not with a singular sandal/shoe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בְּרַגְלוֹ מַכָּה.** אִית דְּאָמְרֵי טַעְמָא שֶׁמָּא יַחְשְׁדוּהוּ שֶׁהַסַּנְדָּל הַשֵּׁנִי טָמוּן לוֹ תַּחַת כְּנָפָיו וּמוֹצִיאוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי דִּלְמָא מְחַיְכֵי עֲלֵיהּ וְשָׁלֵיף לֵיהּ לָזֶה שֶׁבְּרַגְלוֹ וּמַיְתֵי לֵיהּ בְּיָדֵיהּ. וּמִיהוּ בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרַגְלוֹ מַכָּה שָׁרֵי לָצֵאת בְּסַנְדָּל יְחִידִי בָּרֶגֶל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַכָּה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּכָּתוֹ מוֹכַחַת עָלָיו:


בזמן שאין ברגלו מכה – that are those who say that the reason is lest someone suspect him that the second sandal is hidden under his cloak/arms and he takes it out on Shabbat; and there are those who say that perhaps he is making sport about it and pulls it off to the one that is on his foot and brings it in his hand. But however, at the time that there is a wound on his foot it is permitted to go out with an individual sandal on his foot that has not found because his wound forces it upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:4)


**וְלֹא בְקָמֵעַ.** שֶׁתּוֹלִין לִרְפוּאָה:


ולא בקמיע – that he suspends for healing purposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:5)


**שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִן הַמֻּמְחֶה.** מֵאָדָם מֻמְחֶה שֶׁרִפֵּא שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּנֵי אָדָם, אֲבָל קָמֵעַ הַנִּלְקָח מֵאָדָם מֻמְחֶה שֶׁרִפֵּא שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּנֵי אָדָם בִּשְׁאָר קְמֵעוֹת, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלָאו אִיתְמַחֵי קְמֵעָא שָׁרֵי, דְּתַכְשִׁיט הוּא לַחוֹלֶה כְּאֶחָד מִמַּלְבּוּשָׁיו:


שאינו מן המומחה – from an individual specialist who has healed three people, but an amulet which has been taken from an individual specialist who healed three individuals with the rest of the amulets even though he was not smitten, the amulet is permitted for it is an ornament/decoration for the sick person like his one of his clothing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:6)


**בְּקַסְדָּא.** כּוֹבַע שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל:


וקסדא – a bronze helmet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:2:7)


**מַגִּיפִין.** כְּמִין בָּתֵּי שׁוֹקַיִם שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל שֶׁלּוֹבְשִׁים בַּמִּלְחָמָה, וּלְפִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹבְשִׁין אוֹתָן אֶלָּא בִּשְׁעַת מִלְחָמָה, אָסוּר לְלָבְשָׁן בְּשַׁבָּת:


מגיפין – kind of bronze leggings that one wears in battle and because they are not warn other during the time of war, it is prohibited to wear them on the Sabbath.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:1)


**בְּמַחַט נְקוּבָה.** שֶׁתּוֹפְרִים בָּהּ, דְּלָאו תַּכְשִׁיט הוּא, וְחַיֶּבֶת חַטָּאת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמּוֹצִיאָה אוֹתָהּ בְּמַלְבּוּשֶׁיהָ וְלֹא בְּיָדָהּ, שֶׁאֻמָּן שֶׁהוֹצִיא דֶּרֶךְ אֻמָּנוּתוֹ חַיָּב חַטָּאת:


במחט נקובה – that they sew with it, and it is not an adornment and she is liable for a sin-offering even though she takes it out on her clothing and not in her hand, for an artisan who took it out in the manner of his artisanship is liable for a sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:2)


**בְּכוֹלְיָאר.** כְּלִי שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה מְעַגֶּלֶת בְּרֹאשָׁהּ כְּמִין טַבַּעַת שֶׁמְּסַבֵּב הָרֹאשׁ, וְהוּא מַשְּׂאוֹי, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין רֹב הַנָּשִׁים יוֹצְאוֹת בּוֹ:


בכוליאר – a utensil that a woman draws a circle on her head like a ring that surrounds the head, and it is a burden, because most women do not go out with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:3)


**בְּכוֹבֶלֶת.** קֶשֶׁר שֶׁל כֶּסֶף אוֹ שֶׁל זָהָב שֶׁקָּשׁוּר בְּתוֹכוֹ בֹּשֶׂם לְהַעֲבִיר רֵיחַ רַע שֶׁבָּאִשָּׁה:


בכובלת – a band of silver or of gold that has a perfume tied to it to remove bad odors that a woman has.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:4)


**שֶׁל פַּלְיָטוֹן.** מֹר, שֶׁקּוֹרִין מוסק"ו:


של פלייטון – myrrh that we call “Musko.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:3:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹטְרִין.** דְּסָבְרֵי תַּכְשִׁיטִין נִינְהוּ, וּלְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא רַשָּׁאָה דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְיָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים פוטרין – for they hold that these are adornments and ab initio, she is not allowed, for perhaps she will loosen it/pull it off to show it. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:1)


**לֹא יֵצֵא.** אִם לֹא לְצֹרֶךְ מִלְחָמָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:2)


**תְּרִיס.** מָגֵן עָשׂוּי כְּתַבְנִית מְשֻׁלָּשׁ:


תריס – a shield made in a three-fold shape.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:3)


**אַלָּה.** מָגֵן עָגֹל, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם שֶׁל עֵץ. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי אַלָּה מצ"א בְּלַעַ"ז, וּבָעֲרָבִי דסו"ס:


אלה – a round shield and both are made of wood, but I heard that אלה is a “Matza/Mamtze” in the foreign language and in Arabic “Dasus/Davus.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:4)


**וְכִתְּתוּ חַרְבוֹתָם לְאִתִּים.** וְאִי תַּכְשִׁיטִין נִינְהוּ לֹא יִהְיוּ בְּטֵלִים לֶעָתִיד:


וכתתו חרבותם לאתים – but if they are ornaments, they would not be idle in the future.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:5)


**בִּירִית.** אֶצְעָדָה עַל הַשּׁוֹק לְהַחְזִיק בָּתֵּי שׁוֹקַיִם שֶׁלֹּא יִפְּלוּ וְיֵרָאוּ שׁוֹקֶיהָ, הִלְכָּךְ טְהוֹרָה דְּלָאו תַּכְשִׁיט לְנוֹי הוּא, וּכְלִי תַּשְׁמִישׁ נַמִּי לֹא הֲוֵי אֶלָּא כְּלִי הַמְּשַׁמֵּשׁ כְּלִי, דּוּמְיָא דְּטַבְּעוֹת הַכֵּלִים שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרִים:


בירית – a brooch/clasp on the leg to hold the leggings so that they don’t fall and people would see her legs; therefore, she is pure, and it is not an adornment for beauty (see Tractate Shabbat 63a), and it is a useful utensil which is none other than a utensil that guards another utensil, similar to the rings of utensils that are pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:6)


**יוֹצְאִין בָּהּ.** דְּדֶרֶךְ לְבִישָׁה הִיא, וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וּמְחַוְיָא דְּלֹא מְגַלְּיָא שׁוֹקָהּ:


יוצאין בה – for it is the manner of dress and one should not fear lest it becomes loose and she shows it, so that she will reveal her leg.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:7)


**כְּבָלִים טְמֵאִין.** מִשְׁפָּחָה אַחַת הָיְתָה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁהָיוּ פְּסִיעוֹתֵיהֶן גַּסּוֹת וְהָיוּ בְּתוּלֵיהֶן נוֹשְׁרִים, וְהֵטִילוּ שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת בֵּין בִּירִית לְבִירִית שֶׁבְּשׁוֹקֶיהָ, וְנַעֲשָׂה כְּבָלִים, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ פְּסִיעוֹתֵיהֶן גַּסּוֹת וְלֹא יִהְיוּ בְּתוּלֵיהֶן נוֹשְׁרִים, הִלְכָּךְ שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת תַּשְׁמִישׁ דְּאָדָם הוּא וְלֹא תַּשְׁמִישׁ כְּלִי, לְפִיכָךְ הִיא מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה:


כבלים טמאין – there was a family in Jerusalem whose steps were large and their virgins would fall off and they placed a chain between each brooch that was on her legs and they made ankle-chains so that their steps would not be so large and that their virgins wouldn’t fall off; therefore, it is the sex chain of man and not the usage of a utensil , therefore it is susceptible to receive ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:4:8)


**וְאֵין יוֹצְאִין בָּהֶם.** דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא לַשַּׁלְשֶׁלֶת שֶׁהִיא שֶׁל זָהָב וּמְחַוְיָא, דְּכִי שָׁקְלָא לַשַּׁלְשֶׁלֶת לֹא מְגַלְּיָא שׁוֹקָהּ, שֶׁהָרֵי בִּירִית בִּמְקוֹמָהּ עוֹמֶדֶת:


ואין יוצאין בהם – lest the chain falls which is of gold and would show that when he takes the chain, the leg is not revealed, for it is a brooch that stands in its place.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:1)


**חוּטֵי שֵׂעָר.** שֵׂעָר תָּלוּשׁ שֶׁעָשְׂתָה אוֹתוֹ כְּמִין חוּטִין וְקוֹלַעַת בָּהֶן שַׂעֲרָהּ, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ קָשְׁרָה אוֹתָן עַל פַּדַּחְתָּהּ קֶשֶׁר מְהֻדָּק, יוֹצְאָה בָּהֶן, דְּלֹא דָּמִי לְחוּטֵי צֶמֶר וְחוּטֵי פִּשְׁתָּן דִּלְעֵיל, לְפִי שֶׁהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים בָּהֶם, וְאִי מִיתְרַמְיָא לַהּ טְבִילָה אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה שֶׁתַּתִּירֵם וְלֹא אָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ:


חוטי שער – detached hair that she made it like bands and she plaits/twists in them her hair or even ties them on her forehead a fastened tie, she goes out with them for they are not similar to bands of wool or bands of flax from above, because the water enters into them and if she happens to have a ritual immersion, she does not need to be afraid nor come to carry them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:2)


**בֵּין מִשֶּׁלָּהּ בֵּין מִשֶּׁל חֲבֶרְתָּהּ בֵּין מִשֶּׁל בְּהֵמָה.** צְרִיכִי, דְּאִי תָּנָא מִשֶּׁלָּהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּלֹא מָאִיס לַהּ, אֲבָל חֲבֶרְתָּהּ דְּמָאִיס לַהּ אֵימָא נֵחוּשׁ דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא לְהוּ וְאָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ, וְאִי תָּנָא שֶׁל חֲבֶרְתָהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּבַת מִינַּהּ הִיא לֹא מִינְכַּר וְלֹא מְחַיְּכֵי עֲלַהּ, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי לֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא וְאָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיֵיהּ, אֲבָל דִּבְהֵמָה דְּלָאו בַּת מִינַּהּ הִיא וּמִינְכַּר אֵימָא נֵחוּשׁ דִּלְמָא שָׁלְפָא לַהּ וְאָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיֵיהּ, צְרִיכָא:


בין משלה בין משל חברתה בין משל בהמה – it is necessary [to teach all three of them] for if it (i.e., the Mishnah) had only taught “of her own,” because it is not detestable, but of another’s [hair] which is detestable to her, I might say, that we should fear lest she loosen them and carry them; for if it (i.e., the Mishnah) had taught “of another’s,” because it is like her the same class or size, it would not be recognized and would not laugh at her, and because of this, there is nothing to suspect lest she loosen it and carry it, but “of an animal’s” which is not of the same class or size, and because it is recognized, I might suspect perhaps she took it off and carried it, hence, it is necessary [to teach all three].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:3)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵן תְּפוּרִין.** לַשְּׂבָכָה שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ, דְּתוּ לֹא שָׁלְפָא לְהוּ לְאַחֲוְוּיֵי:


בזמן שהן תפורין – to weave into her hair for furthermore, she will not loosen them to carry them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:4)


**לֶחָצֵר.** אַכָּבוּל וְאַפֵּאָה נָכְרִית קָאֵי, דַּאֲסַרוּהָ לְעֵיל לְמֵיפַק בֵּיהּ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְהַשְׁתָּא אַשְׁמְעִינַן דְּלֶחָצֵר מֻתָּר:


לחצר – it is referring to a hair-net and a wig, for she is forbidden to get up and go out with it to the public domain, and now we learn that to the courtyard, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:5)


**פֵּאָה נָכְרִית.** אִשָּׁה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ רֹב שֵׂעָר, לוֹקַחַת שְׂעַר נָשִׁים אֲחֵרוֹת וּמְשִׂימָה בְּרֹאשָׁהּ, וְנִרְאֶה כְּאִלּוּ הוּא שְׂעָרָהּ:


פאה נכרית – a woman who lacks a lot of hair takes the hair of other women and places it on her head, and she appears as if it is her hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:6)


**בְּמוֹךְ שֶׁבְּאָזְנֶיהָ.** שֶׁנּוֹתֶנֶת לִבְלֹעַ לֵחָה שֶׁל צוֹאַת הָאֹזֶן:


במוך שבאזנה – that she puts in to absorb her moisture/secretion of the pus of the ear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:7)


**שֶׁבְּסַנְדָּלָהּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיק הַסַּנְדָּל לְכַף רַגְלָהּ:


שבסנדלה – so that the shoe will not damage the heel of her foot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:8)


**לְנִדָּתָהּ.** בְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם, שֶׁיִּבָּלַע בּוֹ הַדָּם וְלֹא יְטַנֵּף בְּגָדֶיהָ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ קָשׁוּר, דְּאִי נָפַל לֹא אָתְיָא לַאֲתוֹיֵי מִפְּנֵי מְאִיסוּתוֹ. אֲבָל מוֹךְ שֶׁבְּאָזְנָהּ וְשֶׁבְּסַנְדָּלָהּ לֹא תֵּצֵא בּוֹ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן קָשׁוּר וּמְהֻדָּק:


לנדתה – in the same place that will absorb the blood and not soil her clothing, and even though it is not tied, if it falls, she will not come to carry because of his detestability, but the wool that is in her ear and/or that is in her shoe, she will not go out other than if it is tied and fastened.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:9)


**בְּפִלְפֵּל.** שֶׁנּוֹתֶנֶת בְּפִיהָ מִפְּנֵי רֵיחַ הַפֶּה:


בפלפל – that she places in her mouth because of the odor of the mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:10)


**וּבְגַרְגִּיר מֶלַח.** שֶׁנּוֹתֶנֶת לִרְפוּאַת חֹלִי הַשִּׁנַּיִם:


ובגרגיר מלח – that she places for healing the illness of the teeth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:11)


**שֵׁן תּוֹתֶבֶת.** נוֹשֶׁבֶת בַּלְּחָיַיִם בִּמְקוֹם הַשֵּׁן שֶׁנָּפַל לָהּ:


שן תותבת – it rests in her jaw in the place of the tooth that fell out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:12)


**וְשֵׁן שֶׁל זָהָב.** שֵׁן שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנָּה מַרְאִיתוֹ מֵחֲמַת עִפּוּשׁ, מְכַסָּה אוֹתוֹ בְּזָהָב:


ושן של זהב – a tooth that changed its appearance on account of decay, they cover it with gold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:13)


**רַבִּי מַתִּיר.** לָצֵאת בָּהּ, דְּלֹא שָׁלְפָה וּמְחַוְיָא, שֶׁלֹּא לְגַלּוֹת מוּמָהּ:


רבי מתיר – to go out with it for she will not loose it and carry it, in order to not reveal her defect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:5:14)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁהוּא מְשֻׁנֶּה מִשְּׁאָר שִׁנֶּיהָ, דִּלְמָא מְחַיְּכִי עֲלַהּ וְשָׁלְפָא לַהּ וְאָתְיָא לְאָתוֹיֵיהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אוסרים – because it is different from the rest of her teeth, lest she laugh about it and loosens it and comes to carry it. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:1)


**בְּסֶלַע.** מַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו צוּרָה:


בסלע – a coin that has on it an image.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:2)


**שֶׁעַל הַצִּינִית.** מַכָּה שֶׁתַּחַת פַּרְסוֹת הָרֶגֶל, וְקוֹשְׁרִים בָּהּ מַטְבֵּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו צוּרָה לִרְפוּאָה:


שעל הצינית – a wound that is under the toes of the foot (i.e., a bunion, callus) and they tie on it a coin that has an image for healing purposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:3)


**הַבָּנוֹת יוֹצְאוֹת בְּחוּטִין.** הַקְּטַנּוֹת מְנַקְּבוֹת אֶת אָזְנֵיהֶן וְאֵין עוֹשִׂין לָהֶן נְזָמִין עַד שֶׁיִּגְדְּלוּ, וְנוֹתְנִין חוּטִין אוֹ קִסְמִים בְּאָזְנֵיהֶן שֶׁלֹּא יִסָּתְמוּ אָזְנֵיהֶן:


הבנות יוצאות בחוטין – small girls pierce their ears and they don’t make for them earrings until they get older and place threads or chips in their ears so that their ears won’t close up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:4)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ בְקִסְמִים.** רְבוּתָא קָאָמַר, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלָאו תַּכְשִׁיט שֶׁל נוֹי הוּא, אוֹרְחַיְהוּ בְּהָכִי וְלָאו מַשְּׂאוֹי הוּא:


ואפילו בקסמים – an additional thing is mentioned, for even though it is not an adornment of beauty, it was their practice and it was not a burden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:5)


**עַרְבִיּוֹת.** בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבְּעַרְבִיָּא:


ערביות – Jewish girls in Arabia.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:6)


**יוֹצְאוֹת רְעוּלוֹת.** מְעֻטָּפוֹת בְּרֹאשָׁן וּפְנֵיהֶן מְכֻסּוֹת חוּץ מִן הָעֵינַיִם, כְּדֶרֶךְ הָעַרְבִיּוֹת:


רעולות – wrapped up on their heads and their faces are covered other than their eyes in the manner of Arab women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:7)


**מָדִיּוֹת.** בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבְּמָדַי:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:6:8)


**פְּרוּפוֹת.** שֶׁמִּתְעַטְּפוֹת בְּטַלִּית, וְתוֹלוֹת רְצוּעָה בִּשְׂפָתָהּ אַחַת כְּנֶגֶד צַוָּארָהּ, וּבִשְׂפָתָהּ הַשֵּׁנִית כּוֹרֶכֶת אֶבֶן אוֹ אֱגוֹז, וְקוֹשֶׁרֶת הָרְצוּעָה בְּכֶרֶךְ וְאֵין הַטַּלִּית נוֹפֶלֶת מֵעָלֶיהָ:


פרופות – that they wrap themselves in a shawl/cloak and hangs a strap on one edge opposite her neck and with the other edge, she attaches a stone or a nut and ties the strap in a bundle, so that the shawl does not fall from her.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:7:1)


**פּוֹרֶפֶת.** מְחַבֶּרֶת. תַּרְגּוּם קְרָסִים, פּוּרְפִין:


פורפת – attaches; it the Aramaic translation of hooks of cloaks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:7:2)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא תִפְרֹף לְכַתְּחִלָּה.** אַמַּטְבֵּעַ לְחוּדֵיהּ קָאֵי, שֶׁלֹּא תִּפְרֹף לְכַתְּחִלָּה עַל הַמַּטְבֵּעַ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ:


ובלבד שלא תפרוף לכתחילה – it refers alone to the coin, that she should not loop up ab initio on the coin [on the Sabbath] because it is forbidden to carry it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:1)


**הַקִּטֵּעַ.** שֶׁנִּקְטְעָה רַגְלוֹ:


הקטע – whose leg was mutilated/crippled


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:2)


**יוֹצֵא בְקַב שֶׁלּוֹ.** עוֹשִׂים לוֹ כְּמִין דְּפוּס רֶגֶל וְחוֹקֵק בּוֹ מְעַט לָשׂוּם רֹאשׁ שׁוֹקוֹ בְּתוֹכוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ נִסְמָךְ עָלָיו. וּמֻתָּר לָצֵאת בּוֹ, דְּמִנְעָל דִּידֵיהּ הֲוֵי. וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹסֵר, דְּלָאו תַּכְשִׁיט הוּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


יוצא בקב שלו – that make for him a kind of frame of a leg and hollow it out a bit to place the head of his foreleg in it, and it not supported on him, and it is permitted to go out [on the Sabbath] with it for it is his shoe but Rabbi Yosi prohibits it, for it is not an adornment, but the law follows Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:3)


**וְאִם יֶשׁ לוֹ בֵית קִבּוּל כְּתִיתִין.** שֶׁנֶּחְקַק בּוֹ כְּדֵי קִבּוּל שֶׁל כְּתִיתִין דַּקִּין וּמוֹכִין לְהָנִיחַ רֹאשׁ שׁוֹקוֹ עֲלֵיהֶן, מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאַת מַגָּע. אֲבָל אִם אֵין לוֹ אֶלָּא בֵּית קִבּוּל שׁוֹקוֹ בִּלְבַד בְּלֹא כְּתִיתִין, לָאו בֵּית קִבּוּל הוּא לְטֻמְאָה וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ כִּפְשׁוּטֵי כְּלֵי עֵץ, דְּדוּמְיָא דְּשַׂק בָּעִינַן, דְּקִבּוּל שֶׁלּוֹ עָשׂוּי לְטַלְטֵל עַל יָדוֹ מַה שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין בּוֹ, אֲבָל שׁוֹקוֹ אֵינוֹ מִטַּלְטֵל עַל גַּבֵּי הַכְּלִי:


ואם יש לו בית קבול כתיתין – that are hollowed out in it in order to be a receptacle for thin pads and wool to place at the head of his foreleg on it, they receive defilement through contact, but if it has only a receptacle for his foreleg alone without pads, it is not a receptacle for defilement and it is like a simple wooden utensil that is similar to a sackcloth that we require for his receptacle is designed to be carried by hand what we give him, but his foreleg doesn’t carry the utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:4)


**סָמוֹכוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ.** יֵשׁ קִטֵּעַ שְׁתֵּי רַגְלָיו וְהוֹלֵךְ עַל שׁוֹקָיו וְעַל אַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו, וְעוֹשֶׂה סָמוֹכוֹת שֶׁל עוֹר בְּשׁוֹקָיו:


סמוכות שלו – there is a cripple of his two legs who walks on his forelegs and on his leg from under the hip-bone to the ankle/the knee and its surrounding parts and makes leather cushions tied to the cripple’s forelegs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:5)


**טְמֵאִים מִדְרָס.** אִם זָב הוּא, דְּהָא לִסְמִיכוּת גּוּפוֹ עֲבִידֵי וּמִדְרָס הֵן וְנַעֲשׂוּ אַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


טמאים מדרס – if he has a flux which [the pads] are made as supports for his body and they become susceptible to uncleanness caused by treading contact, and they are made the original causes of Levitical uncleanness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:6)


**וְיוֹצְאִין בָּהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת.** דְּתַכְשִׁיט דִּידֵיהּ הֵן:


ויוצאין בהם בבת – for they are his adornment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:7)


**וְנִכְנָסִין בָּהֶן לָעֲזָרָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּתְנַן לֹא יִכָּנֵס לְהַר הַבַּיִת בְּמִנְעָלוֹ, הָנֵי לָאו מִנְעָל נִינְהוּ, דְּלָאו בְּרֹאשׁ רַגְלָיו הֵן:


ונכנסין בהן לעזרה – for even though the Mishnah )Tractate Berakhot, Chapter 9, Mishnah 5) that he should not enter the Temple mount with his shoes, these are not shoes because they are not at the head of his feet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:8)


**כִּסֵּא.** יֵשׁ קִטֵּעַ שֶׁיָּבְשׁוּ וְנִכְוְצוּ גִּידֵי שׁוֹקָיו וְאַף עַל אַרְכֻּבּוֹתָיו אֵינוֹ יָכֹל לֵילֵךְ, וְעוֹשֶׂה כְּמִין כִּסֵּא נָמוּךְ וְיוֹשֵׁב עָלָיו, וּכְשֶׁהוּא מְהַלֵּךְ נִסְמָךְ עַל יָדָיו בְּסַפְסָלִים קְטַנִּים וְעוֹקֵר גּוּפוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ וְנִדְחָף לְפָנָיו וְחוֹזֵר וְנָח עַל אֲחוֹרָיו, וְהַכִּסֵּא קָשׁוּר לַאֲחוֹרָיו:


כסא – there is a cripple whose sinews of his foreleg have dried out and shrunk and even on his knees, he is incapable of walking and makes a kind of low stool and he sits upon it, and when he walks, he supported on his hands by small benches and uproots his body from the earth and is thrust before him and he returns and it is pleasant on his back and the stool is tied to his back.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:9)


**סָמוֹכוֹת.** שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ קִטֵּעַ, עוֹשֶׂה לוֹ סָמוֹכוֹת שֶׁל עוֹר אוֹ שֶׁל עֵץ לְרָאשֵׁי שׁוֹקָיו אוֹ רַגְלָיו הַתְּלוּיִין, וּכְשֶׁהוּא נִשְׁעָן עַל יָדָיו וְהוּא עוֹקֵר עַצְמוֹ נִשְׁעָן גַּם עַל רַגְלָיו קְצָת:


סמוכות – of the same limb, they make for him cushions of leather or of wood for the top of his forelegs or his feet that are hanging and when he leans on his hands and uproots himself, he leans also on his legs a little bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:10)


**וְאֵין יוֹצְאִין בָּהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת.** דְּאַיְדֵי דְּתָלוּ וְלֹא מֵאֲרְגֵי, זִמְנִין דְּמִשְׁתַּלְפֵי וְאָתֵי לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לוֹ כָּל כָּךְ וַהֲווּ מַשְּׂאוֹי:


ואין יוצאין בהם בשבת – for these that suspected and were not weaved/place him on the ground, sometimes they loosen and comes to carry them. And thee are those who say, they are not needed for him so much, and they are a burden [being carried].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:11)


**וְאֵין נִכְנָסִין בָּהֶן לָעֲזָרָה.** דְּמִנְעָל נִינְהוּ:


ואין נכנסין בהן לעזרה – for they are shoes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:12)


**אַנְקַטְמִין.** כְּמִין פַּרְצוּף אָדָם מְשֻׁנֶּה שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין עַל הַפָּנִים לְהַבְעִית בּוֹ הַתִּינוֹקוֹת. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, חֲמוֹר שֶׁהַלֵּיצָנִים עוֹשִׂין וְנוֹשְׂאִין אוֹתוֹ עַל כִּתְפֵיהֶן:


אנקטמין – a kind of visage of a strange man that we place on the face to frighten young children. Another explanation: a donkey that clowns make and carry on their shoulders.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:8:13)


**טְהוֹרִים.** מִלְּקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, דְּלֹא כְּלִי תַּשְׁמִישׁ וְלֹא תַּכְשִׁיט הוּא:


טהורים – from receiving defilement for they are not utensils used nor are they adornments.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:9:1)


**בִּקְשָׁרִים.** בֵּן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ גַּעְגּוּעִים עַל אָבִיו, נוֹטֵל הָאָב רְצוּעָה שֶׁל מִנְעַל יָמִין וְקוֹשְׁרָהּ לוֹ לַבֵּן בִּשְׂמֹאלוֹ, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁאוֹתוֹ קֶשֶׁר עָלָיו סָרִים הַגַּעְגּוּעִים מֵעָלָיו, בִּסְגֻלָּה. וּלְהָכִי תָּנֵי הַבָּנִים, שֶׁאֵין לַבָּנוֹת גַּעְגּוּעִים עַל אֲבִיהֶן כְּמוֹ הַבָּנִים:


בקשרים – a son who has longings for his father, the father takes a strap of the right shoe and ties it to the child with his left [arm] and all the while that this tie is upon him, the longings become feeble from him as an heirloom/treasure and as such it is taught that the sons, unlike the daughters have longings for their fathers like children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:9:2)


**בְּזוֹגִין.** כְּמִין פַּעֲמוֹנִים קְטַנִּים. תַּרְגּוּם פַּעֲמוֹן זָהָב, זַגָּא דְּדַהֲבָא:


בזוגין – like small bells. The Aramaic translation of golden bell is a golden bell.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:1)


**הַחַרְגּוֹל.** מִין חָגָב, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) אֶת הַחַרְגּוֹל לְמִינֵהוּ, וְתוֹלִין אוֹתוֹ בָּאֹזֶן וּמְרַפְּאִים בּוֹ כְּאֵב הָאֹזֶן:


החרגול – a kind of locust, as it is written (Leviticus 11:22): “[of these you may eat the following: locusts of every variety; all varieties of bald locust;] crickets of every variety; [and all varieties of grasshopper],” and we suspend it in the ear and it heals his earache.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:2)


**וּבְשֵׁן שֶׁל שׁוּעָל.** דְּעָבְדֵי לְשֵׁינְתָּא. לְמַאן דְּנָיֵים לְהָקִיצוֹ, תּוֹלִין עָלָיו שֵׁן שֶׁל שׁוּעָל חַי. וּלְמַאן דְּלֹא נָיֵים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּישַׁן, תּוֹלִין עָלָיו שֵׁן שֶׁל שׁוּעָל מֵת:


ובשן שועל – that we use for sleep for one who cannot sleep in order that he sleeps, we suspend upon him the tooth of a dead fox.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:3)


**וּבְמַסְמֵר הַצָּלוּב.** מַסְמֵר שֶׁבְּעֵץ הַתָּלוּי, אִם יְנִיחוּהוּ עַל נֶפַח שֶׁבַּמַּכָּה יָסִיר הַנֶּפַח. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין אוֹתוֹ בְּצַוָּאר מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ קַדַּחַת שְׁלִישִׁית וִירַפְּאֶנּוּ:


ובמסמר הצלוב – a nail that is in a tree that is suspended, if we place it on the swelling that is on the wound, it will remove the swelling. But Maimonides explained that we place it on the neck – he who has a third inflammation and it will cure him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 6:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/6:10:4)


**וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר אַף בְּחֹל אָסוּר.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר, דְּקַיְמָא לָן כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם רְפוּאָה אֵין בּוֹ מִשּׁוּם דַּרְכֵי הָאֱמוֹרִי, וְלֹא קָרִינַן בְּהוּ וּבְחֻקּוֹתֵיהֶם לֹא תֵּלֵכוּ:


ורבי מאיר אומר אף בחול אסור – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir, for we hold that every thing that is for medicinal purposes, it lacks from the ways of the Amorites, and we don’t call it (Leviticus 18:3): “nor shall you follow their laws.”



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:1)


**כְּלָל גָּדוֹל.** הַשּׁוֹכֵחַ עִקַּר שַׁבָּת. כַּסָּבוּר אֵין שַׁבָּת בַּתּוֹרָה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמֵעִקָּרָא שָׁמַע וְעַכְשָׁיו שְׁכָחוֹ:


כלל גדול. השוכח עיקר שבת– since he thought that Shabbat is not in the Torah, and even though that from the beginning, he had heard [about Shabbat] but now had forgotten it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:2)


**אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא חַטָּאת אֶחָת.** עַל כָּל הַשַּׁבָּתוֹת שֶׁחִלֵּל, דְּכֻלָּהּ שְׁגָגָה חֲדָא הִיא דִּכְתִיב (שמות לא) אֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתַי תִּשְׁמֹרוּ, וּמַשְׁמַע שְׁמִירָה אַחַת לְשַׁבָּתוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


אינו חייב אלא חטאת – on all the Sabbaths that he had desecrated for all of it is one [inadvertent] error as it is written (Exodus 31:13): “you must keep my Sabbaths,” and which implies one observance for any Sabbaths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:3)


**הַיּוֹדֵעַ עִקַּר שַׁבָּת.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ שַׁבָּת בַּתּוֹרָה וְנֶאֶסְרוּ בּוֹ מְלָאכוֹת, וְעָשָׂה מְלָאכוֹת הַרְבֵּה בְּשַׁבָּתוֹת הַרְבֵּה עַל יְדֵי שִׁגְגַת שַׁבָּת, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁשַּׁבָּת הַיּוֹם, חַיָּב עַל כָּל שַׁבָּת וְשַׁבָּת חַטָּאת אַחַת, וְעַל זֶה נֶאֱמַר (שם) וְשָׁמְרוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, דְּמַשְׁמַע שְׁמִירָה לְכָל שַׁבָּת וְשַׁבָּת, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁחַיָּב חַטָּאת עַל כָּל שַׁבָּת וְשַׁבָּת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נוֹדַע לוֹ בֵּינְתַיִם וְהֶעְלֵם אֶחָד הוּא, אָמְרִינַן יָמִים שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם הַוְיָן יְדִיעָה, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא שָׁמַע בֵּינְתַיִם שֶׁאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם שַׁבָּת הָיָה, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא נִזְכַּר בַּמְּלָאכוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה בּוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ כָּל שַׁבָּת וְשַׁבָּת שְׁגָגָה אַחַת הִיא:


היודע עיקר שבת – that is Shabbat in the Torah and creative work was forbidden on it, but he did many forms of creative work on many Sabbaths via the inadvertent error on Shabbat for he does not know that Shabbat is today, he is liable for each and every Shabbat one sin, and on this, it is stated (Exodus 31:16): “The Israelite people shall keep the Sabbath” that implies that observance of each and every Shabbat, that is to say that he is liable for a sin-offering on each and every Sabbath and even though that it was not known to him in the meantime and it is one act of forgetfulness, we say that the days that are in-between that is a knowledge that is impossible that he hadn’t heard [about Shabbat] in the meanwhile that this day was Shabbat but he was not reminded of the creative works that he did on it. Therefore, each and every Shabbat is one error.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:1:4)


**הַיּוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהוּא שַׁבָּת וְעָשָׂה מְלָאכוֹת הַרְבֵּה.** שֶׁלֹּא יָדַע שֶׁמְּלָאכוֹת הַלָּלוּ אֲסוּרוֹת, וַעֲשָׂאָן כַּמָּה פְּעָמִים בְּכַמָּה שַׁבָּתוֹת, חַיָּב עַל כָּל אַב מְלָאכָה חַטָּאת אַחַת, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָזַר וּכְפָלָן בְּכַמָּה שַׁבָּתוֹת, כָּל אָב מֵהֶן חֲדָא שְׁגָגָה הִיא, דְּהָא לֹא נוֹדַע לוֹ בֵּינְתַיִם. וְהָכָא לֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר יָמִים שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם הַוְיָן יְדִיעָה לְחַלֵּק, דְּמִשּׁוּם יָמִים שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם אֵין לוֹ לָדַעַת אֵיזוֹ מְלָאכָה אֲסוּרָה וְאֵיזוֹ מְלָאכָה מֻתֶּרֶת, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יָשַׁב וְעָסַק לִפְנֵי חֲכָמִים בְּהִלְכוֹת שַׁבָּת. וְהוּא הַדִּין שֶׁחַיָּב עַל שְׁתֵּי תּוֹלָדוֹת שֶׁל שְׁתֵּי אָבוֹת חֲלוּקוֹת עַל כָּל אַחַת חַטָּאת אַחַת, אֲבָל אִי עָבֵיד אָב וְתוֹלָדָה דִּידֵיהּ, אוֹ שְׁתֵּי תּוֹלָדוֹת שֶׁל אָב אֶחָד, לֹא מִחַיַּב אֶלָּא חֲדָא, כִּדְקָתָנֵי סֵיפָא הָעוֹשֶׂה מְלָאכוֹת הַרְבֵּה מֵעֵין מְלָאכָה אַחַת אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא אַחַת, כְּגוֹן שְׁתֵּי תּוֹלָדוֹת שֶׁל אָב אֶחָד, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוֵי כְּעוֹשֶׂה וְחוֹזֵר וְעוֹשֶׂה בְּהֶעְלֵם אַחַת, וְאֵין חִלּוּק חַטָּאוֹת בְּהֶעְלֵם אַחַת אֶלָּא בְּגוּפֵי עֲבֵרָה שֶׁאֵינָן דּוֹמִים, אוֹ בְּחִלּוּק שַׁבָּתוֹת לְעִנְיַן שִׁגְגַת שַׁבָּת:


היודע שהוא שבת ועשה מלאכות הרבה – that he did not know that the forms of creative work were forbidden and he performed them several times on several Sabbaths, he is liable for each of the chief labors that are forbidden on the Sabbath one sin-offering and even though he went back and repeated them (i.e., the forbidden labors) on several Sabbaths, each principal form of labor is one error and he was not informed about it in the interim and here one cannot say that the days in-between are considered knowledge [of them] to differentiate, for the days in-between he would not know which chief labor is forbidden and which is permitted other than if he sat and engaged before Sages in the laws of Shabbat. And the same law applies that he is liable on two derivatives of two primary forbidden forms of labor whereby each one is divided into one sin-offering apiece, but if he had done a principal form of forbidden labor and its derivative or two derivatives of one principal forbidden labor, he is not liable other than for one [sin-offering] as it is taught at the end of the Mishnah: A person who performs many forbidden actions of one prohibited labor [on the Sabbath], he is not liable for other than for one [sin-offering] such as two derivatives of one primary forbidden labor because it is like a person who performed an action and repeated it in one act of forgetfulness and there is no separation/division of sin-offerings with one act of forgetfulness but other than the actual transgression which is not similar or distinguished on [many] Sabbaths for the matter of an error on Shabbat.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵעַ וְהַחוֹרֵשׁ.** הָא דְּלֹא תָּנָא הַחוֹרֵשׁ בְּרֵישָׁא וַהֲדַר הַזּוֹרֵעַ כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל הָאָרֶץ, לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן שֶׁאִם הָיָה קַרְקַע קָשָׁה וַחֲרָשׁוֹ וּזְרָעוֹ וְאַחַר כָּךְ חָזַר וַחֲרָשׁוֹ, מִחַיַּב אַחֲרִישָׁה שְׁנִיָּה מִשּׁוּם חוֹרֵשׁ:


הזורע החורש - the fact that “ploughing” is not taught at the beginning and the Mishnah and afterwards “sowing” like the way of the whole world is to inform us that if the land was hard and they ploughed and then sewed and then afterwards ploughed, he is liable for the second ploughing because of “ploughing.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:2)


**הַקּוֹצֵר.** בַּזְּרָעִים, וְהַמְּלַקֵּט בָּאִילָנוֹת, חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם קוֹצֵר:


הקוצר – seeds and one gleans/harvests the trees, he is liable because of “reaping.” (Tractate Shabbat 73 b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:3)


**הַמְעַמֵּר.** אוֹסֵף זְרָעִים תְּלוּשִׁים וְצוֹבְרָם אֶל מָקוֹם אֶחָד:


המעמר – collecting detached seeds and collects them in one place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:4)


**הַזּוֹרֶה.** בָּרַחַת לָרוּחַ:


הזורה – passing through to the wind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:5)


**הַבּוֹרֵר.** פְּסֹלֶת, בְּיָדָיו אוֹ בִּכְבָרָה:


הבורר – the refuse in his hand or in the basket used as a sieve.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:6)


**וְהַמְרַקֵּד.** בַּנָּפָה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהָנֵי כֻּלְּהוּ חֲדָא נִינְהוּ דִּלְהַפְרִישׁ פְּסֹלֶת מִתּוֹךְ אֹכֶל נַעֲשׂוּ שְׁלָשְׁתָּן, מִשּׁוּם דִּשְׁלָשְׁתָּן הָווּ בַּמִּשְׁכָּן אַף עַל גַּב דְּדָמוּ לַהֲדָדִי חָשֵׁיב לְהוּ כָּל חֲדָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשַׁהּ. אִי נַמִּי לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָן בְּבַת אַחַת אֶלָּא זֶה אַחַר זֶה:


והמרקד – in a winnow/sieve, and even though that they are similar to each other, each one is considered separately; alternatively, they are not at the same time, but rather one after another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:7)


**הָאוֹפֶה.** לֹא הַוְיָא בַּמִּשְׁכָּן, דְּאֵין אֲפִיָּה אֶלָּא בַּפַּת, וּפַת לֹא שַׁיְכָא בִּמְלֶאכֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן, אֶלָּא תַּנָּא סִדּוּרָא דְּפַת נָקַט, וּמִיהוּ מְבַשֵּׁל שֶׁהוּא מֵעֵין מְלֶאכֶת אוֹפֶה הֲוָה בַּמִּשְׁכָּן, בַּסַּמְּמָנִין שֶׁל צֶבַע תְּכֵלֶת וְאַרְגָּמָן וְתוֹלַעַת שָׁנִי. וְהַמֵּגִיס בִּקְדֵרָה, וְהַנּוֹתֵן כִּסּוּי עַל גַּבֵּי קְדֵרָה הָעוֹמֶדֶת עַל הָאֵשׁ, חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם מְבַשֵּׁל, וְכָל הָנָךְ קַמָּאִי דְּחָשֵׁיב בְּמַתְנִיתִין הַזּוֹרֵעַ הַקּוֹצֵר הַדָּשׁ וְכוּ', כֻּלְּהוּ הָווּ בַּסַּמְּמָנִין בַּצֶּבַע שֶׁל מְלֶאכֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן:


והאופה – there was no [baking] in the Tabernacle for there is no baking other than with bread and bread does not belong in the labors of the Tabernacle but rather, it (i.e., the Mishnah) taught that the arrangement of bread was taken; however, cooking which is considered like the [forbidden] labor of baking was in the Tabernacle with the spices of the color of bluish, purple-dye and scarlet-dyed. If he stirs the pot and places a covering on top of the pot that is standing on the fire, he is liable because of [the prohibition of] cooking and all of these above are considered in our Mishnah: “One who sews, ploughs, threshes, etc., all of these are with spices of color in the building of the Tabernacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:8)


**הַגּוֹזֵז צֶמֶר.** וְכָל שְׁאָר מַלְאֲכוֹתֶיהָ שַׁיְּכֵי בְּצֶמֶר תְּכֵלֶת שֶׁל מְלֶאכֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן:


הגוזז צמר – and all the rest of these labors belong with wool of blue of the labors of the Tabernacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:9)


**הַמְּלַבְּנוֹ.** מְכַבְּסוֹ בַּנָּהָר:


המלבנו – laundering it in the river.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:10)


**הַמְּנַפְּצוֹ.** חוֹבְטוֹ בְּשֵׁבֶט, אִי נַמִּי סוֹרְקוֹ בְּמַסְרֵק:


המנפצו – beat it with a rod; alternatively, comb it with a comb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:11)


**הַמֵּסֵךְ.** אורדי"ר בְּלַעַ"ז:


המיסך - “ordir” in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:12)


**בָּתֵּי נִירִין.** שֶׁנּוֹתֵן שְׁנֵי חוּטִין בְּתוֹךְ הַבֵּית נִיר:


בתי נירין - takes two threads in the midst of the portion of the web produed by pasing the spool with the woof across the warp.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:13)


**הַפּוֹצֵעַ.** מֵסִיר חוּטֵי הָעֵרֶב מֵעַל הַשְּׁתִי אוֹ הַשְּׁתִי מֵעַל הָעֵרֶב, לְצֹרֶךְ אֲרִיגָה:


הפוצע – he removes the threads of the woof that are above the warp, or the warp that are above the woof for the purpose of weaving.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:14)


**וְהַקּוֹשֵׁר וְהַמַּתִּיר.** שֶׁכֵּן צָדֵי חִלָּזוֹן שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ עוֹשִׂין הַתְּכֵלֶת קוֹשְׁרִין וּמַתִּירִין, שֶׁפְּעָמִים צָרִיךְ לִטֹּל חוּטִין מֵרֶשֶׁת זוֹ לְהוֹסִיף עַל זוֹ, וּמַתִּיר מִכָּאן וְקוֹשֵׁר כָּאן:


והקושר והמתיר – for such are the sides of the snail for from it they produce the bluish-dye, they tie and untie for sometimes it is necessary to take threads from this net to add on to the other and untie from here and tie there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:15)


**וְהַתּוֹפֵר וְקוֹרֵעַ.** נַמִּי הֲוֵי בַּיְרִיעוֹת, שֶׁכֵּן יְרִיעָה שֶׁאֲכָלָהּ עָשׁ וְנִקְּבָה בּוֹ נֶקֶב קָטָן וְעָגֹל, צָרִיךְ לִקְרֹעַ לְמַטָּה וּלְמַעְלָה אֶת הַנֶּקֶב, שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא הַתְּפִירָה עֲשׂוּיָה קְמָטִין קְמָטִין:


והתופר – and tearing also that are in the curtain for a curtain that a moth ate and made a small and round hole must tear downwards and upwards so that the sewing of the hole does not have wrinkles/folds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:16)


**וְהַתּוֹפֵר שְׁתֵּי תְפִירוֹת.** וְהוּא שֶׁקְּשָׁרָן, דְּאִי לֹא קְשָׁרָן לֹא קַיְּמֵי. וּמִחַיַּב תַּרְתֵּי, מִשּׁוּם קוֹשֵׁר וּמִשּׁוּם תּוֹפֵר:


והתופר שתי תפירות – that he tied them up, for had he not tied them, they would not exist and he would be liable for [violating] two [principal forms of labor] because of tying and because of sewing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:17)


**הַצָּד צְבִי.** וְכָל מְלֶאכֶת עוֹרוֹ נוֹהֶגֶת בַּתְּחָשִׁים בְּעוֹרוֹתֵיהֶן:


הצד צבי – and all of the labors concerning its hides apply which were used to cover the Tabernacle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:18)


**הַמּוֹלְחוֹ וְהַמְּעַבְּדוֹ.** בַּגְּמָרָא מַקְשֶׁה דְּהַיְנוּ מוֹלֵחַ הַיְנוּ מְעַבֵּד, אֶלָּא אַפֵּיק חַד מִנַּיְהוּ וְעַיֵּל שִׂרְטוּט בִּמְקוֹמוֹ, שֶׁמְּשַׂרְטֵט מֵאֲבוֹת מְלָאכוֹת הוּא:


המולחו והמעבדו – The Gemara (Tractate Shabbat 75b) that salting the skin is the same as curing the skin, but they exclude one of them and place marking lines in its place, for placing marking lines is one of the primary prohibited forms of labor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:19)


**הַמְּמַחְקוֹ.** מְגָרֵד שְׂעָרוֹ:


הממחקו – to scrape his hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:20)


**מְחַתְּכוֹ.** מַקְצִיעוֹ וּמְחַתְּכוֹ לִרְצוּעוֹת וְסַנְדָּלִים:


מחתכו – to trim and cut straps and sandals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:21)


**וְכוֹתֵב וּמוֹחֵק.** הָווּ בַּמִּשְׁכָּן, שֶׁכֵּן רוֹשְׁמִים בַּקְּרָשִׁים לֵידַע אֵיזֶה בֶּן זוּגוֹ, וְכוֹתֵב אוֹת בָּזֶה וְאוֹת בָּזֶה, וּפְעָמִים שֶׁטָּעָה וּמוֹחֵק:


וכותב ומוחק – they were in the Tabernacle that they would mark on boards to know which would be its pair and he would write a letter on this one and a letter one that one and sometimes he would err and erase it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:22)


**מְכַבֶּה וּמַבְעִיר.** בָּאֵשׁ שֶׁתַּחַת דּוּד הַסַּמְּמָנִין:


מכבה ומבעיר – with fire that was under the symbols of the scholars.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:2:23)


**מַכֶּה בַפַּטִּישׁ.** הוּא גְּמַר מְלָאכָה, שֶׁכֵּן אֻמָּן מַכֶּה בַּסַּדָּן בִּגְמַר מְלָאכָה, וְאֵין מַכֶּה בַּפַּטִּישׁ חַיָּב אֶלָּא בִּגְמַר מְלָאכָה. וּמִנְיָנָא דְּרֵישָׁא דְּמָנָה אֲבוֹת מְלָאכוֹת אַרְבָּעִים חָסֵר אַחַת, אַף עַל גַּב דַּהֲדַר חָשֵׁיב לְהוּ חֲדָא חֲדָא, אֲתָא לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דַּאֲפִלּוּ עָבֵיד אֱינַשׁ כָּל מְלָאכוֹת שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם בְּשַׁבָּת אַחַת, בְּהֶעְלֵם אַחַת, אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיִּתְחַיֵּב יוֹתֵר מֵאַרְבָּעִים חַטָּאוֹת, דְּכֻלְּהוּ שְׁאָר מְלָאכוֹת תּוֹלָדוֹת לְהָנָךְ אָבוֹת, וְאִשְׁתַּכַּח דְּעָבֵיד אָב וְתוֹלָדָה דִּילֵיהּ וְלֹא מִחַיַּב אֶלָּא חֲדָא:


מכה בפטיש – the completion of work for so the artisan strikes the block with the completion of the work and the striking with a hammer is not liable until the end of the work [has been reached]. But the number of the first teacher of the Mishnah who counts primary forms of work as forty minus one, even though he returns and considers each one individually comes to teach us that even if a person did all the work in the world on one Shabbat in one act of forgetfulness, it is impossible that he would be liable for more than forty sin-offerings for all of them the rest of the labors are derivatives to these primary forms of work and it is found that he performs a primary form of work and its derivative and is not liable for anything other than one.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:1)


**כָּל הַכָּשֵׁר לְהַצְנִיעַ.** שֶׁהוּא דָּבָר הֶעָשׂוּי לְצֹרֶךְ הָאָדָם:


כל הכשר להצניע – which is something that is made for usage by a human being.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:2)


**וּמַצְנִיעִין כָּמוֹהוּ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ שִׁעוּר הָרָאוּי לְהַצְנִיעוֹ:


ומצניעו כמוהו – that has the appropriate measurements to be stored/hidden away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:3:3)


**אֵין חַיָּב אֶלָּא הַמַּצְנִיעוֹ.** אִם נַעֲשָׂה חָבִיב עַל אָדָם אֶחָד וְהִצְנִיעוֹ, חַיָּב עַל הוֹצָאָתוֹ אִם הוֹצִיאוֹ, אֲבָל אָדָם אַחֵר אֵינוֹ חַיָּב עָלָיו, דִּלְגַבֵּיהּ לָאו מְלָאכָה הִיא:


אין חייב אלא המצניעו -if something was done that was beloved by one person and he stored it away, he is liable for his removal of it if he took it out but another individual is not liable on it for concerning him it is not a [forbidden] form of work.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:1)


**עָצָה.** תֶּבֶן שֶׁל מִינֵי קִטְנִיּוֹת:


עצה – straw of kinds of pulse/peas (Tractate Shabbat 76a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:2)


**כִּמְלֹא פִי גָמָל.** שִׁעוּרוֹ גָּדוֹל מִמְּלֹא פִי פָרָה, וְכִמְלֹא פִי פָרָה לֹא מִחַיַּב בְּעָצָה, דְּהָא לֹא חַזְיָא לְפָרָה:


כמלוא פי גמל – its measurement is larger than that of a cow’s mouthful and the mouthful of a cow is not obligated for straw, for it is not appropriate for a cow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:3)


**עָמִיר.** קַשִּׁין שֶׁל שִׁבָּלִין:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:4)


**פִּי טָלֶה.** נָפִישׁ מִפִּי גְּדִי, הִלְכָּךְ עָמִיר דְּלֹא חָזֵי לִגְדִי לֹא מִחַיַּב כִּמְלֹא פִּי גְּדִי עַד דְּאִיכָּא מְלֹא פִּי טָלֶה, אֲבָל עֲשָׂבִים הוֹאִיל וְחָזֵי לִגְדִי וּלְטָלֶה מִחַיַּב אֲפִלּוּ כִּמְלֹא פִי גְּדִי:


פי טלה – it is larger than the mouth of a kid (i.e., goat), therefore, ears of corn which are not appropriate for a kid, one is not liable for the mouthful of a kid until there is enough for the mouthful of a lamb, but grasses since they are appropriate for a kid and a lamb one is liable even for the mouthful of a kid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:5)


**לַחִין.** הָרְאוּיִין לָאָדָם:


לחין – that are appropriate for humans


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:6)


**כִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת.** דְּזֶה שִׁעוּר לְכָל מַאֲכַל אָדָם בְּשַׁבָּת, אֲבָל כִּמְלֹא פִי גְּדִי לֹא, דְּלַחִים לֹא חָזוּ לִגְדִי:


like dried figs, for this is the measure for all human food on Shabbat but not for the mouthful of a kid for moist foods are not appropriate for kids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:7)


**וּמִצְטָרְפִין.** כָּל אָכְלֵי אָדָם זֶה עִם זֶה:


מצטרפין – all of the foods of a human – these with those.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:8)


**חוּץ מִקְּלִפֵּיהֶן.** שֶׁאֵינָן אֹכֶל וְאֵין מַשְׁלִימִין הַשִּׁעוּר:


חוץ מקליפיהן – which are not [considered] food and do not complete the measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:9)


**וְעֻקְצֵיהֶן.** זְנַב הַפְּרִי דְּהוּא עֵץ בְּעָלְמָא:


ועוקציהן – the tail of the fruit which is a mere tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:10)


**וְסֻבָּן.** קְלִפַּת הַחִטִּים הַנּוֹשֶׁרֶת מֵחֲמַת הַכְּתִישָׁה:


וסובן – husk of wheat that falls off because of crushing/pounding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:11)


**וּמֻרְסָנָן.** הַנִּשְׁאָר בַּנָּפָה. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ אִיפְּכָא, דְּמֻרְסָן הוּא עָבֶה וְגָרוּעַ מִסֻּבִּין:


ומורסנן – that remains in the winnow but Maimonides explains the opposite that their fine flour is thicker and worse than bran flour.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:12)


**חוּץ מִקְּלִפֵּי עֲדָשִׁים.** [שֶׁמִּצְטָרְפִין]:


חוץ מקליפי עדשים – [that combine/are included together].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 7:4:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/7:4:13)


**שֶׁהֵן מִתְבַּשְּׁלוֹת עִמָּהֶן.** לַאֲפוֹקֵי הַקְּלִפּוֹת הַחִיצוֹנוֹת שֶׁהֵן נוֹשְׁרוֹת כְּשֶׁעוֹשֶׂה מֵהֶם גֹּרֶן. וּקְלִפֵּי הַפּוֹלִין בִּזְמַן שֶׁהֵן לַחִים וּמִתְבַּשְׁלִין עִם קְלִפָּתָן, לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מִצְטָרְפִין עִם הָאֳכָלִין לְשִׁעוּר גְּרוֹגֶרֶת, אֲבָל יְבֵשִׁים לֹא, שֶׁאֵינָן נֶאֱכָלִין בִּקְלִפָּתָן לְפִי שֶׁהֵן נִרְאִין כִּזְבוּבִין בַּקְּעָרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שהן מתבשלות עמהן – to exclude the outer husks which fall off when it is harvested and the husks of the beans at the time that they are moist and cooked with their husks; for Rabbi Yehuda they are included together with foods for the measurement of dry figs but not dried foods which are not consumed with their husks because they appear like flies in a bowl, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:1)


**הַמּוֹצִיא יַיִן כְּדֵי מְזִיגַת הַכּוֹס.** שֶׁל בִּרְכַּת הַמָּזוֹן, שֶׁהוּא רֹבַע רְבִיעִית יַיִן חַי, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּמְזְגֶנּוּ לְחֶשְׁבּוֹן עַל חַד שֶׁל יַיִן תְּלָתָא מִמַּיִם וְיַעֲמֹד עַל רְבִיעִית הַלֹּג שֶׁהוּא שִׁעוּר כּוֹס שֶׁל בְּרָכָה:


המוציא [יין] כדי מזיגת כוס – [of wine] for the Blessing after the Meal which is one-fourth part of a רביעית of pure wine in order that he would mix it to a percentage of one [part] of wine and three [parts] of water and he will become consistent with a quarter of a “log” (which equals the volume of six eggs) which is the measurement for a cup [of wine] for a blessing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:2)


**כְּדֵי גְמִיעָה.** מַה שֶּׁאָדָם בּוֹלֵעַ בְּבַת אַחַת, וַחֲלֵב בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה דְּלֹא חָזֵי לִשְׁתִיָּה שִׁעוּרוֹ כְּדֵי לִכְחֹל עַיִן אַחַת:


כדי גמיעה – what a person absorbs at one time but the milk of a ritually impure animal that is not proper for drinking, its measure is to paint the eyelids [for medical or for cosmetic purposes] of one eye (see Talmud Shabbat 78b and the end of Mishnah 3 of our chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:3)


**עַל הַכָּתִית.** מַכָּה שֶׁעַל גַּבֵּי הַסּוּסִים וְהַחֲמוֹרִים מֵחֲמַת הַמַּשְּׂאוֹי. וּמָצָאתִי כָּתוּב שֶׁשְּׁחִין הַיּוֹצֵא בְּעוֹר הַבָּשָׂר, כְּשֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל הוּא עוֹשֶׂה רֹאשׁ לְמַעְלָה וְקָרוּי פִּי כָּתִית. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַדְּבַשׁ עִקָּרוֹ לָאֲכִילָה, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרְפוּאָתוֹ מְצוּיָה, וְהוּא מַשֶּׁהוּ, אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר שִׁעוּרָא זוּטְרָא לְחֻמְרָא:


על הכתית – a wound on the back of horses and donkeys on account of the burden; and I found it written that the inflammation that comes out of the hide of the flesh that when it reaches a boiling point it makes a head about and it is called the “mouth” of a scab, and even though the honey is principally for eating, because its healing [capacity] is found, and it is “something,” we go according to the smallest measure possible for a strict [ruling].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:4)


**אֵבֶר קָטָן.** שֶׁל תִּינוֹק בֶּן יוֹמוֹ, וְהוּא אֶצְבַּע קְטַנָּה שֶׁבָּרֶגֶל:


אבר קטן – [smallest limb] of a one-day old baby which is the little toe of the foot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:5)


**לָשׁוּף.** לְשַׁפְשֵׁף וְלִמְחוֹת בָּהֶן:


לשוף – to rub and dissolve them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:6)


**קִילוֹר.** שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין עַל הָעַיִן:


קילור – that we put on the eye.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:7)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַמַּשְׁקִין.** שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂין מֵהֶם רְפוּאָה:


ושאר כל המשקין – that we don’t use for medicinal purposes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:8)


**שׁוֹפָכִין.** מַיִם סְרוּחִין, וַחֲזוּ לְגַבֵּל בָּהֶן אֶת הַטִּיט:


שופכין – decaying waters and they are fit to stamp the clay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:1:9)


**כֻּלָּן בִּרְבִיעִית.** אַף הַיַּיִן וְהֶחָלָב וְהַדְּבַשׁ, דְּלֹא נֶאֶמְרוּ שִׁעוּרִין בַּמִּשְׁנָה אֶלָּא לְמַצְנִיעֵיהֶן, אֲבָל שְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם אֵין חַיָּבִין אֶלָּא בִּרְבִיעִית. וּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּמַצְנִיעַ עַצְמוֹ בָּעֵי שִׁעוּרָא זוּטָא, וּבְבָצִיר מֵהַאי שִׁעוּרָא לֹא מִחַיַּב, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן כָּל שֶׁאֵין כָּשֵׁר לְהַצְנִיעַ וְאֵין מַצְנִיעִין כָּמוֹהוּ שֶׁיִּתְחַיֵּב עָלָיו מַצְנִיעוֹ בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ברביעית – even wine, milk and honey, whose measurements are not mentioned in the Mishnah other than those who set them aside (see Mishnah Shabbat, Chapter 10, Mishnah 1), but the rest of all people, they are not liable other than for a quarter of a “log”(i.e., the volume of six eggs), and Rabbi Shimon holds that when he himself sets something aside/stores away, he requires a small measurement and less than that amount he is not liable for it, for Rabbi Shimon does not hold that all that is not fit to set aside, we do not set aside/store it like him for he will become liable for it, but sets aside/store away a little bit, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:1)


**אֹזֶן לְקֻפָּה.** לְאוֹחֲזָהּ בָּהּ:


אזן לקופה – to hold on to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:2)


**תְּלַאי.** לִתְלוֹתוֹ בּוֹ, וּבָצִיר מִשִּׁעוּר אֹזֶן לְקֻפָּה:


תלאי – to suspend it and this is less than the measurement of a handle for a basket.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:3)


**מִדַּת מִנְעָל.** לְהַרְאוֹת לָאֻמָּן כְּמִדָּה זוֹ אֲנִי צָרִיךְ, וְשִׁעוּרֵיהּ בָּצִיר מִתְּלַאי:


מדת מנעל – to show to an artisan: “like this measure I need” and its measurement is less than a hanger.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:4)


**נְיָר.** מֵעֲשָׂבִים עוֹשִׂין אוֹתוֹ:


נייר – they make it from grasses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:5)


**קֶשֶׁר שֶׁל מוֹכְסִין.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁאָדָם נוֹתֵן מֶכֶס בְּרֹאשׁ הַנָּהָר מִזֶּה, וְהוּא מוֹסֵר לוֹ חוֹתָם לְהַרְאוֹת לַמּוֹכֵס שֶׁבָּעֵבֶר הָאַחֵר מִן הַנָּהָר לְהַרְאוֹתוֹ שֶׁכְּבָר פָּרַע הַמֶּכֶס, וְדַרְכּוֹ לִכְתֹּב שְׁתֵּי אוֹתִיּוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת לְסִימָן, וְהֵן גְּדוֹלוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי אוֹתִיּוֹת שֶׁלָּנוּ:


קשר של מוכסין – sometimes when a person pays his tax at the head of the river from this side, and he transfers to him a signature to show the him that he already paid the tax and it was his practice to write two large letters as a sign and they were larger than our two letters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:2:6)


**נְיָר מָחוּק.** שׁוּב אֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִכְתֹּב, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ שִׁעוּר גָּדוֹל לִכְרֹךְ עַל פִּי צְלוֹחִית:


נייר מחוק – it is not appropriate to write again, therefore, he needs a large measure to wrap around the mouth of the flask/bottle.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ קָמֵעַ.** לְכַסּוֹת בּוֹ אֶת הַקָּמֵעַ:


לעשות בו קמיע – to cove with it the amulet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:2)


**קְלָף כְּדֵי לִכְתֹּב עָלָיו פָּרָשָׁה קְטַנָּה.** דְּאַיְדֵי דְּדָמָיו יְקָרִין לֹא עֲבַד מִנֵּיהּ קֶשֶׁר מֹכְסִין אֶלָּא תְּפִלִּין וּמְזוּזוֹת, וְלֹא מִחַיַּב בְּשִׁעוּרָא זוּטָא:


קלף כדי לכתוב [עליו] פרשה קטנה – for since his ink was expensive, he didn’t make from it the tax-collector’s receipt but rather [only] Tefillin and Mezuzot and he was not liable for a small measure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:3)


**דְּיוֹ כְּדֵי לִכְתֹּב שְׁתֵּי אוֹתִיּוֹת.** לִרְשֹׁם עַל שְׁתֵּי חֻלְיוֹת שֶׁל כְּלִי אוֹ עַל שְׁנֵי קְרָשִׁים לְזַוְּגָן:


דיו כדי לכתוב שתי אותיות – to mark on two segments of a utensil or on two boards to couple them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:3:4)


**כְּדֵי לִכְחֹל עַיִן אֶחָת.** שֶׁכֵּן צְנוּעוֹת הַהוֹלְכוֹת מְעֻטָּפוֹת אֵין מְגַלּוֹת אֶלָּא עַיִן אַחַת וְכוֹחֲלוֹת אוֹתָהּ:


בדי לכחול עין אחת – for modest women who walk wrapped up do not reveal other than one eye and they color/paint it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:1)


**הַשַּׁבְשֶׁבֶת.** הַצַּיָּדִין מוֹשִׁיבִין נֶסֶר קָטָן בְּרֹאשׁ הַקָּנֶה וְנוֹתְנִים עָלָיו דֶּבֶק, וְהָעוֹף יוֹשֵׁב עָלָיו וְנִדְבַּק בּוֹ, וְצָרִיךְ לָתֵת עָלָיו דֶּבֶק הַרְבֵּה כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא הָעוֹף נִדְבָּק בּוֹ:


השבשבת – the hunters place a planed board at the head of a reed and put glue on it and the bird sits on it and is fastened to it and most put a lot of glue on it in order that the bird will be fastened to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:2)


**[כְּדֵי] לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ נֶקֶב קָטָן.** כְּלִי שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין בּוֹ כֶּסֶף חַי וְסוֹתֵם פִּיו בְּזֶפֶת אוֹ בְּגָפְרִית, וְעוֹשֶׂה בַּסְּתִימָה נֶקֶב קָטָן לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנּוּ הַכֶּסֶף חַי:


לעשות בו נקב קטן – a utensil that place on it pure silver and we close up its mouth with pitch or bitumen/sulfur and it makes while closing It a small hole to remove the pure silver.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:3)


**חַרְסִית.** לְבֵנָה כְּתוּשָׁה:


חרסית – crushed bricks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:4)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת פִּי כוּר.** שֶׁהַמַּפּוּחַ נִכְנָס בּוֹ:


לעשות פי כור – the mechanic’s bellows enter into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:5)


**פִּטְפּוּט.** רֶגֶל לִמְקוֹם מוֹשַׁב הַכּוּר, שֶׁמּוֹשִׁיבִין אוֹתוֹ עַל כַּן וּבָסִיס הֶעָשׂוּי לְכָךְ:


פטפוט – a leg for the place where the smelting pot sits for we set it down it and a base is made for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:6)
