**סֻבִּין כְּדֵי לִתֵּן עַל פִּי הַכּוּר שֶׁל צוֹרְפֵי זָהָב.** בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין פֶּחָמִין צוֹרְפִין הַזָּהָב בְּאֵשׁ שֶׁל סֻבִּין. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר שֶׁרְגִילִים לָתֵת סֻבִּין עַל פִּי הַכּוּר כְּשֶׁמַּתִּיכִים הַזָּהָב:


סובין כדי ליתן על פי הכור של צורפי זהב – in a place where there isn’t charcoal, they smelt gold in fire of coarse-bran. Another explanation: they would customarily place coarse bran on the mouth of the bellows’ hole when they smelt the gold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:7)


**לָסוּד קְטַנָּה שֶׁבַּבָּנוֹת.** בָּנוֹת שֶׁהִגִּיעוּ לְפִרְקָן וְלֹא בָּא לָהֶן הָאֹרַח, בְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים טוֹפְלוֹת אוֹתָן בְּסִיד וְהָאֹרַח מְמַהֵר לָבֹא וּמַשִּׁיר נַמִּי אֶת הַשֵּׂעָר, וּבְנוֹת עֲשִׁירִים טוֹפְלוֹת בְּסֹלֶת, וּבְנוֹת מְלָכִים בְּשֶׁמֶן זַיִת שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיא שְׁלִישׁ:


לסוך קטנה שבבבנות – girls who reached their period of maturity, and they had not come to menstruation, the poor girls would line it with plaster and menstruation would quickly come and also remove the hair and the rich girls with line it with fine-flour, and the daughters of royalty with olive oil that had not grown one-third.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:8)


**כִּלְכּוּל.** הַצְּדָעַיִן, וְסָדִין אוֹתָן בְּסִיד לְהַשְׁכִּיב אֶת הַשֵּׂעָר:


כלכול – the temples, and we paint the skin with a depilatory of plaster to cause the hair to lie down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:4:9)


**אַנְדִּיפֵי.** לְמַטָּה מִן הַצְּדָעַיִן מְעַט, שֶׁקּוֹרִין בַּת צִדְעָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה לֹא כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה:


אנדיפי – lower than the temples a bit and we call it temple, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda nor according to Rabbi Nehemiah.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:1)


**אֲדָמָה.** טִיט אָדֹם:


אדמה – red plaster/clay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:2)


**מַרְצוּפִין.** שַׂקִּין גְּדוֹלוֹת שֶׁנּוֹשְׂאִין בָּהֶם פְּרַקְמַטְיָא בַּסְּפִינוֹת, וְחוֹתְמִין אוֹתָם כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁחוֹתְמִים הָאִגְּרוֹת. וְשִׁעוּרֵיהּ דְּרַבָּנָן זוּטָר מִדְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מרצופין – large sacks which we carry for business on ships and we seal them In the manner in which we seal letters and their Rabbinic measure is less than that of Rabbi Akiba, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:3)


**כְּדֵי לְזַבֵּל כְּרֵשָׁא.** כַּרְתִּי. וְשִׁעוּרֵיהּ זוּטָר מִקֶּלַח שֶׁל כְּרוּב. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


כדי לזבל ברישא – leek-greens and its measure is less than cabbage stalks and Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:4)


**מְלֹא כַף סִיד.** כַּף שֶׁל סַיָּדִין:


מלא כף סיד – spoon of plasterers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:5)


**כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת קֻלְמוֹס.** הַמַּגִּיעַ לַקְּשָׁרִין שֶׁל אֶמְצַע אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו:


כדי לעשות קולמוס – that reaches to the joints of his middle fingers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:6)


**עָב.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִכְתִיבָה:


עבה – which is not appropriate for writing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:7)


**מְרֻסָּס.** מְרֹצָץ וְשָׁבוּר:


מרוסס – crushed and broken.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:8)


**בֵּיצָה קַלָּה.** בֵּיצַת תַּרְנְגֹלֶת. וְאַמַּאי קָרֵי לַהּ בֵּיצָה קַלָּה, שֶׁקַּלָּה לְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר בֵּיצִים. וְהַשִּׁעוּר הוּא כְּדֵי לְבַשֵּׁל כִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת מִמֶּנָּה:


ביצה קלה – the egg of a chicken, and why do we call it the smallest of eggs? For it is easy to cook more than other eggs and the measure required in order to cook it is like a dried fig [size] from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:9)


**טְרוּפָה.** מְעֹרֶבֶת בְּשֶׁמֶן:


טרופה – mixed with oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:5:10)


**וּנְתוּנָה בָאִלְפָּס.** שֶׁהוּחַם כְּבָר, שֶׁהִיא מְמַהֶרֶת לְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל:


ונתונה באלפס – it was already warmed and it cooks speedily.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:1)


**תַּרְוָד.** כַּף:


תרווד – spoon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:2)


**חָף.** שֵׁן מִשִּׁנֵּי הַמַּפְתֵּחַ שֶׁפּוֹתְחִים בָּהּ הַדְּלָתוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


חף – a tooth from the teeth of the key that we open with it doors, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda (see Talmud Shabbat 81a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:3)


**כַּרְכָּר.** שֶׁל אוֹרְגִין, וּמַעֲבִירוֹ עַל הַשְּׁתִי כְּשֶׁהוּא מָתוּחַ לְפָנָיו וְשׁוֹבֵט בּוֹ הַחוּטִין:


כרכר – of weavers and we transfer it on the warp (i.e., longitudinal direction) when it is stretched before him and he presses the threads.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:6:4)


**כְּדֵי לִזְרֹק בִּבְהֵמָה.** דְּלֹא טָרַח אֱינַשׁ לְמִשְׁקַל צְרוֹר מִשּׁוּם עוֹף לְהַבְרִיחוֹ, דִּבְקָלָא בְּעָלְמָא סַגִּי לֵיהּ:


כדי לזרוק בבהמה – an a person doesn’t have to trouble him to throw a stone because of bird to chase it away, for one’s mere voice is enough for him.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:1)


**בֵּין פַּצִּים לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** כְּשֶׁמְּסַדְּרִין פַּצִּימִין וְעַמּוּדִין וְקוֹרוֹת וְעוֹשִׂין שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל קוֹרוֹת עַל הָאָרֶץ וְיֵשׁ חָלָל בֵּין זוֹ לְזוֹ סוֹמְכוֹ תַּחְתָּיו שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעַקֵּם:


בין פצים לחבירו – when they arrange the boards and the pillars and the beams and make rows of beams on the ground, and there is a space tween this one and that one, we support it underneath so that it does not become curved.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:2)


**זֵכֶר לַדָּבָר.** דְּחָשֵׁיב חֶרֶס בַּחֲתִיַּת הָאוּר:


זכר לדבר – for it is considered earthenware when taking the fire out in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:3)


**לַחְשֹׂף.** לִדְלוֹת:


לחשוף – to lift up/raise


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/8:7:4)


**מִגֶּבֶא.** גֻּמָּא קְטַנָּה שֶׁהַמַּיִם נִקְבָּצִים בָּהּ, אַלְמָא חָשֵׁיב חֶרֶס נַמִּי בְּקַבָּלַת הַמַּיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


מגבא – a small hole where the water is gathered in it, so we see that it is considered earthenware also in the receiving of water, and Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:1)


**אָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מִנַּיִן לַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁמְּטַמְּאָה בְמַשָּׂא.** אַיְדֵי דְּאָיְרֵי לְעֵיל בְּאַסְמַכְתָּא גַּבֵּי חֶרֶס, נָקַט נַמִּי לְהָנֵי קְרָאֵי דְּאַסְמַכְתָּא. וּקְרָא דְּתִזְרֵם כְּמוֹ דָּוָה הוּא סָמוּךְ לִקְרָא דְּלֹא יִמָּצֵא בִמְכִתָּתוֹ חֶרֶס דְּאַיְתֵי לְעֵיל. אִי נַמִּי מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנָא מִנַּיִן שֶׁמַּרְחִיצִין אֶת הַמִּילָה וְכוּ'‏ תָּנָא לְהָנָךְ מִנַּיִן דְּדָמוּ לַהּ:


אמר ר"ע מנין לעבודת כוכבים שמטמאת במשא – since they are concerned above with an Asmakhta/a scriptural text used as a support for a rabbinic enactment and the Biblical verse of (Isaiah 30:22): “You will cast them away like a menstruous woman,” it is a support to the Biblical verse that was brought above (Isaiah 30:14 – see Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 8, Mishnah 7): “So that no shard is left in its breakage;” alternatively, because it was necessary to teach (Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 9, Mishnah 3), “from where do we learn that we wash the circumcised child on the third day if it falls on the Sabbath?,” they taught these which are compared to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:2)


**מְטַמְּאָה בְמַשָּׂא.** הַנּוֹשֵׂא אוֹתָהּ יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא נָגַע בָּהּ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיְתָה בְּקֻפָּה וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ. וְאִיפְלִיגוּ רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְאָמְרֵי אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא אֶלָּא בְּמַגָּע כְּשֶׁרֶץ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מטמאה במשא – a person who carries it should wash his clothes and even if he didn’t touch it, such as it was in a box or something like that, and the Rabbis dispute on the statement of Rabbi Akiba and state that he does not become ritually defiled other than through contact like an unclean reptile, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:1:3)


**תִּזְרֵם כְּמוֹ (אשה) דָוָה.** כְּלוֹמַר יְהִי בְּעֵינֶיךָ כְּזָרִים, כְּמוֹ דָּוָה, כְּאִשָּׁה נִדָּה, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְהַדָּוָה בְּנִדָּתָהּ, וּבַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים מִשְׁתַּעֵי קְרָא:


תזרם כמו (אשה) דוה – that is to say, it should be in your eyes like foreigners like depressed like a menstruant woman, as it is written (Leviticus 15:33): “and concerning her who is in menstrual infirmity” and the Biblical verse is speaking about idolatry.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:1)


**שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה:


שהיא טהורה – that it is not susceptible to ritual defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:2)


**בְּלֶב יָם.** וּפְשִׁיטָא דָּאֳנִיָּה בְּלֵב יָם הִיא, אֶלָּא לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דִּסְפִינָה הֲרֵי הִיא כְּיָם, מַה יָּם טָהוֹר, אַף סְפִינָה טְהוֹרָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא שֶׁל חֶרֶס, וַאֲפִלּוּ טְעָנוּהָ בַּיַּבָּשָׁה וְהוֹרִידוּהָ לַיָּם:


בלב ים – for it is obvious that a ship is in the heart of the sea, but rather, it comes to inform us that a ship is a receptacle/pouch; just as the sea is ritually pure, so too the ship is pure, and even it if is of earthenware, and even if it is laden on the dry land and they lowered it into the sea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:3)


**שֶׁזּוֹרְעִים בָּהּ חֲמִשָּׁה זֵרְעוֹנִים.** וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ כְּדֵי לְהַפְרִישׁ בֵּינֵיהֶן הֶפְרֵשׁ הָרָאוּי וְלֹא הֲוֵי עִרְבּוּב:


שזורעים בה חמשה זרעונים – and there is within it in order to set aside between them the appropriate difference so there will not be any confusion/mixture.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:4)


**אַרְבָּעָה בְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הָעֲרוּגָה.** מְמַלֵּא אֶת כָּל הָרוּחַ עַד סָמוּךְ לַקֶּרֶן, וּבָאֶמְצַע אֵינוֹ זוֹרֵעַ אֶלָּא גַּרְעִין אֶחָד, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַגַּרְעִין שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע רָחוֹק שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים מִן הַזָּרוּעַ שֶׁבְּכָל רוּחַ, דְּשִׁעוּר יְנִיקַת כָּל זֶרַע טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵצֶל הַקְּרָנוֹת שֶׁבָּרוּחוֹת הַזְּרָעִים קְרוֹבִים זֶה לָזֶה וְאֵין בֵּינֵיהֶם הַרְחָקָה שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים וְיוֹנְקִין זֶה מִזֶּה, אֵין כָּאן בֵּית מֵחוֹשׁ, דְּאַכִּלְאַיִם בִּלְבַד קָפֵיד קְרָא דְּלֹא לֵיהֱוֵי עִרְבּוּב, וְלִיְנִיקָה לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, כְּדִתְנָן (בבא בתרא פ"ב מי"ב) הָיָה גָּדֵר בֵּינְתַיִם זֶה סוֹמֵךְ לַגָּדֵר מִכָּאן וְזֶה סוֹמֵךְ לַגָּדֵר מִכָּאן, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּיָנְקֵי מִתַּתָּאֵי. וְכָאן יֵשׁ הֶכֵּר גָּדוֹל, שֶׁרוּחַ זוֹ זְרוּעָה צָפוֹן וְדָרוֹם, וְרוּחַ זוֹ זְרוּעָה מִזְרָח וּמַעֲרָב, אֲבָל בֵּין זֶרַע הָאֶמְצָעִי לְזֵרְעוֹנֵי הָרוּחוֹת אֵין הֶכֵּר, וְאִי מְקָרְבֵי הֲוֵי עִרְבּוּב, הִלְכָּךְ צָרִיךְ הֶרְחֵק כְּדֵי יְנִיקָה:


ארבעה בארבע רוחות להערוגה – they fill each direction until near the corner and in the middle, they don’t sow other than one globule in order that the globule that is in the center will be distant three handbreadths from what is sown in each direction, for the measure of absorption of each sown area is one-and-one-half handbreadths , and even though that nearest the corners in the directions of the seeds that are closest to each other, and there isn’t between them a distance of three handbreadths, and they absorb from each other, and in this case there is no room for doubt, for the Biblical verse was strict only about Kilayim/mixed seeds, and there was no confusion, and we don’t concern ourselves about absorption as it is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Bava Batra, Chapter 2, Mishnah 12): “If there was a fence between them, this one relies on the fence from this direction and the other relies on the fence from that direction” and even though they absorb from underneath , and here there is a large recognition that the the this direction is sown to the north and the south and that direction is sown to the east and the west, but between the intermediate seed to the seeds of the directions, there is no recognition, and if they were brought close, there would be confusion; therefore, there is a need for distance in order to absorb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:2:5)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כִּי כָאָרֶץ תּוֹצִיא צִמְחָהּ.** תּוֹצִיא חַד, צִמְחָהּ חַד, זֵרוּעֶיהָ תְּרֵי, תַּצְמִיחַ חַד, הֲרֵי חֲמִשָּׁה. וְשִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים לֵיכָּא לְמֵילַף מִקְּרָא, אֶלָּא קִים לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן דַּחֲמִשָּׁה זֵרְעוֹנִים בְּשִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים לֹא יָנְקֵי הָרוּחוֹת מִן הָאֶמְצָעִי, וְלֹא הָאֶמְצָעִי מִן הָרוּחוֹת, דְּשִׁעוּר יְנִיקַת כָּל זֶרַע טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי רָמִיז לִקְרָא חֲמִשָּׁה זֵרְעוֹנִים, בַּעֲרוּגָה בַּת שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים קָאָמַר. וּבְמַסֶּכֶת כִּלְאַיִם פֵּרַשְׁתִּי הִלְכוֹת עֲרוּגָה וּפְרָטוֹתֶיהָ וְדִקְדּוּקֶיהָ, וְכָאן קִצַּרְתִּי:


שנאמר כי כארץ תוציא צמחה – (through the analysis of words in the verse – Isaiah 61:11: “For just as the earth brings forth her growth, and a garden makes the seed shoot up, (So the LORD God will make victory and renown shoot up in the presence of the nations”]) – "תוציא" /bring forth – is one; "צמחה"/her growth – is one; "זרועיה"/the seed – is two; "תצמיח"/shoot up – is one – that makes five. But the six handbreadths cannot be derived from Biblical verse but rather it is established by the Rabbis that the five rows of plants in six handbreadths, the four corners will not absorb from the middle and the middle will not [absorb] from the corners, for the measurement of absorption of each seed is a handbreadth and a half; therefore, Scripture hints that five rows of plants in one bed that is six handbreadths, is as stated. And in Tractate Kilayim, I explained the laws of a a bed and its details and its specifications, but here I shortened it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:1)


**שֶׁהִיא טְמֵאָה.** דְּיוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי אַכַּתִּי לֹא מַסְרְחָא שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע, וּרְאוּיָה לִקְלֹט וְלִהְיוֹת וָלָד נוֹצָר מִמֶּנָּה, וְקָרִינַן בָּהּ שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע הָרָאוּי לְהַזְרִיעַ:


שהיא טמאה – for on the third day, her semen has not yet become decayed – and she is worthy to conceive and to have a fetus created from her and we call it semen – that is worthy of being sown.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:2)


**לִשְׁלֹשֶׁת יָמִים.** דְּהִקְפִּיד הַכָּתוּב עַל טְמֵאֵי קֶרִי בְּמַתַּן תּוֹרָה, לְכָךְ הִפְרִישָׁן שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים, וְלֹא חָשׁ אִם יַפְלִיטוּ אַחַר כָּךְ בְּיוֹם רְבִיעִי לַהַפְרָשָׁה, דְּמַסְרִיחַ וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְהַזְרִיעַ. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה שֶׁהַפּוֹלֶטֶת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי טְהוֹרָה, וּמַתְנִיתִין מְשַׁבַּשְׁתָּא הִיא, וְתָנֵי מִנַּיִן לְפוֹלֶטֶת שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר הֱיוּ נְכוֹנִים לִשְׁלֹשֶׁת יָמִים, וְלִשְׁלֹשֶׁת יָמִים דְּאָמַר קְרָא הַיְנוּ לַיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, דִּבְיוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי לַהַפְרָשָׁה נִתְּנָה תּוֹרָה. אִי נַמִּי לֹא מְשַׁבַּשְׁתָּא הִיא אֶלָּא [רַבָּנָן דְּרַבִּי] אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה הִיא, דִּסְבִירָא [לְהוּ] הַפּוֹלֶטֶת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי טְמֵאָה, וְלֵית הִלְכְתָא [כְּוָתַיְהוּ]:


לשלשה ימים – for Scripture was strict on those who were defiled by emissions at the Giving of the Torah, therefore, it separated them three days and did not concern itself if they would discharge afterwards on the forth day of separation that decays/is offensive and not worthy of being sown. And the Halakhic decision is that the a woman who discharges on the third day is ritually pure, and our Mishnah is a faulty version, and should teach: “From when does a woman who discharges semen on the third day that she is ritually pure, as it states (Exodus 19:15): “Be ready for the third day: [do not go near a woman].” And the three days that are mentioned in the Biblical verse is equivalent to the third day, for on the third day of separation, the Torah was given. Alternatively, it (i.e., our Mishnah) is not a faulty version but rather, it is [the Rabbis of Rabbi] Eleazar ben Azariah [who] hold that she who emits on the third day is impure, and the Halakha is not [like them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:3)


**מִנַּיִן שֶׁמַּרְחִיצִין וְכוּ'.** אֲפִלּוּ בְּחַמִּין שֶׁהוּחְמוּ בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּאַף בְּיוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי מְסֻכָּן הוּא, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן רִאשׁוֹן וְשֵׁנִי. וְהַמַּיִם חַמִּין מַחְזִיקִין לְגוּף הַתִּינוֹק וּמַבְרִיאִין אוֹתוֹ:


מנין שמרחיצין וכו' – even in hot water that was heated on the Sabbath, for even on the third day, [the child] is in danger, and all the more so, on the first and second days, for hot water strengthens the body of the baby boy and heals him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:3:4)


**לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל זְהוֹרִית.** כְּמִין לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל צֶמֶר אָדֹם, הָיוּ קוֹשְׁרִין חֶצְיוֹ בְּרֹאשׁ הַשָּׂעִיר הַמִּשְׁתַּלֵּחַ לַעֲזָאזֵל וְחֶצְיוֹ בַּצּוּק, וּכְשֶׁהָיָה דּוֹחֶה הַשָּׂעִיר לְמַטָּה הָיָה הַלָּשׁוֹן הַהוּא מַלְבִּין וְיוֹדְעִין שֶׁנִּתְכַּפְּרוּ עֲוֹנוֹתֵיהֶם:


לשון של זהורית – like the language of a red wool that they would tie half on the head of the goat that was sent to Azazel and half on the precipice, and when the goat was pushed down, the strip would turn white and everyone knew that their sins were atoned for.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:4:1)


**מִנַּיִן לְסִיכָה שֶׁהִיא כַשְּׁתִיָּה.** לֹא כִּשְׁתִיָּה מַמָּשׁ לְחִיּוּבֵי עֲלַהּ כָּרֵת, אֶלָּא שֶׁהִיא אֲסוּרָה:


מנין לסיכה שהיא כשתיה – not actually like drinking to make him liable for extirpation, but that it is forbidden.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:1)


**בֵּיצָה קַלָּה.** בֵּיצַת תַּרְנְגֹלֶת שֶׁהִיא קַלָּה לְבַשֵּׁל יוֹתֵר מִכָּל שְׁאָר בֵּיצִים, וְלֹא כְּדֵי לְבַשֵּׁל כֻּלָּהּ אֶלָּא כִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת מִמֶּנָּה, טְרוּפָה וּנְתוּנָה בָּאִלְפָּס:


ביצה קלה – the egg of a chicken is easier to cook more than other eggs, and not in order to cook all of it but like a dried fig’s worth of it,, and it is placed in a tightly covered pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:2)


**וּמִצְטָרְפִין.** כָּל מִינֵי תְּבָלִין זֶה עִם זֶה:


ומצטרפין – all kinds of spices with each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:3)


**אִסְטִיס.** ני"ל בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי, וְצִבְעוֹ דּוֹמֶה לִתְכֵלֶת:


אסטיס – “Neel” in the Arabic language and its color is similar to Tekhelet/the purple-blue thread used on the Tzizit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:4)


**וּפוּאָה.** שָׁרְשֵׁי עֵשֶׂב שֶׁצּוֹבְעִין בָּהֶם אָדֹם, וּבָעֲרָבִי אלפו"ה:


ופואה – the reeds of gras that we color them red, and in Arabic “Alphu-ha.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:5)


**בַּסְּבָכָה.** בְּרֹאשׁ הַסְּבָכָה שֶׁעֲשׂוּיָה כִּקְלִיעָה נוֹתְנִים מְעַט בֶּגֶד:


סבכה – at the head of the net-work/matting which is made like a triple-thread/twist/network in which they place a bit of cloth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:6)


**נֶתֶר.** מִין אֲדָמָה הוּא, וּמַזְהִיר, וְקוֹרְאִים לוֹ אלו"ם בְּלַעַ"ז:


נתר – a kind of soil and it shines, and we called it “Alum” in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:7)


**בֹּרִית.** מִין צֶמַח שֶׁמְּנַקֶּה וּמְטַהֵר:


בורית – a kind af plant that cleans and purifies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:8)


**קִמּוֹנְיָא.** עֵשֶׂב שֶׁמְּנַקִּים בַּעֲפָרוֹ אֶת הַיָּדַיִם לְהַעֲבִיר אֶת הַזֻּהֲמָא, וּבִלְשׁוֹן תַּלְמוּד קָרוּי שְׁלוֹף דּוּץ, וּבָעֲרָבִי קוֹרִין לוֹ אלקל"י:


קמוניא – a grass that cleanses the hands in its dust to remove the filth, and in the language of the Talmud, it is called “pull out that which is stuck in” and in Arabic we call it “Alkeli.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:9)


**וְאַשְׁלָג.** לֹא אִתְפָּרֵשׁ לִי מַה הוּא:


ואשלג – it was not explained to me what this is.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:5:10)


**עַל הַכָּתֶם.** הַנִּמְצָא בַּבֶּגֶד, וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם דַּם נִדָּה הוּא אִם לָאו, מַעֲבִירִין עָלָיו שִׁבְעָה דְּבָרִים לִבְדֹּק אוֹתוֹ בָּהֶם. וּמִכְּלָלָם אַרְבָּעָה סַמָּנִין הַלָּלוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


על הכתם – that is found in the clothing and it is not known if it is the blood of a menstruant woman or not – we remove seven things to examine him with them, and among them are these four signs, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda (see Tractate Niddah, Chapter 6, Mishnah 9).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:1)


**פִּלְפֶּלֶת כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** דְּחַזְיָא לְרֵיחַ הַפֶּה, וְאֵין זֶה פִּלְפֵּל הַמָּצוּי בֵּינֵינוּ:


פלפלת כל שהוא – that appears like scent of the mouth, but this is not the pepper that is found among us.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:2)


**עִטְרָן כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** שֶׁמְּרַפְּאִים בּוֹ מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ כְּאֵב חֲצִי הָרֹאשׁ:


עטרן כל שהוא – that heals a person whomever has a pain in half of his head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:3)


**מִינֵי בְשָׂמִים כָּל שֶׁהֵן.** לְרֵיחַ טוֹב:


מיני בשמים כל שהן – for a good scent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:4)


**מִינֵי מַתָּכוֹת כָּל שֶׁהֵן.** שֶׁרָאוּי לַעֲשׂוֹת מֵהֶן דָּרְבָּן קָטָן:


מיני מתכות כל שהן – that is appropriate to make from them a small goad/end of the handle of a ploughshare/iron point on the staff.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:5)


**מֶקֶק.** רֶקֶב הַנּוֹפֵל מִסְּפָרִים אוֹ מִמִּטְפָּחוֹת שֶׁבָּלוּ, לְשׁוֹן הָמֵק בְּשָׂרוֹ (זכריה יד):


מקק – rottenness that falls from scrolls or covers that wore out – from the language (Zechariah 14:12): “Their flesh shall rot away [while they stand on their feet].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:6)


**שֶׁמַּצְנִיעִים אוֹתוֹ לְגָנְזוֹ.** שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר קָדוֹשׁ טָעוּן גְּנִיזָה:


שמצניעים אותו לנונזו – for every sacred thing required being hidden away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:6:7)


**מְאוּמָה.** אַלְמָא אַחְשְׁבֵיהּ קְרָא לְאִסּוּרֵיהּ. וְתִקּוּן גָּדוֹל הוּא עוֹשֶׂה כְּשֶׁמּוֹצִיא עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה מִבֵּיתוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מאומה – so we see that the Biblical verse thought to forbid it and he does a great repair, and he removes idolatry from his house, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:1)


**הָרוֹכְלִין.** מוֹכְרֵי בְשָׂמִים לְקִשּׁוּטֵי נָשִׁים, וְיֵשׁ לָהֶם קֻפּוֹת קְטַנּוֹת לִצְרוֹרוֹת הַבְּשָׂמִים:


הרוכלין - those who sell spices for the decoration of women and they have small boxes for the stones of the spices.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:2)


**אֵינוֹ חַיָּב אֶלָּא חַטָּאת אֶחָת.** דְּכֻלָּן חֲדָא הוֹצָאָה הִיא:


אינו חייב אלא חטאת אחת – all of them are one “taking out.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:3)


**פָּחוֹת מִכַּגְּרוֹגָרֶת.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּכָל הָאֳכָלִין שִׁעוּרָן כִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת, הָנֵי כֵּיוָן דְּלִזְרִיעָה קַיְּמֵי, אֲפִלּוּ בְּפָחוֹת מִכַּגְּרוֹגֶרֶת נַמִּי מִחַיַּב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶּן בְּתֵירָא שֶׁאוֹמֵר חֲמִשָּׁה:


פחות מכגרוגרת – even though all the foods, their measure is like a dried-fig’s bulk, these, since they exist for planting even with less that a dried-fig’s bulk, they are liable, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda ben Beteyra who said five.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:4)


**זֶרַע קִשּׁוּאִין.** חָשׁוּב מִשְּׁאָר זֵרְעוֹנֵי גִּנָּה, וְכֵן זֶרַע דְּלוּעִין, וְזֶרַע פּוֹל הַמִּצְרִי, וְהוּא שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ פאסל"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


זרע קשואין – it is more important than the other seeds of the garden, and similarly, the seeds of gourds and the seeds of Egyptian beans, and he calls them “Fasli” in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:5)


**חָגָב חַי כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** שֶׁמַּצְנִיעִין אוֹתוֹ לְקָטָן לְשַׂחֵק בּוֹ:


חגב חי כל שהוא – we hide it for a small child to play with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:6)


**מֵת כַּגְּרוֹגֶרֶת.** כְּדִין שְׁאָר אֳכָלִין:


מת כגרוגרת – like the law for other food stuffs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:7)


**צִפֹּרֶת כְּרָמִים.** עוֹף שֶׁמָּצוּי בֵּין הַדְּקָלִין הַבַּחוּרִים:


צפורת כרמים – a bird that is found between the male palm-trees.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:8)


**בֵּין חַיָּה בֵּין מֵתָה כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מִמֶּנּוּ רְפוּאָה לִפְקֹחַ וּלְהַחְכִּים:


בין חיה בין מתה כל שהוא – for we make from it a medication to make bright and to make wise.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 9:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/9:7:9)


**חָגָב חַי טָמֵא כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** וְתַנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר טָמֵא אֵין מַצְנִיעִין אוֹתוֹ לַתִּינוֹק, דִּלְמָא מָיֵת וְאָכֵיל לֵיהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


חגב חי טמא כל שהוא – but the first Tanna/teacher holds that if it is ritually impure, we don’t put it away for a young child, lest he come and eat it, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:1)


**הַמַּצְנִיעַ.** קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת:


המצניע – prior to Shabbat


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:2)


**לְזֶרַע וּלְדֻגְמָא.** לְהַרְאוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ סַמְמָנִים וְיִקְנוּ מִמֶּנּוּ:


לזרע ולדוגמא – to demonstrate that he has artist’s materials and that [people] will buy from him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:3)


**וְלִרְפוּאָה.** וְשָׁכַח בְּשַׁבָּת לְמַה הִצְנִיעַ, וְהוֹצִיאוֹ סְתָם, חַיָּב עָלָיו בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא, שֶׁעַל דַּעַת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה הוֹצִיאוֹ, וְהָא אַחְשְׁבֵיהּ:


ולרפואה – and he forgot on Shabbat why he had stored them away and merely took them out, he is culpable however small the amount, for on the first thought he took it out and considering it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:4)


**אֶלָּא כְשִׁעוּרוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד שִׁעוּר הַמְּפֹרָשׁ בּוֹ:


Rather the amount of every measurement that is described here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:5)


**חָזַר וְהִכְנִיסוֹ.** זֶה שֶׁהִצְנִיעַ פָּחוֹת מִכְּשִׁעוּר וְהוֹצִיאוֹ, אִם נִמְלַךְ בּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לְזָרְעוֹ וְחָזַר וְהִכְנִיסוֹ:


חזר והכניסו – he who stored it away less than its prescribed quantity and removed it, if he considered it that it wasn’t to sow it and then went back and brought it in.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:1:6)


**אֵינוֹ חַיָּב.** בְּהַכְנָסָה זוֹ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יֵשׁ בּוֹ שִׁעוּר שָׁלֵם, דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִמְלַךְ שֶׁלֹּא לְזָרְעוֹ בָּטְלָה מַחְשַׁבְתּוֹ וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּכָל אָדָם:


אינו חייב – in his bringing this in other than if it has the complete prescribed quantity for since he had considered not to so it, his intention was nullified and he is like any person.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:1)


**עַל הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** שֶׁהִיא כַּרְמְלִית, כְּגוֹן שֶׁגְּבֹהָה מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד תִּשְׁעָה וּרְחָבָה אַרְבָּעָה:


על האסקופה – which is a Karmelit such as that it is higher than three [handbreadths] and up to nine [handbreadths] and is wide four [handbreadths].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂה מְלַאכְתּוֹ בְּבַת אֶחָת.** לֹא עָשָׂה עֲקִירָה מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וַהֲנָחָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים שֶׁהֵם מְקוֹם חִיּוּב, דְּבַהֲכִי הַוְיָא מְלָאכָה גְּמוּרָה. אֶלָּא עָקַר מִמְּקוֹם חִיּוּב וְהֵנִיחַ בַּכַּרְמְלִית, שֶׁהוּא פָּטוּר בְּאוֹתָהּ הֲנָחָה, וַהֲדַר עָקַר לַהּ מִכַּרְמְלִית שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקוֹם חִיּוּב וְהֵנִיחַ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא נַעֲשֵׂית עֲקִירָה מִמְּקוֹם חִיּוּב וַהֲנָחָה בִּמְקוֹם חִיּוּב בְּפַעַם אַחַת:


שלא עשה מלאכתו בבת אחת – he did not perform an uprooting from the private domain and a placing down in the public domain which is the place of liability for in that, it would be a complete [cycle] of [forbidden] work, but rather, he uprooted from the place of liability and placed it down in a Karmelit, which is exempt in that placing it down, and then went back and uprooted it from the Karmelit which is not a place of liability and placed it in the public domain, and it was found that there was no uprooting done from a place of liability and a placing down in a place of liability at one [and the same] time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:3)


**אַסְקֻפָּה הַחִיצוֹנָה.** אִצְטַבָּא שֶׁלִּפְנֵי פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת לְצַד רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


אסקופה החיצונה – a balcony before the the opening of he house at the side of the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:2:4)


**עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיא.** כְּלוֹמַר אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הוֹצִיא בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה כָּל הַקֻּפָּה. וְלֹא שָׁנוּ אֶלָּא בְּקֻפָּה מְלֵאָה קִשּׁוּאִין וּדְלוּעִין שֶׁהֵן אֲרֻכִּין וַעֲדַיִן יֵשׁ מֵהֶם לִפְנִים, אֲבָל מְלֵאָה חַרְדָּל שֶׁהֲרֵי יֵשׁ הַרְבֵּה מִמֶּנּוּ כֻּלּוֹ מִבַּחוּץ, נַעֲשָׂה כְּמִי שֶׁהוֹצִיא אֶת כָּל הַקֻּפָּה וְחַיָּב:


עד שיוציא – that is to say, other than if he removed at first all of the basket and we are not teaching other than regarding a basket filled with cucumbers and gourds which are long and there are yet some of them inside. But if it (i.e., the basket) was filled with mustard of which there are a lot of them and all of them are from the outside, it is made as one who had removed the entire basket and he is liable [a sin-offering].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:1)


**שֶׁכֵּן מַשָּׂא בְנֵי קְהָת.** בַּכָּתֵף יִשָּׂאוּ, וִימִין וּשְׂמֹאל וְחֵיק אוֹרְחָא דְּאַרְעָא:


שכן משא בני קהת – they would carry it on their shoulders and the right or left and the bosom are the way of the world.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:2)


**מַרְפְּקוֹ.** הַפֶּרֶק הָאֶמְצָעִי שֶׁבַּזְּרֹעַ, וּבָעֲרָבִי כָּךְ שְׁמוֹ מרפ"ק:


מרפקו – the middle joint of he arm and in Arabic, such is its name: “Marpik.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:3)


**בַּאֲפוּנְדָתוֹ.** אֵזוֹר חָלוּל:


באפונדתו – a hallowed out area.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:4)


**פִּיהָ לְמַטָּה.** אֵין דֶּרֶךְ הוֹצָאָה בְּכָךְ. אֲבָל פִּיהָ לְמַעְלָה דֶּרֶךְ הוֹצָאָה בְּכָךְ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, אֲפוּנְדָתוֹ בֶּגֶד שֶׁלּוֹבֵשׁ סָמוּךְ לִבְשָׂרוֹ לְקַבֵּל הַזֵּעָה, וּרְגִילִין לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ כְּעֵין כִּיסִין, וּכְשֶׁמְּהַפְּכִים אוֹתוֹ מִלְּמַטָּה לְמַעְלָה נִמְצָא פִּי הַכִּיס לְמַטָּה:


פיה למטה – that is not the manner of removing something, but if its opening is upwards, this is the manner of taking something out. Another explanation of אפונדתו – clothing that one wears close to the skin to receive the sweat, and is customary to make it in like pouches/pockets and where they turn it inside out from the bottom to the top, the opening of the pocket is below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:3:5)


**שְׂפַת חֲלוּקוֹ.** הַשָּׂפָה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה שֶׁל חָלוּק:


שפת חלקו – the lowest rim of the shirt/garment.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:1)


**לְפָנָיו וּבָא לוֹ לְאַחֲרָיו פָּטוּר.** דְּנִתְכַּוֵּן לִשְׁמִירָה מְעֻלָּה וְעָלְתָה בְּיָדוֹ שְׁמִירָה פְּחוּתָה:


לפניו ובא לו לאחריו פטור – for he had intended for a better/higher quality guarding, and it ended up being a lesser guarding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:2)


**לְאַחֲרָיו וּבָא לוֹ לְפָנָיו חַיָּב.** דְּנִתְכַּוֵּן לִשְׁמִירָה פְּחוּתָה וְעָלְתָה בְּיָדוֹ שְׁמִירָה מְעֻלָּה:


לאחריו ובא לו לפניו חייב – for he had intended for a lesser guarding, and ended up being a better/higher quality guarding.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:3)


**סִינָר.** כְּעֵין מִכְנָסַיִם קְטַנִּים חוֹגְרוֹת אוֹתָן לִצְנִיעוּת, וְאִם תָּלְתָה בָּהֶן שׁוּם דָּבָר לְהוֹצִיא וּבָא לָהּ לְצַד אַחֵר חַיֶּבֶת:


סינר – a kind of small pants that she wears them as a matter of chastity. And if she suspended on them something to take out and went to the other side, she is liable [for a sin-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:4)


**שֶׁכֵּן רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת חוֹזֵר.** דַּרְכּוֹ לִהְיוֹת חוֹזֵר וְסוֹבֵב סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ, וּמִתְּחִלָּה יָדְעָה שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לְהִתְהַפֵּךְ:


שכן ראוי להיות חוזר – for its manner is to shift around and go about her and from the beginning she knew that at its conclusion, it would reverse itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:4:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אַף מְקַבְּלֵי פִתְקִין.** שֶׁל מֶלֶךְ. הָרָצִים הַנּוֹשְׂאִים אִגְּרוֹת נוֹתְנִים אוֹתָם בְּעֵצִים חֲלוּלִים כִּדְמוּת קָנִים וְתוֹלִים בְּצַוָּארָם, וְדֶרֶךְ אוֹתוֹ קָנֶה לִהְיוֹת חוֹזֵר וְסוֹבֵב מִלְּפָנָיו וּמֵאַחֲרָיו סָבִיב לְצַוָּארוֹ, וְיוֹדֵעַ הָיָה שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לְהִתְהַפֵּךְ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר אף מקבלי פתקין – of the king. Runners carry letters and put them in hallowed out wood in the form of reeds and hang them on their necks and it is the manner of that reed to shift around from in front of him to behind him around his neck [and] he knows that eventually it will turn around, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:1)


**הוֹצִיאוּהוּ שְׁנַיִם פְּטוּרִים.** כִּדְיָלְפִינַן (ויקרא ד) בַּעֲשׂוֹתָהּ אַחַת מִכָּל מִצְוֹת ה', הָעוֹשֶׂה אֶת כֻּלָּהּ וְלֹא הָעוֹשֶׂה מִקְצָתָהּ:


הוציאו שנים פטואים – for we derive it (Leviticus 4:27 - though the exact formulation quoted by the Bartenura commentary does not appear anywhere: "ועשותה אחת [מכל] מצות ה'" – “if any person from among the populace unwittingly incurs guilt by doing any of the things which by the LORD’s commandments [ought not to be done, and he realizes his guilt]”; he who does all of it and not who does part of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:2)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר.** דְּסָבַר אֲפִלּוּ כְּשֶׁאֵין יָחִיד יָכֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ אִם עֲשָׂאוּהָ שְׁנַיִם פְּטוּרִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש פוטר – for he holds that even when one person cannot do it, if two did it [together] they are exempt, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:3)


**אֶת הַחַי בַּמִּטָּה.** עַל הַחַי לֹא מִחַיַּב בְּהוֹצָאָתוֹ אִם אֵינוֹ כָּפוּת, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא נוֹשֵׂא אֶת עַצְמוֹ. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי אָדָם, אֲבָל בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וְעוֹף לֹא, דִּכְמַאן דִּכְפוּתִין דָּמוּ:


את החי במטה – on the living, one does not become liable in removing him, if he is not tied up/bandaged, because he would carry himself and these words refer to a person, but not for cattle, wildlife or fowl, for it is like someone who is tied up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:4)


**וְכֵן כַּזַּיִת מִן הַמֵּת.** חַיָּב אִם הוֹצִיאוֹ, דְּהוֹאִיל וּמְטַמֵּא, הוֹצָאָה חֲשׁוּבָה הִיא לְהַצִּיל עַצְמוֹ מִן הַטֻּמְאָה. וְכֵן כַּזַּיִת מִן הַנְּבֵלָה וְכָעֲדָשָׁה מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ, דְּהָכִי הֲוֵי שִׁעוּרָן לְטֻמְאָה:


וכן כזית מן המת – he is liable if he removed him, for since it is considered that removal defiles to save oneself from defilement, and similarly an olive’s bulk from a carrion and a lentil’s bulk from a creeping thing, for these are their measures for defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:5:5)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר.** אֲפִלּוּ בְּמֵת שָׁלֵם, דַּהֲוֵי מְלָאכָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְגוּפָהּ, דְּכָל שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְסַלְּקוֹ מֵעָלָיו הַוְיָא מְלָאכָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְגוּפָהּ וְלָאו מְלֶאכֶת מַחֲשֶׁבֶת הִיא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון פוטר – even with an entire dead person for this is work that is not required for its body [on the Sabbath], for everything that is not necessary other than to remove it from him is a labor that is not necessary for itself, and it is not a productive work (with a direct purpose), and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:1)


**וְכֵן שְׂעָרוֹ.** הַתּוֹלֵשׁ שְׂעַר רֹאשׁוֹ בְּיָדוֹ:


וכן שערו – he tears the hair of his head by hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:2)


**וְכֵן הַגּוֹדֶלֶת.** שְׂעַר רֹאשָׁהּ וְקוֹלַעַת אוֹתוֹ, תַּרְגּוּם עֲבוֹת (שמות כח) גְּדִילוּ:


וכן הגודלת – the hair of her head and plaits/twists it. It is the Aramaic translation )Exodus 28:14,22): “[and two chains of pure gold] braid [these like corded work, and fasten the corded chains to the frames]” (or “on the breastpiece make] braided [chains of corded work in pure gold]”) “plaiting, wreathing”.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:3)


**הַכּוֹחֶלֶת.** נוֹתֶנֶת כָּחָל בְּעֵינֶיהָ:


הכוחלת – placing powder used for painting the eye-lids/stibium.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:4)


**הַפּוֹקֶסֶת.** מְחַלֶּקֶת שֵׂעָר שֶׁבְּאֶמְצַע הָרֹאשׁ מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן אֵצֶל הַצְּדָעַיִם:


פוסקת – separating the hair that is in the middle of the head to here and there near the temples.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:5)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב חַטָּאת.** כּוֹחֶלֶת מִשּׁוּם כּוֹתֶבֶת, גּוֹדֶלֶת וּפוֹקֶסֶת מִשּׁוּם בּוֹנָה:


רבי אליעזר מחייב חטאת – painting the eyelids because of writing dressing her hair and painting the face with rouge because of [the prohibition against] building.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת.** דְּסָבְרֵי אֵין דֶּרֶךְ כְּתִיבָה בְּכָךְ וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בִּנְיָן בְּכָךְ, וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וְדַוְקָא הַנּוֹטֵל צִפָּרְנָיו וּשְׂעָרוֹ בְּיָדָיו הוּא דְּפָטְרִי רַבָּנָן מֵחַטָּאת וּמִסְּקִילָה, אֲבָל הַנּוֹטֵל שְׂעָרוֹ אוֹ צִפָּרְנָיו בִּכְלִי מוֹדִים חֲכָמִים דְּחַיָּב חַטָּאת. וּמִכִּי נָטַל שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת חַיָּב. וְהַנּוֹטֵל שְׂעַר רֹאשׁ חֲבֵרוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בַּיָּד חַיָּב. וְצִפֹּרֶן שֶׁפָּרְשָׁה רֻבָּהּ אוֹ שֵׂעָר שֶׁנִּתְלַשׁ רֻבּוֹ וּמְצַעֵר אוֹתוֹ, מֻתָּר לִטְּלוֹ בַּיָּד לְכַתְּחִלָּה:


וחכמים אוסרים משום שבות – for they hold that there is no manner of writing in this, and there is no manner of building in this, and the Halakha is according to the Sages. And specifically, removing one’s fingernails or hair with one’s hands is what the Rabbis exempt from a sin-offering and stoning, but removing one’s hair or fingernails with a utensil, the Sages agree that one is liable for a sin-offering, and because he removed two hairs, he liable. But a person who removes the hair of the head of his fellow, even with his hand is liable. But a nail, most of which is separated or hair, most of which is detached and causes him pain, it is permitted to remove it by hand ab initio.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:7)


**הַתּוֹלֵשׁ מֵעָצִיץ נָקוּב חַיָּב.** דַּהֲוֵי כִּמְחֻבָּר, דְּיוֹנֵק מִן הַקַּרְקַע עַל יְדֵי הַנֶּקֶב, שֶׁמֵּרִיחַ לַחְלוּחִית הַקַּרְקַע, וַאֲפִלּוּ הַנֶּקֶב בְּצִדּוֹ. וְשִׁעוּר הַנֶּקֶב כְּדֵי שֹׁרֶשׁ קָטָן:


התולש מעציץ נקוב חייב – this is something attached like something fed from the ground through the hole that smells the moistness of the ground and even a hole at its side, and the measure of the hole in order that its root is small.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 10:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/10:6:8)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר בָּזֶה וּבָזֶה.** דְּלֹא חָשֵׁיב לֵיהּ כִּמְחֻבָּר לְחַיְּבוֹ סְקִילָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ורבי שמעון פוטר בזה ובזה – for he does not consider it as attached to make him liable for stoning, and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:1:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵק מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הוּא מָקוֹם שֶׁהֻקַּף אַרְבַּע מְחִצּוֹת גְּבוֹהוֹת עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים וּבֵינֵיהֶן אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים עַל אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר, עַל כֵּן אֲפִלּוּ יֵשׁ בּוֹ כַּמָּה מִילִין, אִם הֻקַּף לְדִירָה, כְּגוֹן מְדִינָה שֶׁמֻּקֶּפֶת חוֹמָה וְדַלְתוֹתֶיהָ נְעוּלוֹת בַּלַּיְלָה, וּמְבוֹאוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ כְּתָלִין וְלֶחִי בְּרוּחַ רְבִיעִית, וְכֵן תֵּל גָּבֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחָב אַרְבָּעָה, אוֹ חָרִיץ שֶׁהוּא עָמֹק עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחָב אַרְבָּעָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ כֵּלִים, כְּגוֹן סְפִינָה וּמִגְדָּל שֶׁל עֵץ וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶם, אִם יֵשׁ בָּהֶם אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה בְּגֹבַהּ עֲשָׂרָה, כָּל אֵלּוּ רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הֵן. וַאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד כִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד עַד לָרָקִיעַ. וָעֳבִי הַכְּתָלִים שֶׁל רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד נִדּוֹן כִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד. וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הוּא כְּגוֹן הַשְּׁוָקִים וְהָרְחוֹבוֹת, הַמִּדְבָּרוֹת וְהַדְּרָכִים הַמְפֻלָּשִׁים לָהֶם, וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה רֹחַב הַדֶּרֶךְ שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וְלֹא יִהְיֶה עָלָיו תִּקְרָה, וְאִיכָּא לְמַאן דְּאָמַר שֶׁצָּרִיךְ נַמִּי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בּוֹקְעִים בָּהֶם שִׁשִּׁים רִבּוֹא בְּכָל יוֹם כְּדִגְלֵי מִדְבָּר. וַאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אֵינוֹ כִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אֶלָּא עַד עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, וּלְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר הוּא. וּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה וְגָבֹהַּ מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים וּלְמַעְלָה הוּא מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר, אֲפִלּוּ קוֹצִים וּבַרְקָנִים וּגְלָלִין בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים גְּבוֹהִים מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וּלְמַעְלָה וְאֵין בָּהֶם אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה הֲרֵי הֵן מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר, וְכֵן מָקוֹם הַמֻּקָּף מְחִצּוֹת וְאֵין בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה, אוֹ חָרִיץ שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה וְעָמְקוֹ מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד הַתְּהוֹם הֲרֵי זֶה מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר. וּמָקוֹם שֶׁהֻקַּף אַרְבַּע מְחִצּוֹת גָּבְהָן מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד עֲשָׂרָה וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה אוֹ יוֹתֵר, אוֹ תֵּל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה אוֹ יוֹתֵר, גָּבֹהַּ מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד עֲשָׂרָה, אוֹ חָרִיץ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה, עָמֹק מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד עֲשָׂרָה, וּמָבוֹי סָתוּם מִשָּׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹתָיו שֶׁאֵין לוֹ לֶחִי אוֹ קוֹרָה בְּרוּחַ רְבִיעִית, וְהַיָּם, וְהַבִּקְעָה, כָּל אֵלּוּ כַּרְמְלִית הֵן. וּפֵרוּשׁ כַּרְמְלִית, כְּאַרְמְלִית, כְּלוֹמַר כְּמוֹ אַלְמָנָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ בְּתוּלָה וְלֹא נְשׂוּאָה לְבַעַל, כָּךְ רְשׁוּת זוֹ אֵינָהּ לֹא רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְלֹא רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וַאֲוִיר כַּרְמְלִית כְּכַרְמְלִית עַד עֲשָׂרָה, וּמֵעֲשָׂרָה וּלְמַעְלָה בְּכַרְמְלִית הֲוֵי מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר. וְהַמּוֹצִיא מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, אוֹ הַמַּכְנִיס מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, חַיָּב חַטָּאת. מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לְכַרְמְלִית, אוֹ מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְכַרְמְלִית, וּמִכַּרְמְלִית לִשְׁתֵּיהֶן, פָּטוּר אֲבָל אָסוּר. מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד אוֹ מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לִמְקוֹם פְּטוֹר אוֹ מִמְּקוֹם פְּטוֹר לִשְׁנֵיהֶן, מֻתָּר לְכַתְּחִלָּה, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר מִכַּרְמְלִית לִמְקוֹם פְּטוֹר, אוֹ מִמְּקוֹם פְּטוֹר לְכַרְמְלִית, שֶׁהוּא מֻתָּר. וְהַמַּעֲבִיר מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, חַיָּב חַטָּאת. בְּכַרְמְלִית, פָּטוּר אֲבָל אָסוּר. בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וּבִמְקוֹם פְּטוֹר, מֻתָּר לְכַתְּחִלָּה לְטַלְטֵל וּלְהַעֲבִיר בְּכָל הָרְשׁוּת כֻּלּוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא כַּמָּה מִילִין:


הזורק מרשות היחיד לרשות הרבים – the private/individual domain is a place that is surrounded by four partitions ten handbreadths high and between them are four handbreadths by four handbreadths or more, even if there are several miles, if they surrounded for living such as a district/province/large town which is surrounded by a wall and its doors are locked at night and their alleys have three walls and a stake fastened in the ground by the side of a wall, serving as a mark or as fictitious partition/enclosure for the purposes of enabling those who live there to move objects on the Sabbath in the fourth direction, and similarly a large hill that is ten [handbreadths] high and four [handbreadths] wide or an incision/trench that is ten [handbreadths] deep and four [handbreadths] wide and even utensils such as a boat or a tower of wood and things like them, if they have [a circumference of four by four at a height of ten , all of these are the public domain. And the airspace of the private domain is like the private domain up to the sky. And the thickness of the walls of the private domain are judged as the private domain. But the public domain is like the marketplaces and the desert streets and the roads open to them and it should be that the width of the path is sixteen cubits and there should not be a ceiling upon it. And there is one who holds that it also requires that six-hundred thousand people pass through it every day like the marches of the Israelites in the desert (see Tractate Shabbat 98a). But the airspace of the public domain is not like the public domain other than up to ten handbreadths for above from ten [handbreadths] is free/exempt. But a place that lacks four by four [handbreadths] and is or higher than three [handbreadths] is the exempt/free space and even thorns and thistles and excrement in the public domain that are at least three [handbreadths] or higher but lack four by four [handbreadths] are considered an exempt place. But a place that is surrounded by four partitions whose height is from three [handbreadths] to ten and it has four by four [handbreadths] or more, or a hill/mound that has four by four [handbreadths] or more which is higher than three [handbreadths] up to ten or a ditch which has four by four [handbreadths] and is deep from three until ten [handbreadths] or an alley that is closed from three directions and lacks a mark as a fictitious partition/enclosure or a beam on the fourth direction, and the sea and the valley – all of these are a Karmelit. The explanation of a Karmelit (a marked-off plot in a public thoroughfare – which cannot be considered either private or public domain) is like a “widow” – that is to say, like a widow who is not a virgin, nor married to a husband. Such is this domain which is neither a private domain nor a public domain and the airspace of a Karmelit is like a Karmelit up until ten [handbreadths] and above that, it is considered an exempt space. A person who removes something from the private domain to the public domain or brings something in from the public domain to the private domain is liable for a sin-offering (see Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1). From a private domain to a Karmelite and/or from a public domain to a Karmelit, or from a Karmelite to either of them is exempt but it is forbidden. [A person who brings something] from a private domain or from a public domain to an exempt space or from the exempt space to both of them -it is ab initio permitted, and we don’t have to say, from a Karmelit to an exempt space and from an exempt space to a Karmelit it is permitted. But a person who transfers an object from the beginning of the four cubits to the end of the four cubits in the public domain is liable for a sin-offering. In a Karmelit, he is exempt, but it is forbidden. In the private domain and in the exempt place, it is permissible ab initio to carry and transfer throughout the entire domain and even if it several miles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:1:2)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא מְחַיֵּב.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ מִכִּי עָבַר הַחֵפֶץ בַּאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בְּתוֹךְ עֲשָׂרָה הֲרֵי הוּא כְּאִלּוּ נָח. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי קְלוּטָה בְּתוֹךְ עֲשָׂרָה בַּאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לָאו כְּמִי שֶׁהֻנְחָה דָּמְיָא. אֲבָל לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּפָטוּר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ר"ע מחייב – for he holds that since the object has passed in the airspace of the public domain, within ten [handbreadths], it is as if it has come to rest; but the Rabbis hold that something absorbed in the midst of the ten [handbreadths] of airspace of the public domain is not like someone who placed it there, but above from ten [handbreadths] it is an exempt space, and no disputes that he is exempt, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:1)


**כֵּיצַד.** רַבָּנָן קָאָמְרֵי לֵיהּ:


כיצד – the Rabbis mention this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:2)


**שְׁתֵּי גְּזֻזְטְרָאוֹת.** וְהֵן נְסָרִים יוֹצְאִים מִכֹּתֶל הָעֲלִיָּה וְלַחוּץ עַל רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְהַגְּזוּזְטְרָאוֹת עַצְמָן הֵן רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וּכְשֶׁהֵן זוֹ כְּנֶגֶד זוֹ בִּשְׁנֵי צִדֵּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הַמּוֹשִׁיט וְהַזּוֹרֵק מִזּוֹ לְזוֹ פָטוּר, שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ זְרִיקָה וְהוֹשָׁטָה בִּמְלֶאכֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְרֹחַב רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַפְסִיק בֵּינֵיהֶן:


שתי גזוזאראות – these are boards that go out from the wall of the upper story and outward on the public domain, but the balconies themselves are the private domain, and when they are opposite each other on the two sides of the public domain, the individual who stretches out [his hand] and throws from one to the other is exempt, for we do not find throwing and stretching out in the [forbidden] labors of the Tabernacle from the [one] private domain to the [other] private domain and the expanse of the public domain interrupts between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:3)


**הָיוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן בִּדְיוֹטָא אַחַת.** כְּלוֹמַר בָּעֲלִיָּה אַחַת בְּאֹרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְיֵשׁ הֶפְסֵק רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בֵּינֵיהֶן:


היו שתיהן בדיוטא אחת – that is to say, one floor along the length of the private domain and there is an interruption of the public domain between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:4)


**הַזּוֹרֵק פָּטוּר.** הוֹאִיל וּלְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה הוּא, וְלֹא מָצִינוּ זְרִיקָה מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דֶּרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בַּמִּשְׁכָּן, וְהַמּוֹשִׁיט חַיָּב, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמּוֹשִׁיט לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה, שֶׁמָּצִינוּ הוֹשָׁטָה כַּיּוֹצֵא בָּהּ בַּמִּשְׁכָּן מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְאֹרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַפְסִיק בֵּינֵיהֶן:


הזורק פטור – for since he is higher than ten [handbreadths] and we dd not find throwing from one private domain to [another] private domain via the public domain in the Tabernacle but the person who stretches [his hand] is liable, and he though he stretches higher than ten [handbreadths], for we found that stretching forth and similar to it in the Tabernacle from one private domain to [another] private domain and the length of the public domain interrupts between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:5)


**שֶׁכֵּן הָיְתָה עֲבוֹדַת הַלְוִיִּם.** מוֹשִׁיטִים מִזֶּה לָזֶה לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה וְאֹרֶךְ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בָּאֶמְצַע, כֵּיצַד, [שְׁתֵּי] עֲגָלוֹת זוֹ אַחַר זוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּפוֹרְקֵי הַמִּשְׁכָּן הָיוּ מוֹשִׁיטִין לְאוֹתָם שֶׁעַל הָעֲגָלוֹת שֶׁאֶצְלָן הֵן סְמוּכוֹת וּקְרוֹבוֹת, וְהֵן מוֹשִׁיטִין לְאֵלּוּ שֶׁלִּפְנֵיהֶם, וְכָל אַחַת מֵהָעֲגָלוֹת רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הֵן:


שכן היתה עבודת הלוים – stretching forth from this [wagon] to that [wagon] above ten [handbreadths] and the length of the pubic domain in the middle. How so? [Two] wagons one after another in the public domain and those who unload the Tabernacle would stretch forth to those that were on the wagons that were next to them, nearby and close and they would stretch forth to those who were in front of them and everyone from the wagons were in the private domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא זוֹרְקִים.** שֶׁאֵין הַקְּרָשִׁים נִזְרָקִין מִפְּנֵי כָּבְדָּן:


אבל לא זורקים – for the boards were not thrown because of their heaviness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:7)


**חֻלְיַת הַבּוֹר.** עָפָר שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין מֵחֲפִירַת הַבּוֹר רְגִילִין לִתֵּן סְבִיבוֹת פִּי הַבּוֹר כְּמִין חוֹמָה לְהֶקֵּף, וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דְּבוֹר וְחֻלְיָתוֹ מִצְטָרְפִין לַעֲשָׂרָה, וְתָנֵי בּוֹר לְעָמְקוֹ וְתָנֵי סֶלַע לְגָבְהוֹ:


חוליית הבור – dirt that we remove from the digging of pit, it was customary to place around the opening of the pit like a wall and we derive that the pit and its segment of earth cut out in digging a pit and piled up on its borders combine to ten [handbreadths] and we teach that the pit to its depth and we teach a rock to its height.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:2:8)


**הַנּוֹטֵל מֵהֶן.** וּמֵנִיחַ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, אוֹ הַנּוֹטֵל מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְנוֹתֵן עַל גַּבָּן, חַיָּב:


הנוטל מהן – [a person who takes from them] and places in the public domain or wh takes from the public domain and places upon them - is liable [a sin-offering].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵק אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בַּכֹּתֶל.** מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע וְנָח בַּכֹּתֶל הַסָּמוּךְ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיְתָה דְּבֵלָה שְׁמֵנָה וְנִדְבְּקָה בַּכֹּתֶל לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה בַּאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, פָּטוּר, דְּכָל לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְקוֹם פְּטוֹר הוּא:


הזורק ד' אמות בכותל – from the beginning of the four cubits to the end of the four [cubits] and it came to rest on the wall that is close to the public domain, higher than ten handbreadths, such as a case where there was a thick cake of pressed figs that was attached to the wall higher than ten handbreadths in the public domain, it is an exempt space.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:2)


**לְמַטָּה מֵעֲשָׂרָה כְּזוֹרֵק בָּאָרֶץ.** מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְחַיָּב אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מִמְּקוֹם עֲקִירַת הַחֵפֶץ לַכֹּתֶל אֶלָּא אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת מְצֻמְצָמוֹת, לֹא אָמְרִינַן דְּעֹבִי הַדְּבֵלָה מְמַעֵט בְּאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת וְאֵין כָּאן מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְבַטֵּל אֶת הַדְּבֵלָה בַּכֹּתֶל אֵין עָבְיָהּ מְמַעֵט כְּלוּם:


למטה מעשרה כזורק בארץ – from the beginning of the four [cubits] to the end of four [cubits] in the public domain and he is liable, even though there is not from the place of the uprooting of the object to the wall other than four cubits exactly, we did not state the thickness of the cake of pressed figs is excluded from the four cubits and we don’t have here from the beginning of the four [cubits] to the end of the four [cubits] , for it does not exclude the pressed figs on the wall, its thickness does not exclude anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:3)


**וְנִתְגַּלְגֵּל חוּץ לְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת פָּטוּר.** שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נִתְכַּוֵּן לִזְרִיקָה שֶׁל חִיּוּב:


ונתגלגל חוץ לד"א פטור – for he did not intend for a throwing of liability.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:3:4)


**וְנִתְגַּלְגֵּל לְתוֹךְ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת חַיָּב.** וְהוּא דְּנָח מַשֶּׁהוּ חוּץ לְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּתְגַּלְגֵּל לִפְנִים:


ונתגלגל לתוך ד"א חייב – and it came to rest a bit outside of the four cubits prior to rolling inwards.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵק בַּיָּם.** מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע פָּטוּר, דְּכַרְמְלִית הוּא:


הזורק בים – from the beginning of four [cubits] to the end of four [cubits], he is exempt, for it is a Karmelit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:2)


**רְקָק מַיִם.** שֶׁאֵינָם גְּבוֹהִים מִן הָאָרֶץ וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶם רֶפֶשׁ וָטִיט נִקְרָאִים רְקָק:


רקק מים – which are not higher from the land, and there is in them mud and clay/plaster and they are called a [shallow] pool/diluvium.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:3)


**וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְהַלֶּכֶת בּוֹ.** שֶׁרַבִּים מְהַלְּכִים בּוֹ:


ור"ה מהלכת בו – for many [people] walk through it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:4)


**וְכַמָּה הוּא רְקָק.** כַּמָּה הוּא עָמְקוֹ דְּנֵימָא אַכַּתִּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הוּא וְלֹא נַעֲשָׂה כַּרְמְלִית:


וכמה הוא רקק – what is its depth that we can say that it is yet the public domain and it has not become a Karmelit?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:4:5)


**וּרְקָק מַיִם וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְהַלֶּכֶת בּוֹ.** הַאי דְּכַפְלֵיהּ תַּנָּא לְמִלְּתֵיהּ לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דַּאֲפִלּוּ רְקָק רָחָב אַרְבָּעָה הוֹאִיל וְהוּא פָּחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה נִדּוֹן כִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְכָפַל נַמִּי וּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מְהַלֶּכֶת בּוֹ, לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין מְהַלְּכִים בּוֹ רַבִּים אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי הַדְּחַק, הִלּוּךְ עַל יְדֵי הַדְּחַק שְׁמֵיהּ הִלּוּךְ:


ורקק מים ורה"ר מהלכת בו – this is double that was taught for the matter to teach us that even if the pool was four [cubits] wide, for since it was less than ten [cubits] high, it is judged like the public domain, and double also with a public domain going through it to teach us that even though many people don’t walk through it other than in an emergency/need, walking even in an emergency is called walking.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:1)


**מִן הַיָּם לַיַּבָּשָׁה.** מִכַּרְמְלִית לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


מן הים ליבשה – from a Karmelit to the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:2)


**מִן הַיָּם לַסְּפִינָה.** מִכַּרְמְלִית לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד:


מן הים לספינה – from a Karmelit to the private domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:3)


**מְטַלְטְלִין מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ.** אִם הֵן שֶׁל שְׁנֵי בְּנֵי אָדָם מְטַלְטְלִים עַל יְדֵי עֵרוּב דַּהֲוֵי לְהוּ כִּשְׁתֵּי חֲצֵרוֹת:


מטלטלין מזו לזו – if they belong to two individuals, one carries via an Eruv, for it is likened to two separate courtyards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:4)


**מֻקָּפוֹת.** סְמוּכוֹת זוֹ לְזוֹ, כְּמוֹ אֵין מַקִּיפִין בְּבוּעֵי (חלין מ"ו ע"ב):


מוקפות – each is near the other like we don’t surround swellings/abcesses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:5:5)


**אֵין מְטַלְטְלִין מִזּוֹ לָזוֹ.** דְּכִי מַפְרְשִׁי מֵהֲדָדֵי, כַּרְמְלִית מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּינֵיהֶן וּבָטֵל הָעֵרוּב:


אין מטלטלין – for this is how they explain each other: the Karmelit interrupts between them and nullifies the Eruv.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:6:1)


**הַזּוֹרֵק.** בְּשַׁבָּת בְּשׁוֹגֵג, וְנִזְכָּר שֶׁהוּא שַׁבָּת לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּצָאת הָאֶבֶן מִתַּחַת יָדוֹ קֹדֶם שֶׁתָּנוּחַ, אֲפִלּוּ לֹא קְלָטָהּ אַחֵר אֶלָּא שֶׁנָּחָה כְּדַרְכָּהּ פָּטוּר, דְּהָכִי תְּנַן לְקַמָּן עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא תְּחִלָּתָן וְסוֹפָן בִּשְׁגָגָה, וְהַאי תְּחִלָּתוֹ בִּשְׁגָגָה וְסוֹפוֹ מֵזִיד, הוֹאִיל וְנִזְכַּר שֶׁהוּא שַׁבָּת קֹדֶם שֶׁתָּנוּחַ. וּמַתְנִיתִין הָכִי מִפָרְשָׁא, הַזּוֹרֵק וְנִזְכַּר לְאַחַר שֶׁיָּצְאָה מִתַּחַת יָדוֹ, אִי נַמִּי לֹא נִזְכַּר אֶלָּא שֶׁקְּלָטָהּ אַחֵר וְכוּ'‏ פָּטוּר, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ שְׁנַיִם שֶׁעֲשָׂאוּהָ פְּטוּרִים, הָא נָחָה חַיָּב חַטָּאת, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים כְּשֶׁחָזַר וְשָׁכַח, אֲבָל לֹא חָזַר וְשָׁכַח פָּטוּר, שֶׁכָּל חַיָּבֵי וְכוּ':


הזורק – [he throws] on the Sabbath inadvertently and is reminded that it is Shabbat after the stone left from under his hand before it came to rest, even if another did not receive/intercept it, but rather it came to rest in a natural manner, he is exempt, for such is taught further on [in our Mishnah] : until its beginning and its end are inadvertent,” for this, its beginning is inadvertent and its end is on purpose/wanton, for since he was reminded that it was Shabbat prior to it coming to a rest, and our Mishnah, this is how it to be explained: A person who throws and then is reminded [that it is Shabbat] after it left from under his hand; alternatively, he was not reminded until the other [person] intercepted/received it, etc., for this is a case where two who performed this act, they are exempt; for if it came to rest, he Is liable to a sin offering in the four cubits when he returned and forgot, but if he didn’t return and forgot, he is exempt, for all those liable for a sin offering, etc.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/11:6:2)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי נַמִּי מַעֲבִיר חֵפֶץ מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם, שֶׁאִם עָקַר בִּשְׁגָגָה וְנִזְכַּר שֶׁהוּא שַׁבָּת קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּנִיחַ, פָּטוּר:


זה הכלל – which includes also transferring an object form place to place – for if he uprooted it inadvertently and was reminded that it was Shabbat prior his putting it down, he is exempt.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:1)


**הַבּוֹנֶה.** שֶׁאָמְרוּ בַּאֲבוֹת מְלָאכוֹת:


הבונה – that they mentioned in the [thirty-nine] chief labors –


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:2)


**כַּמָּה יִבְנֶה וְיִהְיֶה חַיָּב.** כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


how much can one build and be liable? The smallest quantity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:3)


**מְסַתֵּת.** מְרַבֵּעַ אֶת הָאֶבֶן אוֹ מַחְלִיקָהּ וּמְתַקְּנָהּ, הַכֹּל לְפִי מִנְהַג הַמָּקוֹם. וְהִיא תּוֹלָדָה דְּמַכֶּה בַּפַּטִּישׁ:


מסתת – to make the stone quadrangular or to level it/smooth it, everything is according to the custom of the place, and this is a derivative of he who gives the final stroke with the hammer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:4)


**וְהַמַּכֶּה בַפַּטִּישׁ.** הִיא גְּמַר מְלָאכָה שֶׁל חוֹצְבֵי אֶבֶן, לְאַחַר שֶׁחָצַב הָאֶבֶן מִן הָהָר סָבִיב וְהִבְדִּילָהּ מַכֶּה עָלֶיהָ בַּפַּטִּישׁ מַכָּה גְּדוֹלָה וְהִיא מִתְפָּרֶקֶת וְנוֹפֶלֶת. וְכָל הַגּוֹמֵר מְלָאכָה בְּשַׁבָּת תּוֹלָדָה דְּמַכֶּה בַּפַּטִּישׁ הִיא:


והמכה בפטיש – this is the conclusion of work of those who cut stones, for after he cuts stones from the mountain surrounding him and he has severed it, he strikes a large blow with his hammer and it breaks apart and falls, and all who complete [forbidden] work in Shabbat, it is a derivative of striking the final hammer blow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:5)


**מַעֲצָד.** קוֹפִיץ קָטָן:


מעצד – a small, curved cutting tool/hatchet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:6)


**וְהַקּוֹדֵחַ.** הַנּוֹקֵב:


הקודח – makes a perforation/hole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:7)


**כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** אַכֻּלְּהוּ קָאֵי, אַמְסַתֵּת וְאַמַּכֶּה בַּפַּטִּישׁ וּבַמַּעֲצָד, וְהַקּוֹדֵחַ:


כל שהוא – it refers to all of them – to the one who chisels, and to the one who strikes the final hammer blow and the adze and the one who perforates/digs out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 12:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/12:1:8)


**וּמְלַאכְתּוֹ מִתְקַיֶּמֶת.** שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לְהוֹסִיף עָלֶיהָ:


now that you've done what you did you don't need to any anything to it
