[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:1:3)


**וְאֵינָן דּוֹמִין לְדַלְתוֹת הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ נִתְפָּרְקוּ בְּשַׁבָּת אֵין נִטָּלִין, לְפִי שֶׁדַּלְתוֹת הַבַּיִת אֵינָן מִן הַמּוּכָן, כְּלוֹמַר לֹא נַעֲשׂוּ לְטִלְטוּל:


ואינן דומין לדלתות הבית – and even if they were detached on the Sabbath, they are not taken because the doors of the house are not fashioned as such, that is to say, they were not made to be carried.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:1)


**קֻרְנָס.** פַּטִּישׁ:


קורנס – hammer


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:2)


**הַדְּבֵלָה.** לְאַחַר שֶׁעֲשָׂאָהּ עִגּוּל הִיא עָבָה וְקָשָׁה וּצְרִיכָה קֻרְדֹּם לְחָתְכָהּ:


הדבלה – for after it was made round, it is thick and hard and requires a hatchet to cut it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:3)


**מְגֵרָה.** כְּעֵין סַכִּין אָרֹךְ וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ פְּגִימוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


מגרה – a kind of long file which has many notches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:4)


**לִגְרֹר בּוֹ אֶת הַגְּבִינָה.** לְחָתְכָהּ וּלְחַלְּקָהּ לַחֲלָקִים, שֶׁעַל יְדֵי פְּגִימוֹתָיו מְמַהֵר וְחוֹתֵךְ הַדָּבָר הֶעָב:


לגרר בו את הגבינה – to cut it and divide it into pieces through its notches, he quickly cuts the thick thing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:5)


**מַגְרֵפָה.** לִגְרֹף בָּהּ הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת מִן הֶחָבִית:


מגריפה – to grab with it the dried figs from the barrel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:6)


**רַחַת.** לוּחַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ בֵּית יָד וּשְׁתֵּי דְּפָנוֹת וְזוֹרִין בָּהּ אֶת הַחִטָּה:


רחת – a board that has a handle and two walls and they winnow the wheat with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:7)


**וּמַזְלֵג.** כְּלִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ שִׁנַּיִם וּמְהַפְּכִים בּוֹ אֶת הַקַּשׁ בַּגֹּרֶן:


ומזלג – a utensil that has three teeth and they turn over the straw in the granary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:8)


**כּוּשׁ.** פֶּלֶךְ שֶׁטּוֹוֹת בּוֹ הַנָּשִׁים:


פלך – a spindle that the women spins with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:9)


**כַּרְכָּר.** עֵץ שֶׁל אוֹרְגִים וְדוֹמֶה לְמַחַט שֶׁל סַקָּאִין:


כרכר – wood of weavers and it is similar to the needle of sack-makers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:10)


**לִתְחֹב בּוֹ.** לֶאֱכֹל בּוֹ תּוּתִים וְכָל מִינֵי פְּרִי רַךְ:


לתחוב בו – to consume with it mulberries and all kinds of soft fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:11)


**מַחַט שֶׁל יָד.** מַחַט קְטַנָּה שֶׁתּוֹפְרִים בָּהּ בְּגָדִים:


מחט של יד – a small needle used to sew clothing with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:12)


**לִטֹּל בּוֹ אֶת הַקּוֹץ.** שֶׁנִּכְנַס בִּבְשָׂרוֹ, שֶׁמֻּתָּר לִטְּלוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמֻּתָּר לְהָפִיס מוּרְסָא לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנָּה לֵחָה, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ פֶּה:


ליטול בו את הקוץ – that entered into his skin, it is permitted to take it [out] on the Sabbath just like it is permissible to penetrate an abscess to remove its moistness, as long as he doesn’t intend to make of it a mouth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:13)


**וְשֶׁל סַקָּאִים.** מַחַט גָּדוֹל שֶׁתּוֹפְרִין בָּהּ שַׂקִּין:


ושל סקאים – a large needle that they sew sacks with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:2:14)


**לִפְתֹּחַ בּוֹ אֶת הַדֶּלֶת.** מִי שֶׁאָבְדָה לוֹ מַפְתֵּחַ:


לפתוח בו את הדלת – for whomever has lost his key.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:1)


**קָנֶה שֶׁל זֵיתִים.** קָנֶה שֶׁעָשׂוּי לִבְדֹּק הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁבַּמַּעֲטָן אִם נִתְאַסֵּף שַׁמְנָן בְּתוֹכוֹ וְהִגִּיעַ לִהְיוֹת רְאוּיִין לְבֵית הַבַּד:


קנה של זיתים – a reed that is made to check the olives that are in the pit or vat where olives are packed until they form a viscid mass, if their oil was gathered in them and that they were worthy of the olive press.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:2)


**אִם יֵשׁ קֶשֶׁר בְּרֹאשׁוֹ.** כְּעֵין פְּקָק שֶׁבַּקָּנֶה, מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, לְפִי שֶׁעַל יְדֵי שֶׁהוּא פָּקוּק בְּקֶשֶׁר מִשְׁתַּיֵּר בּוֹ מִן הַשֶּׁמֶן הַזָּב מִן הַזֵּיתִים, וּבוֹדֵק בּוֹ אִם הִגִּיעַ לְעִצּוּר, וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ בֵּית קִבּוּל:


אם יש קשר בראשו – a sort of stopper that is in the reed is susceptible for ritual impurity because it is stopped up at the knot, it is left over in it from the oil that flows from the olives and he checks it if it arrived to stopping and it is a receptacle for him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:3)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ קֶשֶׁר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא חָלוּל אֵין חֲלָלוֹ עָשׂוּי לְקַבֵּל כְּלוּם, הִלְכָּךְ הֲוָה לֵיהּ פְּשׁוּטֵי כְּלֵי עֵץ וְאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה:


ואם לאו – if it has no knot, even though it is hollowed, he did not hollow it out to receive anything. Therefore, he has a flat wooden implement (see Talmud Shabbat 123b) and is not susceptible for ritual impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:3:4)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ נִטָּל בְּשַׁבָּת.** דְּהָא כְּלִי הוּא וְעָשׂוּי לַהֲפֹךְ בּוֹ אֶת הַזֵּיתִים:


בין כך ובין כך ניטל בשבת – for it is an implement and it is used to turn over the olives.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:1)


**מַסָּר הַגָּדוֹל.** מְגֵרָה גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁנּוֹסְרִים בָּהּ קוֹרוֹת:


מסר גדול - a large saw that we saw boards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:2)


**וְיָתֵד שֶׁל מַחֲרֵשָׁה.** כְּלִי גָּדוֹל הֶעָשׂוּי כְּסַכִּין שֶׁבּוֹ עוֹשִׂין חָרִיץ שֶׁל תֶּלֶם הַמַּעֲנָה, וְהָנָךְ קָפֵיד עֲלַיְהוּ וּמְיַחֵד לְהוּ מָקוֹם, דְּלֹא חָזוּ לִמְלָאכָה אַחֲרִיתִי:


ויתד של מחרישה – a large utensil made like a knife with which they make a incision of a turn of the plough/furrow’s length and they are strict with him and designated for them a place that is not worthy for any other labor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:3)


**כָּל הַכֵּלִים נִטָּלִים לְצֹרֶךְ וְשֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כָּל הַכֵּלִים שֶׁמְּלַאכְתָּן לְהֶתֵּר, כְּגוֹן קְעָרוֹת וְכוֹסוֹת, נִטָּלִים לְצֹרֶךְ גּוּפוֹ וּלְצֹרֶךְ מְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל כְּלִי, וְשֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ, אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְגוּפוֹ וְלִמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל כְּלִי אֶלָּא לְטַלְטְלוֹ מֵחַמָּה לַצֵּל אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִגְנְבוּהוּ הַגַּנָּבִים, שָׁרֵי בִּכְלִי שֶׁמְּלַאכְתּוֹ לְהֶתֵּר. וּכְלִי שֶׁמְּלַאכְתּוֹ לְאִסּוּר, כְּגוֹן מְדוּכוֹת וְרֵחַיִם וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶן, לְצֹרֶךְ גּוּפוֹ וּלְצֹרֶךְ מְקוֹמוֹ מֻתָּר, מֵחַמָּה לַצֵּל אוֹ מִפְּנֵי הַגַּנָּבִים אָסוּר:


כל הכלים ניטלים לצורך ושלא לצורך – this is how it should be understood: All the utensils whose labor is for permitted things such as plates and cups which are removed [on the Sabbath] for the necessity of its own body as well as for the necessity of the place of the utensil, as well as that which is not necessary, even if he doesn’t need the implement nor the place of the utensil, other than to carry it on account of the shade or that thieves should not steal it, it is permitted with a utensil that whose labor is something permitted. But a utensil that is used for some kind of prohibited labor, such as mortars and millstone and similar things for its own necessity and for the needs of its place, it is permitted, but from the sun to the shade or because of thieves, it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:4:4)


**רַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה אוֹמֵר אֵין נִטָּלִים אֶלָּא לְצֹרֶךְ.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא לְצֹרֶךְ תַּשְׁמִישׁ הַמְיֻחָד לוֹ בִּלְבַד, וְלֹא לְצֹרֶךְ דָּבָר אַחֵר, וַאֲפִלּוּ לְצֹרֶךְ גּוּפוֹ, כְּגוֹן סַכִּין לַחְתֹּךְ בּוֹ בִּלְבַד שָׁרֵי, וְלֹא לִסְמֹךְ בּוֹ אֶת הַקְּעָרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי נְחֶמְיָה:


רבי נחמיה אומר אין ניטלין אלא לצורך – It is explained in the Talmud (Talmud Shabbat 124b) for the needs of its unique use alone but not for the needs of something else, even for its own needs, such as a knife to cut with alone, it is permitted, and not to support with it the bowl. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Nehemiah.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:5:1)


**מֵעֵין מְלָאכָה.** אֵיזוֹ מְלָאכָה שֶׁתִּהְיֶה, וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵינָהּ מֵעֵין מְלַאכְתָּן הָרִאשׁוֹנָה:


מעין מלאכה – some kind of work that will be and even if it is not the kind of the first work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:5:2)


**לִצּוֹק לְתוֹכָן מִקְפָּה.** עָבָה, דּוּמְיָא דְּעִסָּה מְעֹרֶבֶת בְּמַיִם. וְלֹא פְלִיגֵי אֶלָּא בְּנִשְׁבְּרוּ בְּשַׁבָּת, אֲבָל נִשְׁבְּרוּ מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא מוֹדִים דְּנִטָּלִים אֲפִלּוּ אֵין עוֹשִׂין מֵעֵין מְלַאכְתָּן הָרִאשׁוֹנָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


לצוק לתוכן מקפה – thick [porridge] that is similar to started dough that is mixed in water and they do not dispute other than if they were broken on Shabbat, but if they were broken on the Eve of the Sabbath, everyone agrees that they are removed, even if they don’t do a kind of the first kind of work. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:1)


**הָאֶבֶן שֶׁבְּקֵרוּיָה.** דְּלַעַת יְבֵשָׁה וּמְמַלְּאִים בָּהּ מַיִם, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהִיא קַלָּה אֵינָהּ שׁוֹאֶבֶת אֶלָּא צָפָה, וְנוֹתְנִין בָּהּ אֶבֶן לְהַכְבִּידָהּ:


האבן שבקרויה – a dry gourd and we fill it with water and because it is light, it is not drawing water but rather it floats and we put a stone to make it heavy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:2)


**אִם מְמַלְּאִין.** בַּקֵּרוּיָה:


אם ממלאין – the pumpkin-shell


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:3)


**וְאֵין הָאֶבֶן נוֹפֶלֶת.** שֶׁהִדְּקוּהָ יָפֶה בְּפִי הַקֵּרוּיָה, הֲוָה כְּלִי:


ואין האבן נופלת - for the pounded stone fits well in the mouth of the pumpkin shell and it is a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:4)


**וְאִם לָאו.** הֲרֵי הִיא כִּשְׁאָר אֲבָנִים וְאֵין מְטַלְטְלִים אֶת הַקֵּרוּיָה, שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית בָּסִיס לָאֶבֶן שֶׁנּוֹשָׂאתָהּ:


ואם לאו – then it is like other stones, and we don’t carry the pumpkin shell that had become the base for the stone that carries it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:6:5)


**זְמוֹרָה שֶׁהִיא קְשׁוּרָה לְטָפִיחַ.** לְפַךְ קָטָן שֶׁשּׁוֹאֲבִין בּוֹ מַיִם מִן הַבּוֹר אוֹ מִן הַמַּעְיָן, מְמַלְּאִים בּוֹ, דְּשַׁוְיָא לְהַךְ זְמוֹרָה כְּלִי:


זמורה שהיא קשורה בטפיח – to a small pitcher that we draw from it water from the well or from the spring from which we fill it for this branch is considered a utensil.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:1)


**פְּקַק הַחַלּוֹן.** כְּגוֹן לוּחַ וּמָסָךְ אוֹ שְׁאָר כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁסּוֹתְמִים בּוֹ הַחַלּוֹן:


פקק החלון – such as a tablet or a curtain or other things that they close a window with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:2)


**וְתָלוּי.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִגְרָר בָּאָרֶץ:


ותלוי – that is not dragged on the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:3)


**וְאִם לָאו אֵין פּוֹקְקִין בּוֹ.** שֶׁאִם נִגְרָר עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע כְּשֶׁהוּא שׁוֹמְטוֹ מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ לִסְתֹּם בּוֹ, מֵחֲזֵי כְּמוֹסִיף עַל הַבִּנְיָן, וְסָבַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אֵין מוֹסִיפִין עַל אֹהֶל עֲרַאי בְּשַׁבָּת:


ואם לאו אין פוקקין בו – for if it was dragged on the ground when it is loosened from the ground to close it, it appears like adding to the building, and Rabbi Eliezer holds that we don’t add to a temporary tent on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:7:4)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין קָשׁוּר בֵּין אֵינוֹ קָשׁוּר פּוֹקְקִין בּוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְהָיָה מוּכָן מֵאֶתְמוֹל לְכָךְ, דְּסָבְרֵי רַבָּנָן מוֹסִיפִין עַל אֹהֶל עֲרַאי בְּשַׁבָּת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


בין כך ובין כך – whether it is tied or not tied, we shutter it up with it, since it was prepared/ready from the day before, for this the Rabbis hold that we add on to a temporary tent on the Sabbath, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/17:8:1)


**בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בְּכִסּוּי קַרְקַע.** בַּגְּמָרָא קָאָמַר דִּבְכִסּוּי כֵּלִים כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּשָׁרֵי אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֵית לְהוּ בֵּית אֲחִיזָה, וּבְכִסּוּיֵי קַרְקָעוֹת כְּגוֹן כִּסּוּי שֶׁל בּוֹר וְדוּת כֻּלֵּי עָלְמָא לֹא פְלִיגֵי דְּאָסוּר אִי לֵית לְהוּ בֵּית אֲחִיזָה. כִּי פְלִיגֵי בְּכִסּוּיֵי כֵּלִים הַמְחֻבָּרִים לַקַּרְקָעוֹת, מַר סָבַר כְּקַרְקְעוֹת דָּמְיָן, וּמַר סָבַר לָאו כְּקַרְקְעוֹת דָּמְיָן. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים, שֶׁהַמְחֻבָּר לַקַּרְקַע הֲרֵי הוּא כְּקַרְקַע:


במה דברים אמורים – In the Talmud (Tractate Shabbat 126b), that regarding a lid of vessels, everyone agrees that it is permitted, even though it lacks a handle, and with ground openings, such as the covering for a pit and a cistern no one disputes that it is forbidden if it lacks a handle, but they argue with a handle of a utensil attached to the ground; one opinion holds that it is like the ground and the other opinion holds that is not like the ground; but the Halakha is according to the Sages that what is attached to the ground is like the ground.



## Chapter 18


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:1)


**מְפַנִּין.** אִם צָרִיךְ לִמְקוֹמָן לְהוֹשִׁיב שָׁם אוֹרְחִין לְהָסֵב בַּסְּעוּדָה אוֹ תַּלְמִידִים לִשְׁמֹעַ הַדְּרָשָׁה, וְדַוְקָא לִדְבַר מִצְוָה שָׁרֵי וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְטִירְחָא דְּשַׁבָּת:


מפנין – if one needs their space to sit guests to place divans around the table for a meal or students to hear the sermon, and specifically for a matter of a Mitzvah it is permitted and we don’t concern ourselves with the trouble of Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:2)


**אַרְבַּע וְחָמֵשׁ.** לָאו דַּוְקָא, דְּאִי בָּעֵי אֲפִלּוּ טוּבָא נַמִּי:


ארבע וחמש – not specifically, for if he needs even more also.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא אֶת הָאוֹצָר.** כְּלוֹמַר וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִגְמֹר אֶת הָאוֹצָר כֻּלּוֹ עַד שֶׁיַּגִּיעַ לְקַרְקָעִיתוֹ, דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַשְׁווֹיֵי גֻּמּוֹת:


אבל לא את האוצר – that is to say, only that he should no complete the entire hoarded up treasure until he reaches its bottom, for perhaps he will come to make holes/indentations.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:4)


**מְפַנִּין תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל דְּלֹא חַזְיָא לֵיהּ יוּכַל לְטַלְטְלָהּ וּלְפַנּוֹתָהּ, כֵּיוָן דְּחַזְיָא לְכֹהֵן:


מפנין תרומה טהורה – even an Israelite (i.e., non-Kohen) for whom it is not fit can carry it and clear it away because it is fit for a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:5)


**וּדְמַאי.** דְּחָזֵי לַעֲנִיִּים, כְּדִתְנָן מַאֲכִילִין אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים דְּמַאי, וְאִי בָּעֵי הֲוָה מַפְקִיר לְנִכְסֵיהּ וַהֲוֵי עָנִי וְחָזֵי לֵיהּ, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי חָזֵי לֵיהּ:


דמאי – because it is fit for the poor as is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Demai, Chapter 3, Mishnah 1): “We feed the poor and to quarter given to troops on march/transient poor men produce about which there is a suspicion as to whether or not the tithes were appropriately taken,” and if he requires it he would make ownerless his possessions and would be poor and be fit for it; now also the poor is fit for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:6)


**וּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁנִּטְּלָה תְרוּמָתוֹ.** שֶׁנִּטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וְלֹא נִטְּלָה מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִקְדִּים בֶּן לֵוִי וְלָקַח הַמַּעֲשֵׂר בְּשִׁבֳּלִין דַּעֲדַיִן לֹא חָלָה עָלֶיהָ חוֹבַת תְּרוּמָה, שֶׁאֵין הַדָּגָן חַיָּב בִּתְרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה עַד שֶׁיִּתְמָרֵחַ בַּכְּרִי, וְזֶה שֶׁהִקְדִּים וְלָקַח הַמַּעֲשֵׂר בְּשִׁבֳּלִין אֵינוֹ מַפְרִישׁ אֶלָּא תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר בִּלְבַד, וּמֻתָּר בַּאֲכִילָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הִפְרִישׁוּ מִמֶּנָּה תְּרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה:


ותרומת מעשר שניטלה תרומתו – the tithe of the tithe which the Levite owes to the Kohen that was taken from it but he did not take the Great Tithe (i.e., the 2% that goes from an Israelite to a Kohen) such as the case where a Levite anticipated first and took the [First] Tithe in the ears of corn but had not yet occurred the obligation of Terumah/priest’s due, for the grain is not liable for Terumah until it becomes shaped into an even pile, and he (i.e., the Levite) advanced and took the [First] Tithe of the ears of corn does not separate anything other than the tithe of the tithe which the Levite owes to the Kohen alone, and he is permitted to consume it even though the Great Tithe was not yet separated out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:7)


**וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ.** שֶׁפָּדוּ אוֹתָן הַבְּעָלִים וְנָתְנוּ אֶת הַקֶּרֶן וְלֹא נָתְנוּ אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ, וְשָׁמְעִינַן מֵהָכָא שֶׁהֵם פְּדוּיִין וְהַחֹמֶשׁ הֲוֵי מִלְוֶה גַּבֵּי בְּעָלִים:


ומעשר שני והקדש שנפדו – the owners redeemed them and gave the principal but did not give the [added] one-fifth, and we derive from here that they are redeemed and the one-fifth is a loan to the owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:8)


**אֲבָל לֹא אֶת הַטֶּבֶל.** וַאֲפִלּוּ טֶבֶל דְּרַבָּנָן, כְּגוֹן הַזָּרוּעַ בְּעָצִיץ שֶׁאֵינוֹ נָקוּב:


אבל לא את הטבל – and even eatables forbidden pending the separation of priestly gifts according to the Rabbis such as that which is sown in a pot without a hole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:9)


**וְלֹא אֶת מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְּלָה תְרוּמָתוֹ.** אִם הִקְדִּים בֶּן לֵוִי וְלָקַח הַמַּעֲשֵׂר אַחַר שֶׁנִּתְמָרְחָה הַתְּבוּאָה בַּכְּרִי קֹדֶם שֶׁהִפְרִישׁוּ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה, וְהִפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר וְלֹא הִפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה, זֶהוּ מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁלֹּא נִטְּלָה תְּרוּמָתוֹ הָאָמוּר כָּאן, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּתְמָרְחָה בַּכְּרִי וְנִתְחַיְּבָה בִּתְרוּמָה, כְּשֶׁלּוֹקֵחַ הַמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמַּפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא הִפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנּוּ תְּרוּמָה גְּדוֹלָה טֶבֶל הֲוֵי וְאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת:


ולא את מעשר ראשון שלא נטלה תרומתו – if the Levite anticipated first and he took the [First] Tithe after the grain had been shaped into an even pile before the Great Terumah/priest’s due had been separated from it (i.e., the Israelite’s requirement to give a Kohen two percent), and he (i.e., the Levite) separate the tithe of the tithe (which the Levite owes to the Kohen) but he didn’t separate the Great Terumah – this is First Tithe where Terumah had not been taken which is spoken about here, for since [the grain] had been shaped into an even pile and had become liable for Terumah/priest’s due, when he (i.e., the Levite) takes the First Tithe, even though he separates from it the tithe of the tithe, all the while that he did not separate from it the Great Terumah, it is considered eatables forbidden pending the separation of priestly gifts and it is prohibited to carry it on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:10)


**וְלֹא מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁלֹּא נִפְדּוּ.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ אֲבָל לֹא נִפְדּוּ כַּהֲלָכָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁפָּדָה הַמַּעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי עַל גְּרוּטָאוֹת שֶׁל כֶּסֶף, וְאֵין מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי נִפְדֶּה אֶלָּא בְּמָעוֹת שֶׁיֵּשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם צוּרָה דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) וְצַרְתָּ הַכֶּסֶף בְּיָדְךָ. וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ אֵינוֹ נִפְדֶּה בְּקַרְקַע דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְנָתַן אֶת הָעֶרְכְּךָ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא, דָּבָר הַנִּתָּן מִיָּד לְיָד:


ולא מעשר שני והקדש שלא נפדו – such as the case when they were redeemed, but not redeemed according to Halakha, such as when he redeemed the Second tithe out of fragments of vessels/broken ware, which is no longer a vessel or rubbish (see Tractate Shabbat 123a) and not Second Tithe which is redeemed with money that has upon them a shape/form, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:25): “[You may convert them into money.] Wrap up the money [and take it with you to the place the LORD your God has chosen].” But sanctified things are not redeemed with land, as it is written (Leviticus 27:23): “and he shall pay the assessment as of that day”- a thing that is given from hand to hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:11)


**לוּף.** מִין קִטְנִית שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי חַי אֲפִלּוּ לִבְהֵמָה. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ מִין מִמִּינֵי הַבְּצָלִים:


לוף – a kind of a legume which is not appropriate raw even for animals. But Maimonides explained it is a from the species of onions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:1:12)


**לְעוֹרְבִין.** כְּגוֹן עֲשִׁירִים שֶׁמְּגַדְּלִים עוֹרְבִים לִגְדֻלָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


לעורבין – like the rich people who raise ravens to adulthood, but the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamliel.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:1)


**חֲבִילֵי.** אֲגֻדּוֹת:


חבילי – bundles/bunches.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:2)


**זְרָדִים.** עַנְפֵי הָאִילָן לַחִים שֶׁמְּזָרְדִין אוֹתָם לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמוֹת:


זרדים – branches of the tree that are moist and we trim them for the food of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:3)


**כּוֹפִין אֶת הַסַּל לִפְנֵי הָאֶפְרוֹחִים.** וּמְבַטֵּל כְּלִי מֵהֵיכָנוֹ לֹא הֲוֵי, שֶׁאֵין הָאֶפְרוֹחִים עוֹמְדִים עָלָיו. וְהָאוֹמֵר אֵין כְּלִי נִטָּל אֶלָּא לְצֹרֶךְ דָּבָר הַנִּטָּל, מוֹקֵי לְמַתְנִיתִין בְּצָרִיךְ לִמְקוֹמוֹ שֶׁל כְּלִי:


כופין את הסל לפני האפרוחים – We disturb the utensil (i.e., turn it over from its place so that it is not [functioning] for the young chickens are not standing upon it and the individual who states that the utensil is not being taken other than for the need of the things for which it is taken, our Mishnah establishes that is necessary for the place of the utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:4)


**שֶׁבָּרְחָה.** מִן הַבַּיִת:


שברחה – from the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:5)


**דּוֹחִין אוֹתָהּ.** בַּיָּדַיִם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:6)


**עַד שֶׁתִּכָּנֵס.** וְדַוְקָא דּוֹחִין, אֲבָל לֹא מְדַדִּין, לְפִי שֶׁהַתַּרְנְגֹלֶת מַגְבַּהַת עַצְמָהּ מִן הָאָרֶץ וְנִמְצָא שֶׁהוּא מְטַלְטְלָהּ. אֲבָל אַוָּזִין וּשְׁאָר עוֹפוֹת מְדַדִּין:


עד שתכנס – and specifically drive it but not push and pull because the chicken lifts itself from the ground and it is found that he is carrying it but geese and other fowl one may lead them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:7)


**מְדַדִּין עֲגָלִים.** אוֹחֵז בְּצַוָּארוֹ וּבִצְדָדָיו וְגוֹרְרוֹ וּמַסִּיעוֹ וּמְנַעֲנֵעַ לוֹ רַגְלָיו:


מדדין עגלים – grab hold of its neck and its sides and drag it and assist it to move its legs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:8)


**וְהָאִשָּׁה מְדַדָּה אֶת בְּנָהּ.** אוֹחַזְתּוֹ בִּזְרוֹעָיו מֵאֲחוֹרָיו וְהוּא מֵנִיעַ רַגְלָיו וְהוֹלֵךְ:


והאשה מדדה את בנה – she grabs hold of his arms from behind him and moves his feet and walks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:9)


**שֶׁנּוֹטֵל אַחַת וּמֵנִיחַ אַחַת.** כְּשֶׁהַתִּינוֹק מֵנִיעַ אֶת רַגְלָיו מֵנִיחַ אַחַת וּמַגְבִּיהַּ אַחַת:


שנוטל אחת ומניח אחת – when the baby moves his legs, he places one leg down and lifts up the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:2:10)


**אֲבָל גּוֹרֵר אָסוּר.** שֶׁנּוֹשָׂאתוֹ. וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֵימָתַי בַּמִּשְׁנָה לֹא בָּא לַחֲלֹק אֶלָּא לְפָרֵשׁ דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים, הִלְכָּךְ הִלְכְתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


אבל גורר אסור – when she carries him, and everywhere in the Mishnah where Rabbi Yehuda says, "אימתי"/”from when,” he does not come to dispute, but rather to explain the words of the Sages. Therefore, the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:1)


**אֵין מְיַלְּדִין.** אֵין מוֹשְׁכִין הַוָּלָד מִן הָרֶחֶם בְּיוֹם טוֹב, דְּאִיכָּא טִרְחָא יְתֵירָא:


אין מילדין – we do not draw forth the fetus from the womb on the Jewish holy day, for there is greater trouble.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:2)


**אֲבָל מְסַעֲדִין.** אוֹחֵז אֶת הַוָּלָד שֶׁלֹּא יִפֹּל לָאָרֶץ:


אבל מסעדין – holds the fetus so that it would not fall to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:3)


**חֲכָמָה.** מְיַלֶּדֶת בְּקִיאָה:


חכמה – an expert midwife.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:4)


**מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם.** וְאֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁין לְאִסּוּר תְּחוּמִים:


ממקום למקום – and we don’t worry about the prohibition of [Sabbath] boundaries.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:5)


**וּמְחַלְּלִין עָלֶיהָ אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת.** מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁהִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל הַמַּשְׁבֵּר וּמַתְחִיל הַדָּם לִהְיוֹת שׁוֹתֵת עַד כָּל שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים אַחַר שֶׁיָּלְדָה, בֵּין אָמְרָה צְרִיכָה אֲנִי בֵּין לֹא אָמְרָה צְרִיכָה אֲנִי מְחַלְּלִין. מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד שִׁבְעָה, אָמְרָה צְרִיכָה אֲנִי מְחַלְּלִין, לֹא אָמְרָה צְרִיכָה אֲנִי אֵין מְחַלְּלִין. מִשִּׁבְעָה וְעַד שְׁלֹשִׁים, אֲפִלּוּ אָמְרָה צְרִיכָה אֲנִי אֵין מְחַלְּלִין, אֲבָל עוֹשִׂים צְרָכֶיהָ עַל יְדֵי נָכְרִי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא כְּחוֹלֶה שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ סַכָּנָה, וְדָבָר שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ סַכָּנָה עוֹשִׂין צְרָכָיו עַל יְדֵי נָכְרִי:


ומחללין עליה את השבת – from the time that she sits on the travailing chair and the blood begins to flow gently until all three days after she has given birth, whether she said that “I need” [assistance] or whether she did not say that “I need” [assistance], [we violate (the Sabbath)]; from the third to the seventh [day] if she said, “I need” we violate [the Sabbath], but if she did not say, “I need [assistance],” we don’t violate [the Sabbath]. From the seventh to the thirtieth [day], even if she said: “I need [assistance].” We do not violate [the Sabbath]. But we perform her needs through a non-Jew because she is like a lick person which is not in a dangerous situation, and any thing where there is no danger, we perform her needs through a non-Jew.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:6)


**וְקוֹשְׁרִים הַטַּבּוּר.** שֶׁל וָלָד שֶׁהוּא אָרֹךְ, וְאִם לֹא יִקְשְׁרוּהוּ וְיִכְרְכוּהוּ יֵצְאוּ מֵעָיו. אֲבָל אֵין כּוֹרְתִין אוֹתוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת לְדִבְרֵי תָּנָא קַמָּא:


וקושרים הטבור – of the fetus which is long and if we don’t tie it and fold it, its stomach will come out, but we don’t sever it on Shabbat according to the words of the first Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:7)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר אַף חוֹתְכִין.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי שֶׁחוֹתְכִין אוֹתוֹ וּמְנַקִּים אוֹתוֹ וְנוֹתְנִין עָלָיו אֲבַק הֲדַס וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ:


ר' יוסי אומר אף חותכין – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi, that we cut it and clean it and place upon it a bandage (אבק הדם ) and similar kinds of things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 18:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/18:3:8)


**וְכָל צָרְכֵי מִילָה עוֹשִׂין בְּשַׁבָּת.** וּלְקַמָּן בְּאִידָךְ פִּרְקָא מְפָרֵשׁ לְצָרְכֵי מִילָה מַאי נִיהוּ:


וכל צרכי מילה עושין בשבת – and further on in the next chapter (chapter 19) it will explain what are the needs of circumcision.



## Chapter 19


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אִם לֹא הֵבִיא כְלִי.** אִזְמֵל לָמוּל אֶת הַתִּינוֹק:


רבי אליעזר אומר אם לא הביא כלי – cutting tool/knife to circumcise the baby.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:2)


**מְבִיאוֹ בְשַׁבָּת מְגֻלֶּה.** לְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁחֲבִיבָה מִצְוָה זוֹ, שֶׁמְּחַלְּלִים עָלֶיהָ אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת:


מביאו בשבת מגולה – to announce that this Mitzvah is beloved for we desecrate the Sabbath for it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:3)


**וּבַסַּכָּנָה.** שֶׁגָּזְרוּ גְּזֵרָה עַל הַמִּילָה:


ובסכנה – when they (i.e., foreign powers) made a decree against circumcision.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:4)


**מְכַסֵּהוּ עַל פִּי עֵדִים.** שֶׁיָּעִידוּ שֶׁאִזְמֵל שֶׁל מִצְוָה הוּא מֵבִיא, וְלֹא יַחְשְׁדוּהוּ שֶׁהוּא נוֹשֵׂא שְׁאָר חֲפָצָיו:


מכסהו ע"פ עדים – that they will testify that this cutting tool/knife that he is bringing is for the Mitzvah and that they should not suspect him that he is carrying the rest of his belongings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:5)


**וְלַעֲשׂוֹת בַּרְזֶל.** כְּדֵי לַעֲשׂוֹת אִזְמֵל לְמִילָה:


ולעשות ברזל –in order to make a cutting tool for the circumcision.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:6)


**כָּל מְלָאכָה שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ.** כְּגוֹן מַכְשִׁירֵי מִילָה, הוֹאִיל וְאֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָן מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת אֵין דּוֹחִין אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת, וּפָלֵיג אַדְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


כל מלאכה שאפשר לעשותה - such as the requirements of a religious act of circumcision [to be performed on the Sabbath] since it is possible to perform them on the Eve of the Sabbath (i.e., on Friday), they do not override the [observance of the] Sabbath and he disagrees with Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:1:7)


**שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת.** כְּגוֹן מִילָה עַצְמָהּ שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לָהּ לֵעָשׂוֹת, דִּזְמַנָּהּ בַּשְּׁמִינִי, דּוֹחָה שַׁבָּת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


שאי אפשר לעשותה מע"ש – such as the actual circumcision for it is impossible to do it for its [appropriate] time is the eighth day which overrides the Sabbath and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiba.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:1)


**מוֹהֲלִין.** חוֹתֵךְ אֶת הָעָרְלָה:


מוהלין – to cut the foreskin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:2)


**וּפוֹרְעִין.** הָעוֹר הַמְכַסֶּה רֹאשׁ הַגִּיד:


ופורעין – the skin covering the top of the membrum virile (i.e., the corona)/split the membrane and pull it down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:3)


**וּמוֹצְצִים.** אֶת הַדָּם, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא עוֹשֶׂה חַבּוּרָה, שֶׁאֵין הַדָּם נִתָּק מֵחִבּוּרוֹ אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי מְצִיצָה:


ומוצצין – [to suck] the blood [and compress the blood vessels of the wound in the process] and even though he is making a wound, for the blood does not ooze/secrete [from its junction other than through sucking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:4)


**אִסְפְּלָנִית.** תַּחְבֹּשֶׁת:


אספלנית – bandage/compress/dressing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:5)


**לוֹעֵס בְּשִׁנָּיו.** דְּכָל מַה דְּאֶפְשָׁר לְשַׁנּוֹת מְשַׁנֶּה:


לועס בשיניו – for all that is possible to do [through] a change.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:6)


**אִם לֹא טָרַף יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן.** רְגִילִים הָיוּ שֶׁהָיוּ טוֹרְפִים בִּקְעָרָה יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן וּמְעָרְבִין אוֹתָן יַחַד לִרְפוּאָה עַל הַמִּילָה, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁטּוֹרְפִים בֵּיצִים בַּקְּעָרָה:


אם לא טרף יין ושמן – they were accustomed that they would beat wine and oil in a bowl and mix them together for a healing salve on the circumcision just as they beat eggs in a bowl.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:7)


**חָלוּק.** חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל בֶּגֶד נְקוּבָה, וּמַכְנִיסִין הַמִּילָה בְּאוֹתוֹ הַנֶּקֶב כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַחְזֹר הָעוֹר וִיכַסֶּה אֶת הַגִּיד:


חלוק – a piece of perforated cloth which they enter the circumcised membrum into the perforation in order that the skin does not return and cover up the membrum virile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:8)


**לֹא הִתְקִין.** לֹא הִזְמִין:


לא התקין – he did not prepare it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:9)


**כּוֹרֵךְ עַל אֶצְבָּעוֹ.** דֶּרֶךְ מַלְבּוּשׁ לְשַׁנּוֹתוֹ מִדֶּרֶךְ הוֹצָאָה בַּחֹל:


כורך על אצבע – in the form of a clothing to change it from the manner of taking it out on weekdays.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:2:10)


**אֲפִלּוּ מֵחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת.** לֹא מִיבָּעְיָא מִבַּיִת לְבַיִת בְּאוֹתוֹ חָצֵר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא עֵרְבוּ, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ לְחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְעֹרֶבֶת עִמָּהֶם:


אפי' מחצר אחרת – it is not required/necessary from one house to another house in the same courtyard even though they did not make an Eruv but even from another courtyard which is not [connected by] an Eruv with it.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:1)


**מַרְחִיצִין אֶת הַקָּטָן.** הַאי מַרְחִיצִין לָאו כְּדַרְכּוֹ הוּא, דְּתָנָא סֵיפָא לְפָרוֹשֵׁי רֵישָׁא כֵּיצַד מַרְחִיצִין כְּגוֹן לְזַלֵּף בַּיָּד, אֲבָל בִּכְלִי אֲפִלּוּ לְזַלֵּף אָסוּר, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן לִרְחֹץ כְּדַרְכּוֹ:


מרחיצין את הקטן – this washing is not in its regular manner, as the Tanna/teacher of the end [of the Mishnah] indicates to explain the first part [of the Mishnah] – how do they wash/cleanse? Such as to drip/sprinkle by hand, but even to sprinkle with a utensil is forbidden, and all the more so to wash in the regular manner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:2)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה כוּ'.** פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא וְסָבַר מַרְחִיצִין אֶת הַקָּטָן כְּדַרְכּוֹ בֵּין לִפְנֵי הַמִּילָה בֵּין לְאַחַר הַמִּילָה, וּבַיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי לַמִּילָה נַמִּי בֵּין בְּחַמִּין שֶׁהוּחְמוּ בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת בֵּין בְּחַמִּין שֶׁהוּחְמוּ בְּשַׁבָּת עַצְמוֹ, דְּסַכָּנַת נְפָשׁוֹת הִיא, וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה:


ראב"ע כו' - he disputes the first teacher [of the Mishnah] and he (i.e., Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah) holds that we wash/cleanse the [newborn] baby in the normal manner, whether before the circumcision, [or] whether after the circumcision, and on the third day [after] the circumcision also, whether the water was heated on the eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday) or even whether the waters were heated on the Sabbath itself, for it is a matter of danger to life and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eleazar ben Azariah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:3)


**סָפֵק.** בֶּן שְׁמֹנָה חֳדָשִׁים סָפֵק בֶּן תִּשְׁעָה. דְּאִי בֶּן שְׁמֹנָה כְּאֶבֶן בְּעָלְמָא הוּא וְאֵין מִילָתוֹ דּוֹחָה שַׁבָּת:


ספק – it is doubtful whether a child is eight months old or nine months old, for if he is eight months old, he is like a mere stone and his circumcision does not supersede the Sabbath day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:3:4)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר בְּאַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס.** מִדִּכְתִיב (בראשית טז) הִמּוֹל לָכֶם כָּל זָכָר לְרַבּוֹת אַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא, עָרְלָתוֹ כְּתִיב, מִי שֶׁהוּא כֻּלּוֹ עָרֵל, יָצָא זֶה שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ נְקֵבָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה מתיר באנדרוגינוס – since it is written (Genesis 17:12): “every male among you shall be circumcised,” including an androgynous individual, but [for] the first teacher [of our Mishnah], (Genesis 17:14) “his foreskin” is written, [meaning] he who is totally uncircumcised, excluding the case of someone who is half-female. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:1)


**מִי שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי תִינוֹקוֹת וְכוּ'.** פְּלִיגֵי אָמוֹרָאֵי בַּגְּמָרָא בְּגִרְסַת מִשְׁנָה זוֹ, וְהַגִּרְסָא שֶׁתָּפְסוּ רַבּוֹתַי עִקָּר כָּךְ הִיא, מִי שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי תִינוֹקוֹת, אֶחָד לָמוּל אַחַר שַׁבָּת וְאֶחָד לָמוּל בְּשַׁבָּת, וְשָׁכַח וּמָל אֶת שֶׁל אַחַר שַׁבָּת בְּשַׁבָּת, חַיָּב. אֶחָד לָמוּל בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת וְאֶחָד לָמוּל בְּשַׁבָּת, וְשָׁכַח וּמָל אֶת שֶׁל עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת בְּשַׁבָּת, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב חַטָּאת וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ פּוֹטֵר. וְהָכִי פֵּרוּשָׁהּ, שָׁכַח וּמָל אֶת שֶׁל אַחַר הַשַּׁבָּת בְּשַׁבָּת חַיָּב לְדִבְרֵי הַכֹּל, שֶׁהֲרֵי טָעָה בִּדְבַר מִצְוָה וְלֹא עָשָׂה מִצְוָה כְּשֶׁהִקְדִּים וּמָל אֶת שֶׁל אַחַר הַשַּׁבָּת בְּשַׁבָּת, וּבְהָא אֲפִלּוּ רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ מוֹדֶה:


מי שהיו לו שתי תינוקות וכו' - The Amoraim dispute in the Babylonian Talmud (Tractate Shabbat 137a) concerning the textual reading of this Mishnah but the textual reading that that my teachers grabbed hold of as essential is this: He who had two baby boys, one to circumcise [the day] after the Sabbath (i.e., on Sunday) and one to circumcise on the Sabbath but he forgot and circumcised the one of the day after the Sabbath on the Sabbath is liable [for a sin-offering]. If one was to be circumcised on the Eve of the Sabbath (i.e., on Friday), and one (i.e., the other) to be circumcised on the Sabbath, but he forgot and circumcised the one for the Eve of the Sabbath on the Sabbath, Rabbi Eliezer obligates him to bring a sin-offering and Rabbi Yehoshua [says] that he is exempt. And this its explanation: If he forgot and circumcised the one [who was to be circumcised] after the Sabbath (i.e., on Sunday) on the Sabbath, , according to everyone he is liable [for a sin-offering] for he has erred in the matter of the Commandment and did not perform the Mitzvah when he advanced [the circumcision] and circumcised the one for Sunday on the Sabbath, and in this, even Rabbi Yehoshua admits [that he is liable a sin-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:2)


**אֶחָד לָמוּל בְּשַׁבָּת וְאֶחָד לָמוּל בְּעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת וְשָׁכַח וּמָל אֶת שֶׁל עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת בְּשַׁבָּת, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב חַטָּאת.** דְּמִילָה שֶׁלֹּא בִּזְמַנָּהּ אֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה שַׁבָּת. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּטָעָה בִּדְבַר מִצְוָה שֶׁהָיָה טָרוּד בְּאוֹתוֹ שֶׁל שַׁבָּת וּמִתּוֹךְ כָּךְ טָעָה בָּזֶה, וְאַף בָּזֶה עָשָׂה מִצְוָה שֶׁהֲרֵי רָאוּי הוּא לָמוּל אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵינוֹ דּוֹחֶה שַׁבָּת, סְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר טָעָה בִּדְבַר מִצְוָה וְעָשָׂה מִצְוָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת חַיָּב:


אחד למול בשבת ואחד למול בערב שבת ושכח ומל את של ערב שבת בשבת ר' אליעזר מחייב חטאת – for it is a ritual circumcision that was not at the its [appropriate] time and does not supersede the Sabbath, and even though he erred in the matter of [the performance of] a commandment for he was preoccupied with that Sabbath and because of it, erred in it, and even with this, he performed a Mitzvah for it was appropriate to circumcise [his son] but it does not supersede the Sabbath, for Rabbi Eleazar holds that he erred in the matter of a Mitzvah and performed a Mitzvah which does not supersede the Sabbath and he is liable [for a sin-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:4:3)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ פּוֹטֵר.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ טָעָה בִּדְבַר מִצְוָה וְעָשָׂה מִצְוָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת פָּטוּר, כֵּיוָן דְּסָבוּר הָיָה שֶׁבִּרְשׁוּת בֵּית דִּין הוּא עוֹשֶׂה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ור"י פוטר – for he holds that he erred in the matter of a Mitzvah and performed a commandment that does not supersede the Sabbath=, he exempt [from having to bring a sin-offering] because he holds/thought that it was with the permission of the Jewish court that he did it, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:1)


**נוֹלַד בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת נִמּוֹל לְתִשְׁעָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי יוֹם שְׁמִינִי שֶׁל מָחָר הוּא נִמּוֹל, וְשֶׁמָּא בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת יוֹם הוּא וְנִמּוֹל לַתְּשִׁיעִי. הָיָה הַיּוֹם עֶרֶב שַׁבָּת אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְמוּלוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת הַבָּא, דְּשֶׁמָּא תְּשִׁיעִי הוּא וְהַוְיָא לַהּ מִילָה שֶׁלֹּא בִּזְמַנָּהּ וְאֵינָהּ דּוֹחָה שַׁבָּת, וְצָרִיךְ לְהַמְתִּין עַד לְאַחַר הַשַּׁבָּת שֶׁהוּא עֲשִׂירִי:


If he was born during twilight, he is circumcised on the ninth day as it is the eighth day from the morrow; if twighlight is still considered day, he is thus circumcised on the ninth day. If the day (preceding the twighlight during which he was born) is Friday, he can't be circumcised on the following Shabbat in case it is the ninth day and it is a postponed circumcision which does not supersede Shabbat, he must wait until after Shabbat and be circumcised on the tenth day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:2)


**חָל יוֹם טוֹב לִהְיוֹת אַחַר הַשַּׁבָּת.** אֵין מִילָה שֶׁלֹּא בִּזְמַנָּהּ דּוֹחָה אוֹתוֹ, וְנִמּוֹל לְאַחַד עָשָׂר:


חל יום טוב להיות אחר השבת – no circumcision which is [not performed] at its [appropriate] time may supersede it, and he is circumcised on the eleventh day [after his birth].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:3)


**שְׁנֵי יָמִים טוֹבִים שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה.** דִּקְדֻשָּׁה אַחַת הֵן, וְאֵין מִילָה שֶׁלֹּא בִּזְמַנָּהּ דּוֹחָה אֶת יוֹם טוֹב הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁל רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה, נִמּוֹל לִשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר:


שני ימים טובים של ראש השנה – for they are one level of sanctity and a circumcision [performed] not at its appropriate time does not supersede the Festival observance of the Second Day of Rosh Hashanah; he is circumcised on the twelfth day [from the day of his birth].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:5:4)


**עַד שֶׁיַּבְרִיא.** וְיַעַבְרוּ עָלָיו שִׁבְעָה יָמִים שְׁלֵמִים מֵעֵת לְעֵת מִיּוֹם שֶׁהִבְרִיא, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מָלִין אוֹתוֹ:


עד שיבריא – and seven complete days in astronomical hours should pass from the day that he is healed, and afterwards, we circumcise him (i.e., the child).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:1)


**צִיצִין.** כְּמִין נִימִין שֶׁל בָּשָׂר שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ מִן הָעָרְלָה:


ציצין –hidden shreds of flesh that remain from the foreskin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:2)


**עֲטָרָה.** הִיא שָׂפָה גְּבוֹהָה הַמַּקֶּפֶת אֶת הַגִּיד סָבִיב, וּמִמֶּנָּה מְשַׁפֵּעַ וְיוֹרֵד לְרֹאשׁוֹ. וּבָשָׂר הַחוֹפֶה אֶת רֹב הָעֲטָרָה דְּקָתָנֵי מַתְנִיתִין, לֹא תֵּימָא רֹב הֶקֵּפָהּ אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ רֹב גָּבְהָה בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


עטרה – it is a tall rim which surrounds the membrum virile round about and from it slants and descends to its head and the skin that covers most of the corona of the membrum virile as our Mishnah teaches , one should not say most of the coagulation, but even most of its height in one location.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:3)


**וְאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** אִם כֹּהֵן הוּא, דְּכֹהֵן עָרֵל אָסוּר לֶאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה. נֶאֱמַר בְּפֶסַח (שמות י) תּוֹשָׁב וְשָׂכִיר לֹא יֹאכַל בּוֹ, וְנֶאֱמַר בַּתְּרוּמָה (ויקרא כב) תּוֹשָׁב כֹּהֵן וְשָׂכִיר לֹא יֹאכַל בּוֹ, מַה פֶּסַח אָסוּר לְעָרֵל, אַף תְּרוּמָה אֲסוּרָה לְעָרֵל:


אינו אוכל בתרומה – if he is a Kohen, for an uncircumcised Kohen is prohibited to eat the priest’s due/Terumah. It is said regarding the Passover sacrifice (Exodus 12:45): “No bound or hired laborer shall eat of it,” and it is stated regarding Terumah (Leviticus 22:10): “No bound or hired laborer of a priest shall eat of the sacred donations.” Just as the Passover offering is prohibited to someone uncircumcised, so too the Terumah is prohibited to someone uncircumcised.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:4)


**וְאִם הָיָה בַעַל בָּשָׂר.** שֶׁהָיָה שָׁמֵן וְנִרְאֶה בָּשָׂר שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה מֵעָרְלָתוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּטְּלָה הָעָרְלָה כֻּלָּהּ כְּאִלּוּ אוֹתוֹ בָּשָׂר חוֹזֵר וְחוֹפֶה אֶת הַגִּיד:


ואם היה בעל בשר – if he was fat and flesh appeared that was above his uncircumcised membrum, after the foreskin was taken off completely, as if the flesh returned and covered the membrum virile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:5)


**מְתַקְּנוֹ.** וּמְשַׁפֵּעַ בְּאִזְמֵל מֵאוֹתוֹ עֹבִי:


מתקנו – and he slants the cutting utensil/knife from that thickness [of the fat].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:6)


**מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעָיִן.** שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִרְאֶה כְּעָרֵל:


מפני מראית העין – so that he would not appear as uncircumcised.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:7)


**וְלֹא פָרַע.** לֹא גִּלָּה:


ולא פרע – and he did not reveal it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 19:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/19:6:8)


**כְּאִלּוּ לֹא מָל.** וְיָשׁוּב וְיִפְרַע, וַאֲפִלּוּ סִלֵּק יָדוֹ מִמֶּנָּה. וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא מִתְעַסֵּק בַּמִּילָה בְּשַׁבָּת חוֹתֵךְ בֵּין צִיצִין הַמְעַכְּבִין בֵּין צִיצִין שֶׁאֵין מְעַכְּבִין. לְאַחַר שֶׁסִּלֵּק יָדוֹ, עַל צִיצִין הַמְעַכְּבִין חוֹזֵר, עַל צִיצִין שֶׁאֵינָן מְעַכְּבִין אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר:


כאלו לא מל – and he would go back and split the membrane and pull it down and even if he removed his hand from it – for all the while that he is engaged in the circumcision on the Sabbath, he cuts whether the remaining shreds invalidate the act by omission or whether they don’t invalidate the act by omission after he removes his hand from the shreds which invalidate the act, he returns to the shreds which do not invalidate the act and he doesn’t go back.



## Chapter 20


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:1)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר תּוֹלִין אֶת הַמְּשַׁמֶּרֶת.** שֶׁמְּסַנְּנִים בָּהּ שִׁמְרֵי יַיִן, וּמוֹתֵחַ אֶת פִּיהָ לְכָל צַד בְּעִגּוּל וְנַעֲשֶׂה כְּאֹהֶל עַל חֲלַל הַכְּלִי. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּעָבֵיד אֹהֶל, שָׁרֵי בְּיוֹם טוֹב, דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁירֵי אֹכֶל נֶפֶשׁ שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לַעֲשׂוֹתָן מֵעֶרֶב יוֹם טוֹב מֻתָּר לַעֲשׂוֹתָן בְּיוֹם טוֹב. אֲבָל בְּשַׁבָּת [תּוֹלֶה] לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא, אֲבָל אִם תְּלוּיָה הִיא נוֹתְנִין לְתוֹכָהּ שְׁמָרִים וּמְסַנְּנִין, דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בּוֹרֵר בְּכָךְ:


רבי אליעזר אומר: תולין את המשמרת – they filter in it the sediment of the wine and stretch/extend its mouth over the entire side in a circle and it makes a “tent” over the hollow/cavity of the utensil but even though he made a “tent,” it is permissible on Jewish holy days, for Rabbi Eliezer holds that acts preliminary to the preparation of food where it is possible to do them from the eve of the Jewish holy day, it is permitted to do them on the Jewish holy day, but on the Sabbath, ab initio, he may not suspend [a strainer] , but if it is [already] suspended, he may place into it the sediments, and stretch it for this is not the manner of "בורר"/sifting (which is one of the primary forms of labor prohibited on the Sabbath).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:2)


**אֵין תּוֹלִין אֶת הַמְּשַׁמֶּרֶת.** דְּעָבֵיד אֹהֶל עֲרַאי וּמֵחֲזֵי כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חֹל:


אין תולין את המשמרת - for he is making a temporary tent and it appears like an act of the weekdays.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:1:3)


**וְאֵין נוֹתְנִים לַתְּלוּיָה בְשַׁבָּת.** דַּהֲוָה תּוֹלָדַת בּוֹרֵר אוֹ מְרַקֵּד. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ואין נותנין לתלויה בשבת – for it is a derivative of "בורר"/sifting or "מרקד" /shaking in the sieve, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:1)


**נוֹתְנִים מַיִם.** בְּשַׁבָּת עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁמָרִים הַנְּתוּנִים בַּמְשַׁמֶּרֶת מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם. כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּצַּלּוּ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַשְּׁמָרִים צְלוּלִים וְיָזוֹב כָּל יֵינָם. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁמָרִים שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ בֶּחָבִית, וְקוֹלְטִין הַמַּיִם טַעַם הַיַּיִן, וּמוֹצִיאִין אוֹתָן בְּשַׁבָּת וְשׁוֹתִים אוֹתָן, וְאֵין בָּזֶה מִשּׁוּם בּוֹרֵר:


נותנין מים – [the pour water] on the Sabbath on top of the sediment that is placed in the strainer/filter in order that they will dilute them so that the sediment will be clear and that all their wine will flow. Another explanation: on top of the sediment that remains in the barrel, and the water retains the taste of the wine and they release it on the Sabbath and drink it, and there is nothing in this of [the prohibition of] "בורר" /sifting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:2)


**אֶת הַיַּיִן.** מִפְּנֵי הַקְּמָחִים לְבָנִים שֶׁמִּתְהַוִּים בּוֹ:


את היין – because of the white flour that exists in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:3)


**בְּסוּדָרִין.** הַמְיֻחָדִין לְכָךְ. וְלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר דִּלְמָא סָחִיט לְהוּ, הוֹאִיל וַעֲשׂוּיִין לִמְלָאכָה זוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה בְּיָדוֹ בַּסּוּדָר כְּמִין גֻּמָּא בְּפִי הַכְּלִי כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּרֵד הַיַּיִן בַּמִּדְרוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהוּא עוֹשֶׂה בְּחֹל:


בסודרין – that are uniquely designed for this and we should not decree lest that he is “pressing out”/ “wringing out,” for they are made for this labor, so that it doesn’t have to be done by hand with a kerchief like a bulrush in the utensil in order the wine will descend down a declivity, so that he will not do it as he would on a weekday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:4)


**וּבִכְפִיפָה מִצְרִית.** סַל הֶעָשׂוּי מִצּוּרֵי הַדֶּקֶל. וְהוּא שֶׁלֹּא תִּהְיֶה הַכְּפִיפָה גְּבוֹהָה מִקַּרְקָעִית הַכְּלִי הַתַּחְתּוֹן טֶפַח, שֶׁלֹּא יֵעָשֶׂה אֹהֶל עֲרַאי:


ובכפיפה מצרית – a basket made of the bands made of palm-bark so that the basket should not be higher than the bottom of the lowest utensil by a handbreadth, so that the tent is not made temporary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:5)


**וְנוֹתְנִים בֵּיצָה בִמְסַנֶּנֶת.** שֶׁנָּתְנוּ בָּהּ חַרְדָּל לְהִסְתַּנֵּן, שֶׁרְגִילִין לִתֵּן בֵּיצָה טְרוּפָה בַּדְּבָרִים הָעֲכוּרִין וְהֵם מִתְלַבְּנִים וּמִזְדַּכְּכִים עַל יָדָהּ:


ונותנין ביצה במסננת – that they placed in it mustard to be filtered for it is customary to place a scrambled egg in decaying things and they become whitened and clear through this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:6)


**אֵנוּמְלִין.** יַיִן וּדְבַשׁ וּפִלְפְּלִין:


ינומולין – wine, honey and peppers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:7)


**לָגִין.** גָּדוֹל מִכּוֹס וְקָטָן מֵחָבִית:


לגין – lager than a cup and smaller than a barrel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:2:8)


**הַכֹּל לְפִי הָאוֹרְחִים.** אִם יֵשׁ לוֹ אוֹרְחִין מְרֻבִּין עוֹשֶׂה הַרְבֵּה, בֵּין בְּשַׁבָּת בֵּין בְּיוֹם טוֹב בֵּין בַּמּוֹעֵד. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא שֶׁעוֹשִׂין יֵנוּמוּלִין בְּשַׁבָּת כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁהֵן רוֹצִים:


הכל לפי האורחין – if he will have many guests, he makes a lot whether on Shabbat, on the Jewish holy day or during the Intermediate Days of the Festival, and the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher that we make wine mixed with honey [and pepper] according to what they want.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:1)


**חִלְתִּית.** כָּךְ שְׁמָהּ בַּעֲרָבִי, וְהִיא חַמָּה וְנוֹהֲגִין לְאָכְלָהּ בַּמְּקוֹמוֹת הַקָּרִים, וְאֵין שׁוֹרִין אוֹתָהּ בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּמֵחֲזֵי כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חֹל:


חלתית – [assa foetida]. This is its name in Arabic, and it is hot and it is the custom to eat it in cold places, and we do not dissolve it [the resin of assa foetida] on the Sabbath because it appears like an act of the weekdays.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:2)


**אֶת הַכַּרְשִׁינִין.** מֵצִיף עֲלֵיהֶם מַיִם בִּכְלִי לִבְרֹר פְּסָלְתָּן, שֶׁהַפְּסֹלֶת צָף לְמַעְלָה:


את הכרשינין – pour water in a utensil to separate their refuse , for the refuse floats to the top.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:3)


**וְלֹא שָׁפִין אוֹתָן.** בַּיָּד לְהַשִּׁיר פְּסָלְתָּן, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ בּוֹרֵר:


ולא שפין אותן – by hand to remove their refuse for this is "בורר"/sifting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:4)


**אֲבָל נוֹתֵן בְּתוֹךְ הַכְּבָרָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפְּסָלְתָּן נוֹפֵל לִפְעָמִים דֶּרֶךְ נִקְבֵי הַכְּבָרָה וְנִמְצָא מִתְבָּרֵר מֵאֵלָיו:


אבל נותן לתוך הכברה – and even though their refuse falls sometimes through the holes of the sieve and it becomes selected on its own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:5)


**הַתֶּבֶן.** שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹשִׂים מִן הַקַּשׁ שֶׁמְּחַתְּכִין אוֹתָן בַּמּוֹרִיגִים וְנַעֲשָׂה כָּל זְנַב הַשִּׁבָּלִין תֶּבֶן:


התבן – that they would make from the straw/stubble and they would cut it with a threshing sledge and each tail of the ears of corn would be made into straw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:6)


**מֹץ.** הוּא מִזְּקַן הַשִּׁבֹּלֶת הָעֶלְיוֹן, וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה, וְכוֹבְרִים אוֹתוֹ בִּכְבָרָה שֶׁיִּפֹּל הַמֹּץ:


מוץ – this is old upper ears of corn/spike and it is not fit for an animal’s eating and we sift it in a sieve so that the chaff will fall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:3:7)


**אֲבָל נוֹטֵל בִּכְבָרָה וְנוֹתֵן לְתוֹךְ הָאֵבוּס.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַמֹּץ נוֹפֵל מֵאֵלָיו, דְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן מֻתָּר כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אבל נוטל בכברה ונותן לתוך האבוס – and even though the chaff falls on its own, since it is a thing that one doesn’t intend to happen, it is permissible according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:1)


**גּוֹרְפִין.** בְּשַׁבָּת אֵבוּס שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַשּׁוֹר שֶׁמְּפַטְּמִים אוֹתוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְעָרֵב הָעַפְרוּרִית שֶׁבָּאֵבוּס בַּתֶּבֶן וּבַשְּׂעוֹרִים שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים לְפָנָיו וְיָקוּץ בְּמַאֲכָלוֹ, וּמְסַלְּקִין תֶּבֶן שֶׁלְּפָנָיו לִצְדָדִים כְּשֶׁהוּא רַב כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִדְרְסֶנּוּ בְּרַגְלָיו וְיִתְלַכְלֵךְ בִּרְעִי:


גורפין – on the Sabbath, the feeding receptacle/manger that is in front of the ox, we fatten it so that it will not become combined with the sandy matter in the grain that is in the manger in the straw and barley that we place before him and he will cut himself in his food and we remove the straw that is before him to the sides when there is a lot of it so that the animal will not tread on it with his foot and become dirty from the excrement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** אַתַּרְוַיְהוּ קָאֵי, אֶחָד גְּרִיפַת הָאֵבוּס וְאֶחָד תֶּבֶן שֶׁלְּפָנָיו לֹא יְסַלְּקֶנּוּ לִצְדָדִים. וְלֹא נֶחְלְקוּ רַבִּי דּוֹסָא וַחֲכָמִים אֶלָּא בְּאֵבוּס שֶׁל כְּלִי, אֲבָל בְּאֵבוּס שֶׁל קַרְקַע הַכֹּל מוֹדִים שֶׁאֵין גּוֹרְפִים, דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַשְׁווֹיֵי גֻּמּוֹת. וְרַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ אֵבוּס שֶׁל כְּלִי אַטּוּ אֵבוּס שֶׁל קַרְקַע. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אוסרים – it refers to both – cleaning out the manger and the straw that is in front of him (i.e., the animal), he should not sweep it to the sides but Rabbi Sosa and the Sages only argued with regard to the real manger (see Talmud Shabbat 140b in Rashi – as opposed to a piece of ground fenced in and used as a manger/אבוס של קרקע ). But a piece of ground that is fenced in and used as a manger, all agree that we do not clean it out, lest one make indentations/holes, , but the Rabbis decreed that a real manger is just like a piece of ground that is fenced in and used as a manger. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:4:3)


**נוֹטְלִים מִלִּפְנֵי בְהֵמָה זוֹ וְנוֹתְנִים לִפְנֵי זוֹ.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן טִלְטוּל דְּלֹא חָזֵי הֲוֵי, אֶלָּא וַדַּאי חָזֵי, דְּאֵין בְּהֵמָה קָצָה בְּמַאֲכַל הַנִּטָּל מִלִּפְנֵי חֲבֶרְתָהּ. וְדַוְקָא מִלִּפְנֵי הַחֲמוֹר וְנוֹתְנִין לִפְנֵי הַשּׁוֹר וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּזֶה, אֲבָל לֹא מִלִּפְנֵי הַשּׁוֹר וְנוֹתְנִים לִפְנֵי הַחֲמוֹר, שֶׁמַּאֲכַל הַשּׁוֹר מָאוּס בָּרִירִין הַיּוֹצְאִים מִפִּיו וְאֵין הַחֲמוֹר אוֹכֵל מִמֶּנּוּ:


נוטלים מלפני בהמה זו ונותנין לפני זו – and we don’t say that it does not appear like carrying , because it really appears like it, because an animal is not set aside with food that is taken from its fellow [animal]. And specifically, from before a donkey and we place it (i.e., the food) before a ox, and similarly to it, but not something that is before the ox and placing it before the donkey, for the food of an ox is detestable with the spittle that comes out of its mouth, and the donkey will not eat of it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:1)


**הַקַּשׁ שֶׁעַל הַמִּטָּה.** סְתָמָא לְהַסָּקָה וּמֻקְצֶה הוּא, וּבָא לִשְׁכַּב עָלָיו וּמְנַעְנְעוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא רַךְ וְצָף לִשְׁכַּב עָלָיו:


הקש שעל מטה – merely for kindling and it is Muktzeh/something set aside/designated for weekday use only and when he comes to lie on it and moves it about in order to be soft and looks to lie on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:2)


**לֹא יְנַעְנְעוֹ בְיָדוֹ.** דְּמֻקְצֶה הוּא, אֲבָל מְנַעְנְעוֹ בְּגוּפוֹ בִּכְתֵפָיו, דְּטִלְטוּל מִן הַצַּד הוּא וְלָאו שְׁמֵיהּ טִלְטוּל:


לא ינענענו בידיו – for it is Muktzeh but he may move it with his body [or] his shoulders, for it is carrying from the side, and it is not called carrying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:3)


**אוֹ שֶׁהָיָה עָלָיו כַּר אוֹ סָדִין.** דְּגַלֵּי דַּעְתֵּיהּ דְּאַקְצְיֵהּ לִשְׁכִיבָה, מֵעַתָּה תּוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו:


או שהיה עליו כר או סדין – for he revealed his intention that he set it aside for sleeping so from now it has the status of a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:4)


**מַכְבֵּשׁ.** שְׁנֵי לוּחוֹת שֶׁמְּשִׂימִין בֵּינֵיהֶן הַבְּגָדִים לְאַחַר כְּבִיסָתָן וּמְהַדְּקִין הַלּוּחַ הָעֶלְיוֹן עַל הַבְּגָדִים הַמֻּנָּחִין בַּלּוּחַ הַתַּחְתּוֹן כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא קִפּוּלָן נָאֶה:


מכבש – two boards that they place the clothes between them after laundering them and they fasten the upper board on the clothing that are lying on the lower board, in order that they fold nicely.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:5)


**מַתִּירִין.** דְּהַיְנוּ לְצֹרֶךְ שַׁבָּת לִטֹּל אֶת הַבְּגָדִים, אֲבָל לֹא כּוֹבְשִׁים דְּהַיְנוּ צֹרֶךְ חֹל:


מתירין – that is for the needs of the Sabbath to take the clothing, but we do not press/tighten them for that is a need of the weekdays.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 20:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/20:5:6)


**וְשֶׁל כּוֹבְסִים לֹא יִגַּע בּוֹ.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא עָשׂוּי לְתַקֵּן בְּגָדִים וּמִהַדַּק טְפֵי, וְהַתָּרָתוֹ דּוֹמָה לִסְתִירָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ושל כובסים לא יגע בו – because it is made to repair clothing and fasten them more , and loosening them is similar to tearing down/destroying and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda..



## Chapter 21


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:1)


**נוֹטֵל אָדָם אֶת בְּנוֹ.** בֶּחָצֵר וְהָאֶבֶן בְּיָדוֹ וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן דִּמְטַלְטֵל לָאֶבֶן:


נוטל אדם את בנו – in the courtyard and the stone is in his hand, but we don’t say that he is carrying regarding the stone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:2)


**כַּלְכַּלָּה.** סַל:


כלכלה – basket


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:3)


**וְהָאֶבֶן בְּתוֹכָהּ.** וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיוּ פֵּרוֹת בַּסַּל, שֶׁאִם אֵין בּוֹ פֵּרוֹת נַעֲשֶׂה בָּסִיס לְדָבָר הָאָסוּר וְאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ. וְצָרִיךְ נַמִּי שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בְּתוֹכוֹ פֵּרוֹת שֶׁאִם יַשְׁלִיכֵם בָּאָרֶץ יִמָּאֲסוּ, כְּגוֹן תּוּתִים וַעֲנָבִים וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶם, אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ בּוֹ פֵּרוֹת כְּגוֹן אֱגוֹזִים וּשְׁקֵדִים, מְנַעֵר אֶת הַפֵּרוֹת וְהֵם נוֹפְלִים. וּפֵרוֹת הַנִּמְאָסִים נַמִּי כְּגוֹן תּוּתִים וַעֲנָבִים, אִם אֶפְשָׁר לְסַלְּקָם לְצִדֵּי הַכַּלְכַּלָּה וּלְנַעֵר הָאֶבֶן לְבַדָּהּ וּלְהַשְׁלִיכָהּ, אָסוּר לְטַלְטְלָהּ עִם הָאֶבֶן. וּמַתְנִיתִין אָיְרֵי כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּפְחַת מִצִּדֵּי הַכַּלְכַּלָּה אוֹ שׁוּלֶיהָ וְהָאֶבֶן דֹּפֶן לָהּ, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בַּכַּלְכַּלָּה בְּלֹא הָאֶבֶן:


והאבן בתוכה – and it is the case that there will be fruit/produce in the basket, for if there were no fruit in it, it would be made the basis for a prohibited thing for it is prohibited to carry it, and there needs also that there would be in it fruit so that if he casts them on the ground, they would be detestable, such as mulberries and grapes and similar things, but if there were no fruit in it, such as nuts and almonds, he would empty the fruit and they would fall, but the detestable fruit also such as mulberries and grapes, if it is possible to remove them to the sides of the basket and to remove the stone alone, and to cast it away, for it is prohibited to carry it with the stone. But our Mishnah is speaking about the case where it had broken through from the sides of the basket or its rim at the bottom of the vessel and the stone was a wall to it, that it is impossible to use the basket without [the presence] of the stone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:4)


**תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה עִם הַטְּהוֹרָה וְעִם הַחֻלִּין.** אַגַּב הַטְּהוֹרָה וְאַגַּב הַחֻלִּין. אֲבָל טְמֵאָה בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשַׁהּ לָאו בַּת טִלְטוּל הִיא:


תרומה טמאה עם הטהורה ועם החולין – subsidiary to the pure produce and subsidiary to the non-holy produce but the unclean is separate and is not carried.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:1:5)


**אַף מַעֲלִין אֶת הַמְדֻמָּע בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה.** סְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה בְּמֵאָה סְאִים שֶׁל חֻלִּין, מֻתָּר לְהַעֲלוֹתָהּ לִסְאָה שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה מֵהֶן בְּשַׁבָּת וְיִהְיוּ כֻּלָּן חֻלִּין וּמֻתָּרִין לְזָרִים, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן מְתַקֵּן הוּא, דִּתְרוּמָה שֶׁנָּפְלָה בַּחֻלִּין חָשְׁבִינַן לַהּ כְּאִלּוּ מֻנַּחַת לְבַדָּהּ וְאֵינָהּ מְעֹרֶבֶת, וּכְשֶׁעוֹלָה בְּאֶחָד וּמֵאָה הַתְּרוּמָה עַצְמָהּ שֶׁנָּפְלָה עוֹלָה, הִלְכָּךְ לָאו מְתַקֵּן הוּא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אף מעלין את המדומע באחד ומאה – A Se’ah of Terumah/priest’s due that fell into one-hundred Se’ah of non-holy produce, it is permissible to raise it up to a Se’ah of Terumah from them on Shabbat and all of it (i.e., the rest) would be non-holy produce, and it is permissible for foreigners (i.e., non-priests) and we don’t say that he is making things legally fit for use by giving the priest’s dues, for the Terumah that fell in the non-holy produce, we consider it as if it was placed alone, and didn’t become mixed up, and when it comes up as one-part in one-hundred and one, the Terumah itself that fell comes up.
