Therefore, it is not repairing, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:1)


**מַטָּהּ עַל צִדָּהּ.** מַטֶּה הֶחָבִית עַל צִדָּהּ אִם צָרִיךְ לִטֹּל מִן הַיַּיִן, וְהָאֶבֶן נוֹפֶלֶת, וְלֹא יִטֹּל אוֹתָהּ בַּיָּדַיִם:


מטה על צדה – incline the barrel on its side if it is necessary to take from the wine and stone falls, but he he should not take it with his hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:2)


**הָיְתָה בֵין הֶחָבִיּוֹת.** וּמִתְיָרֵא שֶׁלֹּא תִּפֹּל הָאֶבֶן עַל הֶחָבִיּוֹת וְתִשְׁבְּרֵם, מַגְבִּיהָהּ לְחָבִית כֻּלָּהּ וּמְסַלְּקָהּ מִבֵּין הֶחָבִיּוֹת, וְשָׁם מַטֶּה אוֹתָהּ עַל צִדָּהּ:


היתה בין החביות – and he is afraid that the stone won’t fall on the barrels and break them, he should lift the entire barrel and remove it from among the barrels and there tilt/incline it on its side.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:3)


**נוֹעֵר אֶת הַכַּר וְהֵן נוֹפְלוֹת.** כְּשֶׁהוּא צָרִיךְ לַכַּר וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לִמְקוֹם הַכַּר, אֲבָל אִם צָרִיךְ לִמְקוֹמוֹ מַגְבִּיהַּ הַכַּר עִם הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁעָלָיו. וְלֹא אֲמָרָן אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁשָּׁכַח הַמָּעוֹת עַל הַכַּר מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת, אֲבָל הֵנִיחָן שָׁם בְּמִתְכַּוֵּן נַעֲשָׂה הַכַּר בָּסִיס לְדָבָר הָאָסוּר וְאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלוֹ וְלֹא לְנַעֵר הַמָּעוֹת שֶׁעָלָיו:


נוער את הכר והן נופלות – when he needs the cushion, but he doesn’t need the place of the cushion. But if he needs its place, he lifts the cushion with the coins that are upon them, and we don’t say other than that other than when he forgot the coins on the cushion from the Eve of the Sabbath but he intentionally placed them there, the cushion becomes the basis for a prohibited thing and it is prohibited to carry it nor to shake the coins that are upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:4)


**לַשְׁלֶשֶׁת.** דָּבָר שֶׁל טִנּוּף כְּגוֹן רֹק אוֹ רְעִי אוֹ צוֹאָה:


לשלשת – a matter of filth such as spittle, secretion or excrement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:5)


**מְקַנְּחָהּ בִּסְמַרְטוּט.** וְלֹא יִתֵּן עָלֶיהָ מַיִם, דִּסְתָם כַּר שֶׁל בֶּגֶד הוּא, וּבֶגֶד שְׁרִיָּתוֹ בַּמַּיִם זֶהוּ כִּבּוּסוֹ:


בסמרטוט מקנחת – but he should not put water upon it, for a mere cushion is of cloth and cloth is soaked in water which is its cleansing/washing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:6)


**הָיְתָה.** עַל כַּר:


היתה – on the cushion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:7)


**שֶׁל עוֹר.** דְּלָאו בַּר כִּבּוּס הוּא:


של עור – which is not washed/cleansed – he puts waster upon it until it (i.e., the dirt) is gone and the secretion goes away, but no actual cleansing, for since mere cushions and pillow cases are soft and require cleanings with soft leathers; but however, soaking is their cleansing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:2:8)


**נוֹתֵן עָלֶיהָ מַיִם.** עַד שֶׁתִּכְלֶה וְתֵלֵךְ הַלַּשְׁלֶשֶׁת. אֲבָל כִּבּוּס מַמָּשׁ לֹא, דְּהוֹאִיל וּסְתָם כָּרִים וּכְסָתוֹת רַכִּים נִינְהוּ שַׁיָּךְ בְּהוּ כִּבּוּס בְּעוֹרוֹת רַכִּים, וּמִיהוּ שְׁרִיָּתָן לֹא זֶהוּ כִּבּוּסָן:



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:1)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים מַגְבִּיהִין מֵעַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן עֲצָמוֹת וּקְלִפִּין.** בַּגְּמָרָא קָאָמַר שֶׁאֵין אָנוּ סוֹמְכִים עַל מִשְׁנָתֵנוּ כְּמוֹת שֶׁהִיא שְׁנוּיָה, אֶלָּא מֻחְלֶפֶת הַשִּׁיטָה, שֶׁבֵּית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִין מַגְבִּיהִין מֵעַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן עֲצָמוֹת וּקְלִפִּין, וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים מְסַלֵּק אֶת הַטַּבְלָא שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלֶיהָ תּוֹרַת כְּלִי אֲבָל לֹא יְטַלְטֵל הָעֲצָמוֹת וְהַקְּלִפִּין בַּיָּדַיִם, דְּבֵית הִלֵּל כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה. וּמִיהוּ לֹא שָׁרוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל אֶלָּא בַּעֲצָמוֹת וּקְלִפִּין דְּחָזוּ לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא חָזוּ לְמַאֲכַל אָדָם, אֲבָל אִי לֹא חָזוּ אַף לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה מוֹדוּ בֵּית הִלֵּל דַּאֲסוּרִים לְטַלְטֵל, דְּבִכְהַאי גַּוְנָא אֲפִלּוּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מוֹדֶה:


ב"ש אומרים מגביהין מעל השלחן עצמות וקליפין – In the Gemara (See Talmud Shabbat 143a) that we do not rely upon our Mishnah as it is taught but the statements must be reversed (i.e., the authorities for the two opinions must be exchanged) for it is the School of Hillel that states that we take up [on the Sabbath] table bones and shells and the School of Shammai that states that one removes the whole board and shakes it, for it has the status of a utensil, but he should not carry the bones and shells by hand, for the School of Hillel is like Rabbi Shimon and the School of Shammai is like Rabbi Yehuda. But, however, the School of Hillel did not permit other than the bones and shells that are fit for the food for an animal, even though they are not fit for the food of a person, but if they are not fit even for food for an animal, the School of Hillel admits that it is prohibited to carry them , for in such a manner even Rabbi Shimon admits to.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:2)


**מַעֲבִירִין מֵעַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן פֵּרוּרִין פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת.** אֲפִלּוּ פָּחוֹת מִכַּזַּיִת, וְטַעְמָא כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בְּסָמוּךְ, שֶׁהֵן רְאוּיִין לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה:


מעבירין מעל השלחן פרורין פחות מכזית – even less than an olive’s bulk and the reason is as it is explained shortly that they are fit for the food of cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:3)


**וְשֵׂעָר שֶׁל פּוֹלִין.** שַׁרְבִיטִים שֶׁהַקִּטְנִית גָּדֵל בָּהֶן:


ושער של פולין – a twig that the beans grow on them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:4)


**עוֹר בֵּית אֲחִיזָה.** בֵּית אֲחִיזָה שֶׁל עוֹר שֶׁיֹּאחֲזֶנּוּ בּוֹ:


עור בית אחיזה – a leather handle that one holds onto.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:5)


**אֵין מְקַנְּחִין בּוֹ.** שֶׁכְּשֶׁאוֹחֲזוֹ נִסְחָט בֵּין אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו וַהֲוֵי פְּסִיק רֵישֵׁיהּ וְלֹא יָמוּת, דְּמוֹדֶה בֵּיהּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אין מקנחין בו – for when he holds it, it is pressed out/flows between his fingers and there is an unavoidable result of an act that Rabbi Shimon admits to.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:6)


**בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ.** בֵּין יֵשׁ לוֹ בֵּית אֲחִיזָה בֵּין אֵין לוֹ בֵּית אֲחִיזָה, נִטָּל בְּשַׁבָּת כְּשֶׁהוּא נָגוּב:


בין כך ובין כך – whether or not it has a handle it is taken on Shabbat when it is dry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 21:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/21:3:7)


**וְאֵינוֹ מְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה.** דְּאֵינוֹ לֹא כְּלִי עֵץ וְלֹא בֶּגֶד וְלֹא שַׂק וְלֹא מַתֶּכֶת:


ואינו מקבל טומאה – it is not a wooden utensil nor a cloth/garment nor a sack nor metal.



## Chapter 22


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:1)


**מַצִּילִין מְזוֹן שָׁלֹשׁ סְעֻדוֹת.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּכֵלִים הַרְבֵּה, דְּאִלּוּ בִּכְלִי אֶחָד אָמְרוּ בְּפֶרֶק כָּל כִּתְבֵי דְּכַמָּה דְּבָעֵי מַצִּיל:


מצילין מזון ג' סעודות – and even in many utensils, for if it was In one utensil, they said in the chapter [sixteen of Tractate Shabbat, Mishnah two] “all the [holy] writings,” that as many as is necessary, one saves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:2)


**לָכֶם.** כָּל אֶחָד מְזוֹן שָׁלֹשׁ סְעוּדוֹת:


לכם – food for everyone for three [Sabbath] meals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:3)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִסְפֹּג.** שֶׁלֹּא יָשִׂים סְפוֹג לִשְׁאֹב הַיַּיִן וְלַחְזֹר וּלְהַטִּיף, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לַסְּפוֹג בֵּית אֲחִיזָה, דְּלֵיכָּא חֲשַׁשׁ סְחִיטָה, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהוּא עוֹשֶׂה בַּחֹל. וַאֲפִלּוּ לִקַּח בְּיָדוֹ שֶׁמֶן וּדְבַשׁ שֶׁהֵן עָבִים וְנִדְבָּקִים וּלְקַנֵּחַ יָדוֹ בִּשְׂפַת הַכְּלִי אָסוּר, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה כְּמַעֲשֵׂה חֹל:


ובלבד שלא יספוג – that he should not place a sponge to absorb the wine and to go back and to cause it to drip and even though one must sponge the handle, for there is no fear of a wringing/squeezing out they were brought iin, even to take in his hand oil or honey which are thick and are affixed and clean his hand on the rim of the utensil, it is prohibited that he do so in the manner that he does it on weekdays.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:4)


**אֵין סוֹחֲטִין אֶת הַפֵּרוֹת.** דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ מְפָרֵק תּוֹלָדָה דְּדָשׁ:


אין סוחטין את הפירות – for he is taking it apart/severing it which is a derivative of threshing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:5)


**אֲסוּרִין.** גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֹט לְכַתְּחִלָּה:


אסורין – a decree lest he squeeze it out/wring it ab initio.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אִם לָאֳכָלִין.** הָיוּ אוֹתָן פֵּרוֹת מְכֻנָּסִים, הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן מֻתָּר, דְּלֹא נִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּמַה שֶּׁזָּבוּ וְלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר בְּהוּ שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֹט:


ר"י אומר אם לאוכלין – those same fruits were collected that which comes out from them is permitted, for it is not satisfactory for him with what flowed out and we cannot make a decree lest he wring them out/squeeze them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:7)


**וְאִם לְמַשְׁקִין.** הָיוּ מְכֻנָּסִין, דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ בְּמַאי דְּנָפַק מִנַּיְהוּ, הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן אָסוּר, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֹט. וּבְזֵיתִים וַעֲנָבִים מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה לַחֲכָמִים דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּכִנְּסָן לָאֳכָלִים הַיּוֹצֵא מֵהֶן אָסוּר, כֵּיוָן דְּדַרְכָּן לִסְחִיטָה קָיְמֵי, כִּי אָתוּ לִידֵי מַשְׁקֶה יָהֵיב דַּעְתֵּיהּ בְּהָכִי. וּבִשְׁאָר מִינֵי פֵּרוֹת מוֹדִים חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו דַּרְכָּן לִסְחִיטָה. לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא בְּתוּתִים וְרִמּוֹנִים, דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְדַמֶּה לְהוּ לִשְׁאָר פֵּרוֹת וַחֲכָמִים מְדַמּוּ לְהוּ לְזֵיתִים וַעֲנָבִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ואם למשקין – they were collected for it was satisfactory for him what came out from them – that which comes out from them is prohibited as a decree lest he squeeze them, and with olives and grapes, Rabbi Yehuda agrees with the Sages that even though he brought them in to eat, that which comes out from them is prohibited since they exist for squeezing when they come to a drink, he would give his attention to this, but in regard to the rest of the kinds of fruits, the Sages agree with Rabbi Yehuda, since it was not the manner for squeezing, they didn’t dispute other than with mulberries and pomegranates, for Rabbi Yehuda compares them to the rest of the priests and the Sages compare them to olives and grapes, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:1:8)


**חַלּוֹת דְּבַשׁ.** כְּשֶׁהֵן מְרֻסָּקִים זָב הַדְּבַשׁ מֵעַצְמוֹ מִתּוֹךְ הַשַּׁעֲוָה וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְסָחְטוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַתִּיר, וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים, גָּזְרוּ אַטּוּ שֶׁאֵין מְרֻסָּקִין. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


חלות דבש – when they are crushed/chopped, the honey flows on its own from the midst of the wax and it is not the manner to squeeze/wring it; therefore, Rabbi Eliezer permits it and the Sages forbid it, as a decree lest they are chopped/crushed; but the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:1)


**כֹּל שֶׁבָּא בְּחַמִּין.** שֶׁנִּתְבַּשֵּׁל:


כל הבא בחמין – that was boiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:2)


**מְדִיחִין.** דַּהֲדָחָתוֹ אֵינָהּ בִּשּׁוּלוֹ. אֲבָל לֹא שׁוֹרִין:


מדיחין – since rinsing it is not its cooking/preparation, but we don’t steep them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:3)


**חוּץ מִן הַמָּלִיחַ הַיָּשָׁן.** דַּג מָלִיחַ שֶׁעָבְרָה עָלָיו שָׁנָה מִשֶּׁנִּמְלַח:


חוץ מן המליח הישן – a salty fish that passed a year since it had been salted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:2:4)


**וְקוּלְיָס הָאִסְפָּנִין.** דָּג שֶׁקְּלִפָּתוֹ דַּקָּה, וַהֲדָחָתוֹ בְּחַמִּין הוּא גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּלוֹ:


וקוליס האספנין – a fish whose shell is thin and rinsing it in hot waters is the completion of its cooking.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:1)


**שׁוֹבֵר אָדָם אֶת הֶחָבִית.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מְקַלְקֵל:


שובר אדם את החבית – because it is damaged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:2)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּלִי.** לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ פֶּה נָאֶה:


ובלבד שלא יתכוין לעשותה כלי – to make for it a nice spout.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:3)


**אֵין נוֹקְבִים מְגוּפָה.** הַדְּבוּקָה בְּפִי חָבִית, אֶלָּא נוֹטֵל אֶת כֻּלָּהּ, דְּכִי נָקִיב לַהּ מְתַקֵּן פִּתְחָא הוּא:


אין נוקבים מגופה – that is attached to the mouth of the barrel, for if he were to pierce it, he would he is repairing the opening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַתִּיר.** דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ פֶּתַח חָבִית בְּכָךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי מתיר – for it is not the manner for the opening of the barrel with this, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:5)


**וְלֹא יִקְּבֶנָּה מִצִּדָּהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר הָא דְּשָׁרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לִהְיוֹת נוֹקֵב אֶת הַמְּגוּפָה, לֹא שָׁרֵי אֶלָּא לְמַעְלָה בְּרֹאשׁ הַמְּגוּפָה, דְּלָאו אוֹרְחָא לְמֵעֲבַד פִּתְחָא הָתָם אֶלָּא נוֹטֵל כָּל הַמְּגוּפָה. אֲבָל מִצִּדָּהּ, זִימְנִין דְּעָבֵיד לֵיהּ לַנֶּקֶב בְּצַד הַמְּגוּפָה מִשּׁוּם פִּתְחָא וְאֵינוֹ רוֹצֶה לְפָתְחָהּ לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁלֹּא יִפְּלוּ צְרוֹרוֹת אוֹ עָפָר בַּיַּיִן:


ולא יקבנה מצדה – that is to say, that which Rabbi Yosi permits to pierce the bung/stopper, is nor permitted other than above at the top of the stopper, for it is not the manner to make an opening there, but rather, he takes the entire bung/stopper, but from its side, as sometimes when he makes a perforation in the side of the bung, because he does not want to open it above so that stones or dirt will not fall into the wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:6)


**מְמָרֵחַ.** וְיֵשׁ כָּאן מִשּׁוּם מְמַחֵק:


ממרח – and there is here because of erasing/blotting out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:7)


**בַּעֲרָב.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


בערב – the name of a place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:3:8)


**חוֹשְׁשָׁנִי לוֹ מֵחַטָּאת.** אִם מֵרַח הַשַּׁעֲוָה לְדַבְּקָהּ בְּדָפְנֵי הַכְּלִי סְבִיב הַנֶּקֶב:


חוששני לו מחטאת – if the wax is smoothed over to cleave to the walls of the utensil around the perforation/hole.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:1)


**לְתוֹךְ הַבּוֹר.** שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מַיִם:


לתוך הבור – where there is no water in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:2)


**שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׁמוּר.** שֶׁלֹּא יַסְרִיחַ מֵחֲמַת הַחֹם. וְהָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַשְׁווֹיֵי גֻּמּוֹת שֶׁבְּקַרְקָעִית הַבּוֹר כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׁוֶה לְהוֹשִׁיב שָׁם הַקְּדֵרָה:


שיהא שמור – that it will not smell badly on account of the heat, and this comes to teach us that we don’t worry perhaps that he will make indentations in the bottom of the well in order that it will it will be even to place there the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:3)


**וְאֶת הַמַּיִם הַיָּפִים.** הָרְאוּיִים לִשְׁתִיָּה:


ואת המים היפים – that is fit for drinking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:4)


**בָּרָעִים.** בְּתוֹךְ מִקְוֵה מַיִם רָעִים שֶׁאֵינָן רְאוּיִן לִשְׁתִיָּה. וּמִלְּתָא דִּפְשִׁיטָא הִיא, וּמִשּׁוּם סֵיפָא נָקַט לַהּ, דִּתְנַן וְאֶת הַצּוֹנְנִים [בַּחַמָּה] בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁיֵּחֲמוּ, מַהוּ דְּתֵימָא נִגְזֹר דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאַטְמוֹנֵי בָּרֶמֶץ, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן:


ברעים – within the collection of bad waters which are not fit for drinking, and this is a simple matter, for on account the of the end of the Mishnah, he (i.e., the teacher of the Mishnah) took it, as it is taught in the Mishnah: “and cold water into hot water in order that it should become warm” for you might have thought that we would have made a decree lest we come to hide it in hot ashes; hence it comes to tell you that this is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:5)


**מִי שֶׁנָּשְׁרוּ כֵלָיו.** שֶׁנָּפְלוּ בַּמַּיִם בְּשַׁבָּת:


מי שנשרו [כליו] – that fell in the water on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:6)


**מְהַלֵּךְ עִמָּהֶן וְאֵינוֹ חוֹשֵׁשׁ.** שֶׁמָּא יַחְשְׁדוּ אוֹתוֹ שֶׁכִּבְּסָן:


מהלך עמהן ואינו חושש – lest they should suspect hm that he laundered them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:7)


**הִגִּיעַ לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** הַסְּמוּכָה לִמְבוֹא הָעִיר, שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם הַמִּשְׁתַּמֵּר:


הגיע לחצר החיצונה – that is near the entrance of the sit which is a guarded place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:8)


**שׁוֹטְחָן בַּחַמָּה.** לְיַבְּשָׁן:


שוטחן בחמה – to dry them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:4:9)


**אֲבָל לֹא כְנֶגֶד הָעָם.** שֶׁיַּחְשְׁדוּהוּ שֶׁכִּבְּסָן. וּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ דְּחוּיָה הִיא, שֶׁהֲלָכָה בְּיָדֵינוּ כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁאָסְרוּ חֲכָמִים מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעַיִן אֲפִלּוּ בְּחַדְרֵי חֲדָרִים אָסוּר, הִלְכָּךְ אָסוּר לְשָׁטְחָן אֲפִלּוּ שֶׁלֹּא כְּנֶגֶד הָעָם:


אבל לא כנגד העם – for they would suspect him that he laundered them, but this Mishnah is superseded/made inoperative, since the Halakha in our hands is that anything that the Sages forbade because of avoiding the semblance of wrong-doing (i.e., for appearance sake – see Talmud Betzah 9a), even in the strictest privacy, it is forbidden; therefore, it is forbidden to spread them out even not in the view of other people.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:1)


**וְנִסְתַּפֵּג.** וְקִנֵּחַ:


ונסתפג – to wipe off/cleanse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ בְעֶשֶׂר אֲלוּנְטִיּוֹת.** סְדִינִים שֶׁמְּקַנְּחִים בָּהֶן, וְנִסְתַּפֵּג בָּהֶן זֶה אַחַר זֶה, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא נְפִישֵׁי מַיָּא בְּכָל חַד וָחַד, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי לֹא יְבִיאֵם בְּיָדוֹ לְתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ עַל יְדֵי עֵרוּב, שֶׁאֵין כָּאן אִסּוּר הוֹצָאָה אֶלָּא גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִשְׁכַּח וְיִסְחֲטֵם בְּבֹאוֹ:


אלונטיות – sheets that we cleanse with them and dry off with them one after another even though there is not a lot of water on them, nevertheless, he should not he should not bring them in his hand into his house, even through an Eruv, for there is no prohibition of taking it out here but rather a decree lest he wring it out/squeeze it since they are many.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:3)


**אֲבָל עֲשָׂרָה בְּנֵי אָדָם.** הוֹאִיל וּמְרֻבִּין הֵם מַדְכְּרִי אַהֲדָדֵי. וַאֲפִלּוּ חֲדָא אֲלוּנְטִית לַעֲשָׂרָה בְּנֵי אָדָם, דְּהַשְׁתָּא נְפִישֵׁי בָּהּ מַיָּא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי מְבִיאִין אוֹתָהּ בְּיָדָם וְלֹא גָּזְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֲטוּהָ הוֹאִיל וּמְרֻבִּים הֵן:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:5:4)


**פְּנֵיהֶם יְדֵיהֶם וְרַגְלֵיהֶם.** אוֹרְחָא דְּמִלְּתָא נָקַט, וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל גּוּפָן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ אֶחָד מֵבִיא בְּיָדוֹ אֲלוּנְטִית שֶׁנִּסְתַּפֵּג בָּהּ, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יִסְחֹט:


פניהם ידיהם ורגליהם – it the manner of the thing that the Mishnah took [in using these terms] and the same law applies for their entire body. But, the Halakha is not according to this Mishnah but rather, even if one brings a wrapping cloth/sheet in his hand that he dried off with, and we don’t suspect lest he will wring it out/squeeze it.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:1)


**סָכִין.** שֶׁמֶן בְּשַׁבָּת:


סכין – oil on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:2)


**וּמְמַשְׁמְשִׁין.** בַּיָּד עַל כָּל הַגּוּף לַהֲנָאָה:


וממשמשין – by hand on the entire body for pleasure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא מִתְעַמְּלִין.** לְשַׁפְשֵׁף בְּכֹחַ:


אבל לא מתעמלין – to rub with strength/power.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:4)


**וְלֹא מִתְגָּרְדִין.** בְּמִגְרֶדֶת, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ (איוב ב) וַיִּקַּח לוֹ חֶרֶשׂ לְהִתְגָּרֵד בּוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוֵי כְּעֻבְדָּא דְּחֹל:


ולא מתגררין – with a strigil and similar to it (Job 2:8): “He took a potsherd to scratch himself [as he sat in ashes],” because it is like a deed of the weekday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:5)


**אֵין יוֹרְדִין לְפוֹלִימָא.** בִּקְעָה מְלֵאָה מַיִם וְתַחְתֶּיהָ טִיט כְּמוֹ דֶּבֶק, וְיֵשׁ בָּהּ מְקוֹמוֹת שֶׁיִּטְבַּע הָרוֹחֵץ שָׁם בְּאוֹתוֹ טִיט וְיִדְבַּק בּוֹ, וְאֵינוֹ יָכֹל לַעֲלוֹת עַד שֶׁיִּתְקַבְּצוּ בְּנֵי אָדָם וְיַעֲלוּהוּ בְּקֹשִׁי גָּדוֹל וּבְדֹחַק. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, בִּקְעָה שֶׁטִּיט שֶׁלָּהּ מַחְלִיק וְהָרוֹחֵץ שָׁם נוֹפֵל וּבְגָדָיו נִשּׁוֹרִים בַּמַּיִם, וְאָתֵי לִידֵי סְחִיטָה:


אין יורדין לפולימא – a valley filled with water and under it plaster/mud like glue and there are places where the bather will sink in that mud and become attached to it and he is unable to go up until a group of people gather together and take him up with great difficulty and in an emergency. Another interpretation: a valley where its mud is smooth and he who bathes there falls and whose clothing fall off in the water and he comes to squeezing/wringing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:6)


**אַפִּקְטְוִזִין.** לְהָקִיא. וּפֵרוּשׁוֹ אַפִּיק טְוִי זָיֵן, כְּלוֹמַר לְהוֹצִיא הַמָּזוֹן מִמְּקוֹם בִּשּׁוּלוֹ שֶׁהִיא הָאִצְטוֹמְכָה. אַפִּיק, מוֹצִיא. טְוִי, מִתְבַּשֵּׁל, צְלִי אֵשׁ מְתַרְגְּמִינַן טְוִי נוּר. זָיֵן, מָזוֹן. וְדַוְקָא לִשְׁתּוֹת מַשְׁקֶה שֶׁמְּבִיאוֹ לְהָקִיא הוּא שֶׁאָסוּר בְּשַׁבָּת, אֲבָל לְהַכְנִיס אֶצְבָּעוֹ לְתוֹךְ פִּיו כְּדֵי לְהָקִיא מֻתָּר. וְהֵיכָא דְּאִית לֵיהּ צַעְרָא וְאִם יָקִיא יִתְרַפֵּא, מֻתָּר אֲפִלּוּ עַל יְדֵי מַשְׁקֶה:


אפקטויזין – to vomit; and its explanation (see Talmud Shabbat 123a-b) is to cause to bring up roast to nourish, that is to say, to take out the food from the place it is cooked which is the stomach, it emits [and] removes roasted that is cooked, roasted by fire, which we translate as roasted by fire food and specifically to drink a liquid that he brings into him to vomit – which is prohibited on the Sabbath, but to place one’s finger into his mouth in order to vomit is permitted and where he has pain and if he vomits, he will recover, it is permissible even through liquids.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:7)


**וְאֵין מְעַצְּבִין אֶת הַקָּטָן.** לְתַקְּנוֹ וּלְיַשֵּׁב עַצְמוֹתָיו וְחוּלְיוֹת שִׁדְרָתוֹ, מִשּׁוּם דְּמֵחֲזֵי כְּבוֹנֶה. וְלֹא אֲמָרָן אֶלָּא לְאַחַר זְמַן, אֲבָל בְּיוֹם לֵידָה שָׁרֵי. מְעַצְּבִין, לְשׁוֹן יָדֶיךָ עִצְּבוּנִי וַיַּעֲשׂוּנִי (שם י):


ואין מעצבין את הקטן – to repair him and to settle his bones and vertebra of his spinal column because it appears like “building,” (i.e., one of the prohibited forms of work on the Sabbath), and we don’t say so other than after the time, but it is permitted on the day of birth. מעצבין – from the language (Job 10:8): “Your hands have shaped and fashioned me.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:8)


**וְאֵין מַחֲזִירִין אֶת הַשֶּׁבֶר.** עֶצֶם שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ, אֶלָּא הֲלָכָה מַחֲזִירִין אֶת הַשֶּׁבֶר בְּשַׁבָּת:


ואין מחזירין את השבר – the bone that was broken, but the Halakha is not according to this Mishnah but the Halakha is that we do set a fractured bone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:9)


**שֶׁנִּפְרְקָה יָדוֹ.** שֶׁיָּצָא הָעֶצֶם מִן הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁלּוֹ:


שנפרקה ידו – that the bone went out from its joint.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 22:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/22:6:10)


**לֹא יִטְרְפֵם.** לְשׁוֹן בֵּיצִים טְרוּפוֹת בַּקְּעָרָה, שֶׁמִּשְׁתַּטֵּף [בְּצוֹנֵן] עַל מְקוֹם הַשֶּׁבֶר, דְּמֵחֲזֵי דְּלִרְפוּאָה קָעָבֵיד:


לא יטרפם – like the language of scrambled eggs in a bowl, and they wash them in [cold] water on the place of the break and it appears like healing.



## Chapter 23


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:1:1)


**שׁוֹאֵל.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמַר הַלְוֵנִי. דְּהַלְוָאָה לִזְמַן מְרֻבֶּה מַשְׁמַע, וְקַיְמָא לָן סְתָם הַלְוָאָה שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, הִלְכָּךְ אָתֵי מַלְוֶה לִכְתֹּב עַל פִּנְקָסוֹ כָּךְ וְכָךְ הִלְוֵיתִי לִפְלוֹנִי, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁכַּח:


שואל. ובלבד שלא יאמר הלוני – for it implies a loan for a long time and we hold that an undefined loan is for thirty days; therefore the lender comes to write on his writing tablet/merchant’s account book that I have loaned such-and-such to so-and-so in order that he would not forget it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:1:2)


**מַנִּיחַ טַלִּיתוֹ אֶצְלוֹ.** אִם אֵינוֹ מַאֲמִינוֹ וְלוֹקֵחַ אוֹתוֹ וּמַקְדִּישׁוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּחוֹבוֹת שֶׁקָּבוּעַ לָהֶן זְמַן יְכוֹלִין לְהַקְדִּישׁ בְּשַׁבָּת:


מניח טליתו אצלו – if he (i.e., the lender) doesn’t believe him (i.e., the borrower) and he takes it and dedicates it as Temple property on the Sabbath for [loan[ obligations that have a fixed time [for return] one can dedicate [them] on the Sabbath (though we learn in Tractate Arakhin, Chapter 7, Mishnah 5, that one does not dedicate to the Temple something that is not his).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:1)


**פַּרְפְּרוֹתָיו.** מִינֵי מַעֲדַנִּים:


פרפרותיו – kinds of dainties/sweets.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא מִן הַכְּתָב.** אִם כָּתַב מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת כָּךְ וְכָךְ אוֹרְחִים כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁכְּחֵם, לֹא יִקְרָא בְּאוֹתוֹ כְּתָב בְּשַׁבָּת, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְחֹק. אִי נַמִּי שֶׁמָּא יִקְרָא בְּשִׁטְרֵי הֶדְיוֹטוֹת, וְאֵין מֻתָּר לִקְרוֹת בְּשַׁבָּת אֶלָּא בַּתּוֹרָה שֶׁבִּכְתָב, וּבַתּוֹרָה שֶׁבְּעַל פֶּה לְאַחַר שֶׁכְּתָבוּהָ, וּבְפֵרוּשֵׁיהֶן, אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר דְּבָרִים אוֹ בְּסִפְרֵי חָכְמוֹת שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִדִּבְרֵי נְבוּאָה אוֹ מִפֵּרוּשֵׁיהֶן, אֲסוּרִים:


אבל לא מן הכתב – if he wrote it from the Eve of the Sabbath (i.e., Friday): “so-and-so are guests” so that he would not forget, he should not read from the same document on the Sabbath, as a preventive measure lest he erase it; alternatively, lest he read private (not Hebrew) documents for it is prohibited to read anything on Shabbat other than the Written Torah and the Oral Torah, but other things or works of scholars that are not from the words of the prophets or their commentaries are forbidden (see Tractate Shabbat 149a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:3)


**מֵפִיס.** מֵטִיל גּוֹרָל לְחַלֵּק לְמִי יַגִּיעַ כָּל מָנָה וּמָנָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:4)


**עִם בָּנָיו וְעִם בְּנֵי בֵּיתוֹ.** שֶׁהֵן סְמוּכִים עַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹ, שֶׁאֵין כָּאן קְפֵדָא. אֲבָל עִם אַחֵר לֹא, דִּבְנֵי חֲבוּרָה הַמַּקְפִּידִים זֶה עַל זֶה שֶׁאֵינָן מוֹחֲלִין וְאֵינָן מְוַתְּרִים זֶה לָזֶה עוֹבְרִים מִשּׁוּם מִדָּה וּמִשּׁוּם מִשְׁקָל וּמִשּׁוּם מִנְיָן וּמִשּׁוּם לֹוִין וּפוֹרְעִין דְּגָזְרוּ בְּהוּ רַבָּנָן שֶׁמָּא יִכְתְּבוּ:


עם בניו ועם בני ביתו – for they are dependent upon his table for there is no legal objection, but with others one cannot [do so], for members of the group that are strict with each other for they do not pardon and do not renounce each other, violate this because of the measurement and the weight and the number because they loan and collect and the Rabbis decreed on this lest they write it down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:5)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְכַּוֵּן וְכוּ'.** מַתְנִיתִין חֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, מֵפִיס אָדָם עִם בָּנָיו וְעִם בְּנֵי בֵּיתוֹ עַל הַשֻּׁלְחָן, וַאֲפִלּוּ מָנָה גְּדוֹלָה כְּנֶגֶד מָנָה קְטַנָּה, וְדַוְקָא עִם בָּנָיו וְעִם בְּנֵי בֵּיתוֹ אֲבָל עִם אַחֵר לֹא, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹת מָנָה גְּדוֹלָה כְּנֶגֶד מָנָה קְטַנָּה, וְאָז הוּא דִּבְיוֹם טוֹב אָסוּר וּבְחֹל שָׁרֵי, אֲבָל אִם נִתְכַּוֵּן לַעֲשׂוֹת מָנָה גְּדוֹלָה כְּנֶגֶד מָנָה קְטַנָּה אֲפִלּוּ בְּחֹל אָסוּר, מִשּׁוּם קֻבְיָא, דְּגֶזֶל הוּא וְאַסְמַכְתָּא לֹא קַנְיָא, וְהַאי אַסְמַכְתָּא הִיא, דְּסָמִיךְ עַל הַגּוֹרָל אִם יִפֹּל לוֹ הַגּוֹרָל עַל הַמָּנָה הַגְּדוֹלָה יִזְכֶּה בָּהּ, וּלְפִיכָךְ תָּלָה עַצְמוֹ אַף לְגוֹרַל הַקְּטַנָּה עַל הַסָּפֵק, וְאִלּוּ יָדַע מִתְּחִלָּה שֶׁכֵּן, לֹא הָיָה מִתְרַצֶּה:


ובלבד שלא יתכוין וכו' – Our Mishnah is deficient, and should be read as follows: a person may conduct a lottery with his children and with the members of his household [dependent] upon his table and even a large portion corresponding to a small portion and specifically with his children and the members of his household, but not with others, provided that he does not intend to make a large portion correspond with a small portion, which is forbidden even on weekdays because dice-playing is theft and collateral security with the condition of forfeiture beyond the amount to be secured does not purchase/gives no title and this is a collateral security with the condition of forfeiture beyond the amount to be secured that is dependent upon the lottery if the lottery will fall to him on the large portion, he will be worthy of it, and therefore, he has left himself in doubt even for a lottery for a small portion through doubt , for had he known from the outset that this would be the case, he would not have accepted it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:6)


**חֲלָשִׁים.** גּוֹרָלוֹת, כְּמוֹ חוֹלֵשׁ עַל גּוֹיִם (ישעיה יד):


חלשים – lotteries such as (Isaiah 14:12): “A vanquisher of nations.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:7)


**עַל הַקָּדָשִׁים בְּיוֹם טוֹב.** שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בְּיוֹם טוֹב, לְחַלֵּק אוֹתָם בֵּין הַכֹּהֲנִים:


על הקדשים ביום טוב – that were slaughtered on Yom Tov/the Festival Day to distribute/divide them among the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:2:8)


**אֲבָל לֹא עַל הַמָּנוֹת.** שֶׁל קָדָשִׁים שֶׁל אֶתְמוֹל:


אבל לא על המנות – on yesterday’s Holy Things.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:1)


**לֹא יִשְׂכֹּר אָדָם פּוֹעֲלִים.** דִּכְתִיב (ישעיה נח) מִמְצֹא חֶפְצְךָ וְדַבֵּר דָּבָר:


לא ישכור אדם פועלים – as it is written (Isaiah 58:13): “Nor look to your affairs, nor strike bargains.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:2)


**וְלֹא יֹאמַר אָדָם לַחֲבֵרוֹ כוּ'.** לְדִיּוּקָא נָקְטֵיהּ, לֹא יֹאמַר לַחֲבֵרוֹ שְׂכֹר לָנוּ פּוֹעֲלִים, אֲבָל אוֹמֵר לוֹ הֲנִרְאֶה שֶׁתַּעֲמֹד עִמִּי לָעֶרֶב, כְּלוֹמַר עַכְשָׁיו נִרְאֶה אִם תָּבֹא אֵלַי לִכְשֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם יוֹדְעִים שֶׁעַל מְנָת לְשָׂכְרוֹ לִפְעֻלָּתוֹ הוּא מַזְהִירוֹ, כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא מְפָרֵשׁ לֵיהּ שְׂכִירוּת בְּהֶדְיָא שָׁרֵי, דְּקַיְמָא לָן דִּבּוּר אָסוּר הִרְהוּר מֻתָּר:


ולא יאמר אדם לחבירו וכו' – it was taken for an implied opinion/deduction that he should not say to his fellow: “hire for us workers,” but he says to him: “that it appears that you will stand with me this eening, that is to say, now it appears that if you come to me when it gets dark, and even though that both of them know that on the condition of hiring him for his work he warns him, since he does not spell out his salary explicitly, it is permitted, for we hold that loud recitation is prohibited, but thinking/fantasizing is permitted (on the Sabbath).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:3)


**אֵין מַחְשִׁיכִין עַל הַתְּחוּם.** לְקָרֵב עַצְמוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת עַד סוֹף הַתְּחוּם וּלְהַחְשִׁיךְ שָׁם שֶׁיְּהֵא קָרוֹב לִמְקוֹם הַפּוֹעֲלִים אוֹ לַפַּרְדֵּס לְהָבִיא פֵּרוֹת, דְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁאָסוּר לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת אָסוּר לְהַחְשִׁיךְ עָלָיו, אֲבָל מַחְשִׁיךְ הוּא לִהְיוֹת קָרוֹב לָצֵאת וְלִשְׁמֹר פֵּרוֹתָיו, דְּזֶה דָּבָר הַמֻּתָּר בְּשַׁבָּת לִשְׁמֹר פֵּרוֹתָיו:


אין מחשיכין על התחם – to draw himself close on the Sabbath to the place within which is it is permitted to move (i.e., the Sabbath limits) until the end of the limit and to make preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath there that he will be close to the place of the workers or to the orchard to bring fruit, for everything that is prohibited to do on the Sabbath, it is prohibited to make preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath but he can make preparations to be close to go out and guard his fruits for this is something that is permitted to do on the Sabbath to guard/watch his fruits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:4)


**וּמֵבִיא פֵרוֹת בְּיָדוֹ.** הוֹאִיל וְעִקַּר מַחְשַׁבְתּוֹ לֹא הָיְתָה לְכָךְ:


ומביא פירות בידו – since the essence of this thinking was not for this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:3:5)


**כְּלָל אָמַר אַבָּא שָׁאוּל.** אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא פָּלֵיג, דְּאָסַר כָּל הַחְשָׁכָה וְלֹא מַפְלִיג בֵּין הַחְשָׁכָה שֶׁל מִצְוָה לְהַחְשָׁכָה שֶׁל רְשׁוּת, וַאֲתָא אִיהוּ וְאָמַר דְּהַחְשָׁכָה שֶׁל מִצְוָה שַׁרְיָא, שֶׁכְּשֵׁם שֶׁמֻּתָּר לוֹמַר לַחֲבֵרוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת תִּהְיֶה מְזֻמָּן לֵילֵךְ לְאַחַר חֲשֵׁכָה לְהָבִיא אָרוֹן וְתַכְרִיכִים לַמֵּת, כָּךְ מֻתָּר לְהַחְשִׁיךְ עַל הַתְּחוּם כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּהְיֶה מְזֻמָּן לְאַחַר חֲשֵׁכָה לְהָבִיא אָרוֹן וְתַכְרִיכִים. וְסֵיפָא דִּתְנַן מַחְשִׁיכִים עַל הַתְּחוּם [לְפַקֵּחַ] עַל עִסְקֵי כַּלָּה וְעַל עִסְקֵי הַמֵּת, אַבָּא שָׁאוּל הִיא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


כלל אמר אבא שאול – He disputes the First Teacher [of our Mishnah] that prohibited all preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath, but he doesn’t make a distinction between preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath of a commandment and something that is optional, and he comes and states that preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath is permitted for just as it is permitted to tell his fellow on the Sabbath that he should be designated to go after dark to bring a coffin and shrouds for the dead person, so too, it is permitted to be make preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath in order that he will be designated after dark to bring the coffin and shrouds – and at the end of the Mishnah as it is taught: we can make preparations to be ready for work on the exit of the Sabbath to watch over the affairs of a bride and on the affairs of the dead. This is the opinion of Abba Shaul and the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:1)


**לְפַקֵּחַ עַל עִסְקֵי כַלָּה.** לְעַיֵּן וְלַחְקֹר בְּצָרְכֵי הַכַּלָּה:


לפקח על עסי כלה – to look out for and research the needs of he bride.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:2)


**חֲלִילִין.** כְּלֵי נִגּוּן חֲלוּלִין שֶׁקּוֹלָן מְעוֹרֵר הַבֶּכִי:


חלילין – a hollowed musical instrument whose sound gives rise to crying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:3)


**לֹא יִסְפֹּד בָּהֶן יִשְׂרָאֵל.** קְנָסָא הוּא מִשּׁוּם דְּמוּכְחָא מִלְּתָא שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּבְאוּ:


לא יספוד בהן ישראל – it is fine because he matter is proved that they were brought for Jews.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:4)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן בָּאוּ מִמָּקוֹם קָרוֹב.** אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נוֹדַע לָנוּ בְּבֵרוּר שֶׁבָּאוּ מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַתְּחוּם וְלֹא הֱבִיאָם מִחוּץ לַתְּחוּם:


אלא א"כ באו ממקום קרוב – other than if it is known to us clearly that they came from a place within the Sabbath limit, and that they were not brought from outside the Sabbath limit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:4:5)


**אָרוֹן.** לְעַצְמוֹ שֶׁיִּקְבֹּר בּוֹ נָכְרִי, אוֹ לִמְכֹּר:


ארון – for himself that he will bury in it an idolater or to sell it.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:1)


**סָכִין.** בְּשֶׁמֶן:


סכין – [anoint] with oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:2)


**וּמְדִיחִין.** בְּמַיִם. וּפוֹקְקִין נְקָבָיו, סוֹתְמִין נְקָבָיו הָעֶלְיוֹנִים וְהַתַּחְתּוֹנִים בְּבֶגֶד אוֹ בְּשׁוּם דָּבָר כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּכָּנֵס בָּהֶם הָרוּחַ וְיִתְפַּח:


ומדיחין – with water, and they stop up his urinary organs. They close up his upper urinary organs and the bottom ones with a cloth or because of a matter in order that wind should not enter into him and become blown up/swelled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:3)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יָזִיז בּוֹ אֵבֶר.** שֶׁלֹּא יְטַלְטֵל וְיַגְבִּיהַּ לֹא יָדוֹ וְלֹא רַגְלוֹ וְלֹא רִיסֵי עֵינָיו, שֶׁאָסוּר לְטַלְטֵל הַמֵּת אוֹ אֵבֶר מֵאֲבָרָיו, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמֻּתָּר לִגַּע בּוֹ. וְכֵן כָּל מֻקְצֶה מֻתָּר בִּנְגִיעָה וְאָסוּר בְּטִלְטוּל. וּבֵיצָה שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה בְּשַׁבָּת אוֹ בְּיוֹם טוֹב אֲסוּרָה אֲפִלּוּ בִּנְגִיעָה, שֶׁמִּפְּנֵי כַּדּוּרִיתָהּ נְגִיעָתָהּ זֶהוּ טִלְטוּלָהּ:


ובלבד שלא יזיזו בו אבר – that he should not carry and raise neither his hand nor his foot nor the eye lashes, for it is prohibited to carry the dead person or a limb from his limbs even though it is permitted to touch him and similarly, all things that are forbidden for use or handling on the Sabbath, it is permitted to touch it but it is prohibited to carry it and an egg that was laid on the Sabbath or on a Festival day it is prohibited even to touch it for since the well (which was in the shape of a ball), touching it is like carrying it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:4)


**וְשׁוֹמְטִים אֶת הַכַּר מִתַּחְתָּיו.** וְנִמְצָא מֻטָּל עַל הַחֹל, אֲבָל לֹא מְטַלְטְלִין לְהָנִיחוֹ עַל הַחֹל, דְּהָא תָּנָא רֵישָׁא וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יָזִיז בּוֹ אֵבֶר:


ושומטים את הכר מתחתו – and it is found lying on the sound but we don’t carry it to place it on the sand – for the Teacher of the first part [of the Mishnah] taught: as long as he does not move even a limb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:5)


**בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁיַּמְתִּין.** שֶׁלֹּא יַסְרִיחַ מֵחֲמַת חֹם הַסְּדִינִים וְהַכָּרִים:


בשביל שימתין – so that it does not decay as a result of the heat of the sheets and pillows.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:6)


**קוֹשְׁרִים אֶת הַלֶּחִי.** שֶׁל מֵת שֶׁהָיָה פִּיו הוֹלֵךְ וְנִפְתַּח:


קושרין את הלחי – of he dead person for his mouth would continue to open.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:7)


**וְלֹא שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה.** לְהִסָּגֵר מַה שֶּׁנִּפְתַּח, דְּהַיְנוּ מֵזִיז אֵבֶר, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא יוֹסִיף לְהִפָּתַח:


ולא שיעלה – to close what was open, which is moving a limb, but rather, it should not continue to open.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:8)


**סוֹמְכִים אוֹתָהּ בְּסַפְסָל.** שֶׁהֲרֵי תּוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו:


סומכים אותה בספסל – for it has the status of a utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:9)


**וְלֹא שֶׁתַּעֲלֶה.** דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ בּוֹנֶה:


ולא שתעלה – for it would be like “building.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:10)


**אֵין מְעַמְּצִין.** אֶת עֵינָיו בְּשַׁבָּת אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר יְצִיאַת נֶפֶשׁ, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ מֵזִיז אֵבֶר:


אין מעצמין – [they do not close] his eyes on the Sabbath, even after the soul has left, for it is like moving a limb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 23:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/23:5:11)


**שׁוֹפֵךְ דָּמִים.** שֶׁבְּדָבָר מֻעָט מְקָרֵב מִיתָתוֹ:


שופך דמים – for in a small manner, he brings his death closer.



## Chapter 24


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:1)


**מִי שֶׁהֶחְשִׁיךְ.** נוֹתֵן כִּיסוֹ לְנָכְרִי. מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁלוּחוֹ שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא לִשָּׂא אֶת כִּיסוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת, קִים לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן דְּאֵין אָדָם מַעֲמִיד עַצְמוֹ עַל מָמוֹנוֹ, וְאִי לֹא שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ אָתֵי לַאֲתוֹיֵי אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


מי שהחשיך. נותן כיסו לנכרי – while it is still daylight, and even though he is the agent of an Israelite to carry his money-bag on the Sabbath, the Rabbis have established that a person will not restrain himself from defending his property (see Talmud Shabbat 72a), for if they don’t permit him, he will come to carry four cubits in the public domain [on Shabbat].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:2)


**וְאִם אֵין עִמּוֹ נָכְרִי כוּ'.** הָא יֵשׁ עִמּוֹ נָכְרִי, לַנָּכְרִי יָהֵיב לֵיהּ, מַאי טַעְמָא, חֲמוֹר אַתָּה מְצֻוֶּה עַל שְׁבִיתָתוֹ, נָכְרִי אִי אַתָּה מְצֻוֶּה עַל שְׁבִיתָתוֹ. וּכְשֶׁמֵּנִיחַ כִּיסוֹ עַל הַחֲמוֹר מִשֶּׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ מְנִיחוֹ עָלֶיהָ כְּשֶׁהִיא מְהַלֶּכֶת, כְּלוֹמַר לְאַחַר שֶׁעָקְרָה רַגְלֶיהָ לֵילֵךְ, דְּלֹא עָבְדָה עֲקִירָה, וּכְשֶׁרוֹצָה הַבְּהֵמָה לַעֲמֹד נוֹטְלוֹ מֵעָלֶיהָ, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁתַּחְזֹר וְתַעֲקֹר רַגְלֶיהָ לֵילֵךְ יְנִיחֶנָּה עָלֶיהָ, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא תַעֲבִיד הַבְּהֵמָה עֲקִירָה וַהֲנָחָה, דְּאִי שָׁבֵיק לַהּ לְמֶעְבַּד עֲקִירָה וַהֲנָחָה וְהוּא מְחַמֵּר אַחֲרֶיהָ וּמַנְהִיגָהּ נִמְצָא מְחַמֵּר אַחַר בְּהֶמְתּוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת וְאָסוּר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ טְעוּנָה אֶלָּא כָּל דְּהוּ, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כ) לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה [כָּל] מְלָאכָה אַתָּה וּבְהֶמְתְּךָ, אֵיזוֹ הִיא מְלָאכָה שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית בֵּין הָאָדָם וְהַבְּהֵמָה, הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר זֶה מְחַמֵּר:


ואם אין עמו נכרי כו' – but if he has a heathen with him, he will give it to the heathen. What is the reason? A donkey, you are commanded to let his animals rest; but a heathen, you are not commanded to let him rest. For when he places his money-bag on the donkey when it gets dark [on Friday], he leaves it on her while she is walking, that is to say, after she has uprooted her leg to walk, she has not performed “uprooting” and when the animal wishes to stand, he takes it from upon her, but after she resumes and uproots her leg to walk, he places it upon her, in order that the animal not perform uprooting and placing down, for if he left her to perform uprooting and placing down, and he loads her or leads her, it is found that he is loading his animal on Shabbat and that is prohibited even though she is not loaded other than with something [minimal] , as it is written (Exodus 20:10): “You shall not do [any] work, you, [your son or daughter, your male or female slave,] or your cattle…” What is the work that is done whether by a person or the animal? One could say that it is loading/piling on.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:3)


**הִגִּיעַ לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** מִלְּתָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשַׁהּ הִיא וְלֹא בְּדִין כִּיסוֹ קָא מַיְרֵי:


הגיע לחצר החיצונה – it is thing that is taught separately for itself, and the law of his money-bag that we are speaking of.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:4)


**לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** שֶׁל עִיר, שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם הַמִּשְׁתַּמֵּר רִאשׁוֹן, כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא לְפָרֵק הַחֲמוֹר נוֹטֵל בְּיָדוֹ מֵעָלָיו כֵּלִים הַנִּטָּלִין בְּשַׁבָּת:


לחצר החיצונה – of the city which a place where it will be guarded first when he comes to unload the donkey, he takes by his hand from it utensils that are handled on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:1:5)


**וְשֶׁאֵינָם נִטָּלִים מַתִּיר אֶת הַחֲבָלִים.** שֶׁל אֻכָּף שֶׁהֵן קְשׁוּרִים, וְהַשַּׂקִּין נוֹפְלִים:


ושאינם ניטלים מתיר את החבלים – of the saddle which are tied up and the sacks fall.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:1)


**פְּקִיעֵי עָמִיר.** קַשִּׁין שֶׁל שִׁבֳּלִים שֶׁאֲגָדָן, מַתִּירִין אוֹתָן, דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁהֵן קְשׁוּרִין לָאו אֹכְלָא נִינְהוּ, וּמַתִּירִין אוֹתָן לִישַׁוִּינְהוּ אֹכְלָא, אֲבָל פִּסְפּוּס, לְפַזְּרָן כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁרְגִילִין לְפַזֵּר עֲשָׂבִים לִפְנֵי בְּהֵמָה כְּדֵי שֶׁתָּרִיחַ רֵיחָן וְיִהְיוּ יָפִין לָהּ לְאָכְלָן, אָסוּר בִּפְקִיעֵי עָמִיר, דְּמֵאַחַר דְּאִתְעֲבִידוּ אֹכְלָא בְּהֶתֵּר הַפְּקִיעִין, וְהַפִּסְפּוּס אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְתַעֲנוּג בְּעָלְמָא, וּמִטְרַח בְּדָבָר שֶׁהוּא כְּבָר אֹכֶל לֹא טָרְחִינַן:


פקיעי עמיר – straw of ears of corn that were tied together, we loosen them, for all the while that they are tied together they are not food/eatables, and we loosen them to make them into food, but we spread them to scatter them in the manner that we scatter grasses before the animal in order that it would smell it their scent and it would be nice for her to consume them, but it is prohibited with bundles of sheaves, for after they have made it into food, they untying bundles/bunch and the that which is spread, it is not other than for mere enjoyment, but he is troubling himself with something that is already food, we don’t trouble ourselves with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:2)


**וּמְפַסְפְּסִים אֶת הַכֵּפִים.** עֲנָפִים לַחִים שֶׁל אֶרֶז, מְפַזְּרִים וְשׁוֹטְחִים אוֹתָן לִפְנֵי הַבְּהֵמָה שֶׁתָּרִיחַ רֵיחָן, דִּבְלָאו פִּסְפּוּס לֹא הָווּ אֹכְלָא:


ומפספסים את הכיפים – moist cedar branches we spread out and stretch them in front of the animal in order that it would smell their scent for without spreading them out, they are not food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא אֶת הַזֵּרִין.** הֵן הֵן פְּקִיעֵי עָמִיר, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַפְּקִיעִין יֵשׁ לָהֶן שְׁנֵי קְשָׁרִים, אֶחָד בְּרֹאשָׁן וְאֶחָד בְּסוֹפָן, וְהַזֵּרִין יֵשׁ לָהֶן שְׁלֹשָׁה קְשָׁרִים, קֶשֶׁר אֶחָד יָתֵר בְּאֶמְצָעָן. וְקָאָמְרָה מַתְנִיתִין שֶׁאֵין מְפַסְפְּסִים אֶת הַזֵּרִין וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן דְּחוּקִים זֶה בָּזֶה וּמִתְחַמְּמִים וְהַבְּהֵמָה קָצָה בָּהֶן, אֶלָּא מַתִּיר שְׁלֹשָׁה אֲגָדֵיהֶן בִּלְבַד, דְּהַתָּרַת אֲגָדֵיהֶן מְשַׁוִּי לַהּ אֹכְלָא כִּפְקִיעִין:


אבל לא את הזירין – they are bundles of sheaves but these bundles have two ties, one at their head and another at their end, but the small bunches/capsules have three bands (see Talmud Shabbat 155a) - one additional connection in their middle and our Mishnah mentions it for we do not spread these bundles of sheaves, even though they are pressed against each other and become heated, for the animals set them aside but rather, he loosens three bunches only, for the loosening of the bands is a burden for her and the food is like bunches [when tied with two bands].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:4)


**אֵין מְרַסְּקִין אֶת הַשַּׁחַת.** אֵין מְחַתְּכִין עֵשֶׂב שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה, וְהִיא אַסְפַּסְתָּא, מִשּׁוּם דְּטִרְחָא שֶׁלֹּא לְצֹרֶךְ הוּא:


אין מרסקין את השחת – we don’t cut grass of grain and this is fodder for cattle because it is a trouble that is not necessary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:2:5)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר בֶּחָרוּבִין לִבְהֵמָה דַקָּה.** שֶׁשִּׁנֶּיהָ דַּקּוֹת, וְקָשִׁין לָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה מתיר בחרובין לבהמה דקה – since its teeth are thin and hard for her, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:1)


**אֵין אוֹבְסִין.** [אֵין] מַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ עַל כָּרְחוֹ וְתוֹחֲבִים לוֹ בִּגְרוֹנוֹ, וּפֵרוּשׁ אוֹבְסִין, עוֹשִׂין לָהּ אֵבוּס בְּתוֹךְ מֵעֶיהָ:


אין אובסין – we [don’t] force feed it and stick it down its throat; and the explanation of "אובסין" is making a manger of her stomach (i.e., fill it up to swelling – see Talmud Shabbat 155b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:2)


**וְלֹא דוֹרְסִים.** שֶׁדּוֹרֵס הַמַּאֲכָל לְתוֹךְ גְּרוֹנָהּ. וּמִיהוּ לֹא הֲוֵי כְּמוֹ אוֹבְסִין:


ולא דורסים – when he stuff’s food into the camel’s throat, however, this is not like "אובסין"/not making her a manger of her stomach (fill up to swelling).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:3)


**אֲבָל מַלְעִיטִים.** שֶׁתּוֹחֵב לוֹ הַמַּאֲכָל לְמָקוֹם שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַחְזִיר:


אבל מלעיטים – that you insert the food to a place where it can come back (and be rechewed).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:4)


**אֵין מְמָרְאִין.** מְפַטְּמִין, לְשׁוֹן וְחֵלֶב מְרִיאִים (ישעיה א). וּפֵרוּשׁ הַמְרָאָה, שֶׁתּוֹחֵב לוֹ הַמַּאֲכָל לִפְנִים מִבֵּית הַבְּלִיעָה בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכֹל לְהַחְזִיר:


ואין ממראין – to fatten, from the language of (Isaiah 1:11): “and suet of fatlings” – and the explanation is that they insert in it (i.e., the calf) the food inside from the esophagus, in a place where it cannot come back up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:5)


**וּמְהַלְקְטִין לַתַּרְנְגוֹלִין.** שֶׁתּוֹחֵב הַמַּאֲכָל לְתוֹךְ פִּיו בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַחְזִיר:


ומהלקטין לתרנגולים – that he inserts the food into its mouth into a place where it can come back up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא גוֹבְלִין.** אֵין לָשִׁין אוֹתוֹ בַּמַּיִם:


אבל לא גובלין – they do not knead it in water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:7)


**אֵין נוֹתְנִין מַיִם לִפְנֵי דְבוֹרִים.** שֶׁאֵין מְזוֹנוֹתֵיהֶן עָלָיו, שֶׁיּוֹצְאִין וְאוֹכְלִין בַּשָּׂדֶה וּמַיִם מְצוּיִין לָהֶם בָּאֲגַמִּים:


אין נותנין מים לפני דבורים – for their food is not upon him (i.e., the owner) since they go out and eat in the fields and water is found for them in lakes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:3:8)


**דּוֹרְסִיאוֹת.** יוֹנִים שֶׁגְּדֵלִים בַּבָּתִּים. וְנִקְרָאִים דּוֹרְסִיאוֹת עַל שֵׁם הוֹרְדּוּס הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהָיָה מְגַדֵּל מֵהֶן בְּאַרְמוֹנוֹ:


דורסיאות – doves that grow up in houses and they are called דורסיאות – on account of King Herod who raised them in his palace.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:1)


**מְחַתְּכִין אֶת הַדְּלוּעִין.** הַתְּלוּשִׁין לִפְנֵי הַבְּהֵמָה, וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּסְתָמָן לָאו לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה קַיְּמֵי אֶלָּא לָאָדָם:


מחתכין את הדלועין – that are detached before the cattle and even though they usually are not the food for animals, they exist only for humans.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:2)


**וְאֶת הַנְּבֵלָה.** שֶׁנִּתְנַבְּלָה הַיּוֹם, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת הָיְתָה עוֹמֶדֶת לָאָדָם וְלֹא לַבְּהֵמָה:


ואת הנבלה – that had become ritually forbidden by unskillful slaughtering that day, and even though it was at twilight, it stood for humans and not for animals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:4:3)


**רבי יהודה אומר אם לא היתה נבילה מע״ש אסורה.** דכל מידי דחזי לאינש לא מקצה ליה לבהמה. ואפילו חולה מע״ש, סובר שתתרפא. ואין הלכה כרבי יהודה:


רבי יהודה אומר אם לא היתה נבילה מע"ש אסורה – for anything that is appropriate for humans, it is not set aside for animals, and even if it is sick from the Eve of the Sabbath, we hold that it would heal, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:1)


**מְפִירִין.** בַּעַל לְאִשְׁתּוֹ וְאָב לְבִתּוֹ:


מפירין – a husband to his wife and/or a a father to his daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:2)


**וְנִשְׁאָלִין.** לֶחָכָם:


ונשאלין – to a Sage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:3)


**שֶׁהֵן לְצֹרֶךְ הַשַּׁבָּת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּדַר שֶׁלֹּא יֹאכַל הַיּוֹם. וְאַנִּשְׁאָלִין דַּוְקָא קָאֵי, דְּאִלּוּ בַּעַל אוֹ אָב מֵפֵר בֵּין נְדָרִים שֶׁהֵן לְצֹרֶךְ הַשַּׁבָּת בֵּין נְדָרִים שֶׁאֵינָן לְצֹרֶךְ הַשַּׁבָּת, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכֹל לְהָפֵר אֶלָּא בְּיוֹם שָׁמְעוֹ בִּלְבַד. וּנְדָרִים שֶׁהֵן לְצֹרֶךְ הַשַּׁבָּת אֲפִלּוּ הָיָה לוֹ פְּנַאי לְהִשָּׁאֵל עֲלֵיהֶם קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת נִשְׁאָלִין עֲלֵיהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת:


שהן לצורך השבת – such as that he vowed that he would not eat today and if made enquiry it specifically speaks of, for if it were a husband or a father annulling [a vow], whether they are for vows for the needs of the Sabbath, or whether they are for vows that are not for the needs of the Sabbath, for since he cannot to annual other than during the day of it being heard alone, and vows which are for the needs of the Sabbath, even if he had the free time to enquire about them before the [onset of] Shabbat, he enquires about them on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:4)


**וּפוֹקְקִין אֶת הַמָּאוֹר.** הַחַלּוֹן שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ הָאוֹרָה נִכְנֶסֶת, סוֹתְמִין אוֹתוֹ בְּלוּחַ אוֹ בִּשְׁאָר כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁרְגִילִים לִסְתֹּם בּוֹ:


ופוקקי את המאור – [he plugs/stops up] the window that from which the light enters he closes it up with a board or with the rest of anything that it is customary to stop it up with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:5)


**וּמוֹדְדִין אֶת הַמַּטְלִית.** כְּגוֹן אִם הָיְתָה טְמֵאָה וְנָגְעָה בְּטָהֳרוֹת, מוֹדְדִין אוֹתָהּ אִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֶצְבָּעוֹת לֵידַע אִם נִטְמְאוּ הַטָּהֳרוֹת אִם לָאו, דְּמַטְלִית פְּחוּתָה מִשָּׁלֹשׁ עַל שָׁלֹשׁ אֵינָהּ לֹא מִטַּמְּאָה וְלֹא מְטַמְּאָה:


ומודדין את המטלית – it if was ritually impure and came into contact with something ritually pure, we measure it [to see] if it has three fingers by three fingers to know if it the ritually pure things were defiled or not for a strip/patch that is less than three by three does not defile nor becomes defiled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:6)


**וְאֶת הַמִּקְוֶה.** לֵידַע אִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה בְּרוּם שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת. שֶׁאֵלּוּ מְדִידוֹת שֶׁל מִצְוָה הֵן, לְפִיכָךְ מֻתָּר לִמְדֹּד אוֹתָן בְּשַׁבָּת:


ואת המקוה – to know if it has a cubit by a cubit at a depth of thee cubits, for it they are measurements of a Mitzvah, therefore, it is permissible to measure them on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:7)


**שֶׁפָּקְקוּ אֶת הַמָּאוֹר.** אֶת הַחַלּוֹן. וְקָרוּי מָאוֹר, שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ הָאוֹרָה נִכְנֶסֶת:


שפקקו את המאור – the window and it is called – "מאור" – for through it the light enters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:8)


**בְּטָפִיחַ.** פַּךְ שֶׁל חֶרֶס:


בטפיח – an earthenware pitcher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:9)


**מְקֵדָה.** כְּלִי חֶרֶס:


מקדה – an earthenware utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:10)


**בְּגֶמִי.** לְהָכִי נָקַט גֶּמִי, שֶׁרָאוּי לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה וְלֹא מְבַטֵּל לֵיהּ לִהְיוֹת קֶשֶׁר שֶׁל קְיָמָא:


בגמי – for this reason, it (i.e., the Mishnah) took גמי/bulrush or reed-grass which is appropriate for the food of cattle and is not eliminated from being a permanent knot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:11)


**לֵידַע אִם יֵשׁ בַּגִּיגִית פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח.** כְּמִין שְׁבִיל קָטָן הָיָה בֵּין שְׁנֵי בָּתִּים שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מְקוּרֶה, אֶלָּא שֶׁגִּיגִית מֻנַּחַת עַל גַּבּוֹ, וְהָיוּ חַלּוֹנוֹת פְּתוּחוֹת מִן הַבָּתִּים אֶל הַשְּׁבִיל, וְהָיוּ חוֹשְׁשִׁין שֶׁמָּא יָמוּת מֵת בְּבַיִת אֶחָד וּתְהֵא הַטֻּמְאָה בָּא מִן הַחַלּוֹן אֶל הַשְּׁבִיל וּמִן הַשְּׁבִיל אֶל הַבַּיִת הָאַחֵר דֶּרֶךְ הַחַלּוֹן הַפָּתוּחַ, לְפִיכָךְ פָּקְקוּ אֶת הַחַלּוֹן הַפָּתוּחַ לַבַּיִת שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה בְּתוֹכוֹ בְּטָפִיחַ שֶׁל חֶרֶס וְגַבּוֹ לְצַד הַשְּׁבִיל, וּכְלִי חֶרֶס אֵינוֹ מִטַּמֵּא מִגַּבּוֹ, וְחוֹצֵץ, כִּי חָשְׁשׁוּ שֶׁמָּא אֵין בְּסֶדֶק הַגִּיגִית פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְנִמְצֵאת הַגִּיגִית מַאֲהֶלֶת עַל הַשְּׁבִיל וְהַטֻּמְאָה בָּאָה דֶּרֶךְ הַשְּׁבִיל מִבַּיִת זֶה לְבַיִת זֶה. אַחַר כָּךְ הֻצְרְכוּ לִפְתֹּחַ הַחַלּוֹן וְלִטֹּל אוֹתוֹ טָפִיחַ הַפָּקוּק בַּחַלּוֹן, וּבָאוּ לֵידַע אִם יֵשׁ בְּאוֹתוֹ סֶדֶק שֶׁל גִּיגִית פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח וְאֵין שָׁם אֹהֶל בְּאוֹתוֹ שְׁבִיל לְהָבִיא אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁהַטֻּמְאָה יוֹצְאָה מִן הַשְּׁבִיל דֶּרֶךְ סֶדֶק שֶׁבַּגִּיגִית כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה. אוֹ אֵין בַּסֶּדֶק שֶׁל גִּיגִית פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח, וְנִמְצֵאת הַשְּׁבִיל כְּאֹהֶל וּמְבִיאָה אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה מִבַּיִת לְבַיִת. וּמָדְדוּ מְקֵדָה שֶׁל חֶרֶס וּקְשָׁרוּהָ בְּגֶמִי וְהוֹשִׁיטוּהָ כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה, לִרְאוֹת אִם הָיָה בְּסֶדֶק הַגִּיגִית פּוֹתֵחַ טֶפַח אִם לָאו:


לידע אם יש בגיגית פותח טפח – a sort of small passageway that was between two houses so was not natural but rather that the barrel was placed on top of it, and their windows were open from the houses to the passageway, and they were worried lest someone die in one house and that the ritual impurity would come from the window to the passageway and from the passageway to the other house via the open window, therefore, they stopped up the open window to the house where the ritual defilement was inside with an earthenware pitcher and its back was to the side of the passageway for the earthenware utensil does not become defiled from its back and leaves a space between the two partitions, but they were concerned lest there wasn’t in the slit of the barrel an opening of a handbreadth, and it would be found that the barrel overshadows on the passageway and the ritual defilement comes through the passageway from one house to the other house. After that, it is was necessary to open the window and ot take that stopped up pitcher in the window and they came to know if there was in that slit of the barrel an opening of a handbreadth, and if there wasn’t a tent in that passageway to bring in the ritual defilement. For the ritual defilement goes out from the passageway via the slit in the barrel towards above, or if the slit in the barrel is not open one handbreadth, and it was found that the passageway itself was like a tent and brought the ritual defilement from one house to the other. And they measured the earthenware fire pot/potsherd for carrying fire and tied it with bulrushes and stretched it towards above to see if there was in the slit of the barrel opened a handbreadth or not.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Shabbat 24:5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Shabbat/r/24:5:12)


**ומדבריהם למדנו שפוקקין וקושרין ומודדין בשבת.** ובלבד שלא יהיה קשר של קיימא, והמדידה תהיה של מצוה, או להתלמד על דבר הוראה:


ומדבריהם למדנו שפוקקין וקושרין ומודדין בשבת – as long as it was not a permanent knot, for the measurement would be for a Mitzvah or to practice on what is a decision/instruction.
